Volume III Part 2
[279]
atha nikṣepākhyasya dvitīyapadasya vidhir ucyate
tatra nikṣepasya svarūpaṃ nārada āha |
svadravyaṃ yatra visrambhān nikṣipaty aviśaṅkitaḥ |
nikṣepo nāma tat proktaṃ vyavahārapadaṃ budhaiḥ || iti |
upanidhinyāsau nikṣepāvāntaraviśeṣau | tayoḥ svarūpam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
anākhyātaṃ vyavahitam asaṃkhyātam adarśitam |
mudrāṅkitaṃ ca yad dattaṃ tad aupanidhikaṃ smṛtam ||
rājacorārātibhayād akṣadānāṃ ca vañcanāt |
sthāpyate ‘nyagṛhe yat tu sa nyāsaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || iti |
nārado ‘pi |
asaṃkhyātam avijñātaṃ samudraṃ yan nidhīyate |
taj jānīyād upanidhiṃ nikṣepaṃ gaṇitaṃ viduḥ ||
[280] iti | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
vāsanastham anākhyāya hate ‘nyasya samarpyate |
dravyaṃ tadaupanidhikaṃ pratideyaṃ tathaiva tat || iti | (YDh 2.65)
vāsanaṃ karaṇḍādi tatsthaṃ dravyaṃ rūpasaṃkhyadiviśeṣam akathayitvā samudram anyahaste rakṣaṇārthāya sthāpyate tad dravyam aupanidhikam | nikṣepasthāpanavidhim āha manuḥ |
kulaje vṛttasaṃpanne dharmajñe satyavādini |
mahāpakṣe dhaniny āpte nikṣepaṃ niḥkṣiped budhaḥ || iti | (MDh 8.179)
bṛhaspatir api |
sthānaṃ gṛhaṃ sthalaṃ caiva tad ṛṇaṃ vividhān guṇān |
satyaṃ śaucaṃ bandhujanaṃ parīkṣya sthāpayen nidhim || iti |
tasya nikṣepasya punar dvaividhyam āha nāradaḥ |
sa punar dvividhaḥ proktaḥ sākṣimān itaras tathā |
pratidānaṃ tathaivāsya pratyayaḥ syād viparyaye ||
[281] iti | bṛhaspatir api |
sasākṣikaṃ raho dattaṃ dvividhaṃ tad udāhṛtam |
putravat paripālyaṃ tu vinaśyaty anavekṣayā ||
sthāpitaṃ yena vidhinā yena yac ca vibhāvitam |
tathaiva tasya dātavyam adeyaṃ pratyanantaram || iti |
sthāpaketarasya yasya sthāpite dravye svāmyam asti sa iha pratyantara ity ucyate | manur api |
yo yathā nikṣiped dhaste yam arthaṃ yasya mānavaḥ |
sa tathaiva grahītavyo yathā dāyas tathā grahaḥ || iti | (MDh 8.180)
dāyo dānaṃ sthapanam iti yāvat | graho grahaṇam | pālayituḥ phalam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
dadato yad bhavet puṇyaṃ hemarūpyāmbarādikam |
tat syāt pālayato nyāsaṃ tathaiva śaraṇāgatam || iti |
bhakṣakasya doṣas tenaiva darśitaḥ |
bhartṛdrohe yathā nāryāḥ puṃsaḥ putrasuhṛdvadhe |
doṣo bhavet tathā nyāse bhakṣitopekṣite nṛṇām || iti |
daivādyupaghāte tu na doṣaḥ syād ity āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
dvaivarājopaghātena yadi tannāśam āpnuyāt |
grahītṛdravyasahitaṃ tatra doṣo na vidyate ||
[282] iti | garhītur iti śeṣaḥ | rājaśabdenāsamādheyanimittam upalakṣyate | ata eva kātyāyanaḥ |
arājadaivike cāpi nikṣiptaṃ yatra nāśitam |
grahītuḥ sahabhāṇḍena dātur naṣṭaṃ tad ucyate || iti |
nārado ‘pi |
grahītuḥ saha so ‘rthena naṣṭo naṣṭaḥ sa dāyinaḥ |
daivarājakṛte tadvan na cet taj jihmakāritam || iti |
daivagrahaṇaṃ taskaropalakṣaṇārtham | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
na dāpyo ‘pahṛtaṃ taṃ tu rājadaivikataskaraiḥ | iti | (YDh 2.66)
manur api |
caurair hṛtaṃ jalenoḍham agninā dagdham eva ca |
na dadyād yadi tasmāt sa na saṃharati kiṃcana || iti | (MDh 8.189)
yadi tasmād dhanāt stokam api sa gṛhṇāti tadā dadyād ity arthaḥ | tathā ca sa eva |
[283]        samudre nāpnuyāt kiṃcid yadi tasmān na saṃharet || iti | (MDh 8.188)
kvacid yena kenacid dhetunā naṣṭam api grahītā mūladvāreṇa na dāpya ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
jñātvā dravyaviyogaṃ tu dātā yatra vinikṣipet |
sarvopāyavināśe ‘pi grahītā naiva dāpyate || iti |
upekṣādinā nāśe tu bṛhaspatir āha |
bhedenopekṣayā nyāsaṃ gṛhītvā yadi nāśayet |
na dadyād yācyamāno vā dāpyaḥ syāt sodayaṃ bhavet || iti |
kātyāyano ‘pi |
nyāsādikaṃ paradravyaṃ prakṣiptaṃ samupekṣitam |
ajñānanāśitaṃ caiva yena dāpyaḥ sa eva tat || iti |
atra viśeṣam āha vyāsaḥ |
bhakṣite sodayaṃ dāpyaḥ samaṃ dāpya upekṣite |
kiṃcid ūnaṃ pradāpyaḥ syād dravyam ajñānanāśitam || iti |
yācanānantaram adattasya paścād daivarājopaghāte sthāpakāya mūlyamātraṃ deyam | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
yācanānantaraṃ nāśe daivarājakṛte ‘pi saḥ |
grahītā pratidāpyaḥ syāt anyathā daṇḍam arhati ||
[284] iti | mūlamātram iti śeṣaḥ | pratyarpaṇavilambamātrāparādhe savṛddhikadāpanāyogāt | yācanānantaram adāne daṇḍam āha nāradaḥ |
yācyamānas tu yo dātur nikṣepaṃ na prayacchati |
daṇḍyaḥ sa rājño bhavati naṣṭe dāpyaś ca tatsamam || iti |
yaḥ punaḥ sthāpakānanujñayā nikṣepam upabhuṅkte tasya daṇḍam āha sa eva |
yatrārthaṃ sādhayet tena nikṣeptur ananujñayā |
tatrāpi daṇḍyaḥ sa bhavet taṃ ca sodayam āvahet || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
ājīvan svecchayā daṇḍyo dāpas taṃ cāpi sodayam || iti | (YDh 2.67)
atra daṇḍo ‘pi sama eva grāhyaḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
[285]        nikṣepasyāpahartāraṃ tatsamaṃ dāpayed damam |
tathopanidhihartāram aviśeṣeṇa pārthivaḥ || iti | (MDh 8.192)
bṛhaspatir api |
grahītā nihnute yatra sākṣibhiḥ śapathena vā |
vibhāvya dāpayen nyāsaṃ tatsamaṃ vinayen nṛpaḥ || iti |
sthāpakasyānṛtavāditve daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
nikṣepo yaḥ kṛto yena yāmāṃś ca kulasaṃnidhau |
tāvān eva sa vijñeyo vibruvan daṇḍam arhati || iti | (MDh 8.194)
sasākṣikanikṣepo sākṣivacanaviruddhaṃ vadan daṇḍyaḥ | asākṣike tu bṛhaspatir āha |
raho datte nidhau yatra visaṃvādaḥ pravartate |
vibhāvakaṃ tatra divyam ubhayor api ca smṛtam || iti |
grahītṛsthāpakayor anṛtavāditve daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
nikṣepasyāpahartāram anikṣeptāram eva ca |
sarvair upāyair anvicchec chapathaiś caiva vaidikaiḥ ||
[286]        yo nikṣepaṃ nārpayati yaś cānikṣipya yācate |
tāv ubhau coravac chāsyau dāpyau vā tatsamaṃ damam || iti | (MDh 8.190–91)
nikṣiptaṃ dravyam akāle dadato dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ | tad āha kātyāyanaḥ |
grāhyas tūpanidhiḥ kāle kālahīnaṃ tu varjayet |
kālahīnaṃ dadad daṇḍaṃ dviguṇaṃ ca pradāpyate || iti |
yad bhayād upanidhir anyasya haste nyastas tad bhayātīte kāle sa grāhyaḥ | tad bhayātīte ‘pi kāle svayam eva nārpaṇīyaḥ, “sakṛd yācitam arpayet” iti bṛhaspatismaraṇāt | tad bhaye vartamāne svayam eva dīyamānaṃ kālahīnaṃ tad dānaṃ dauṣṭyenaiveti tad dadato ‘pi daṇḍo yuktaḥ | yas tu balāvaṣṭambhena nikṣepaṃ na dadāti taṃ rājā nigṛhya dāpayed ity āha manuḥ |
teṣāṃ na dadyād yadi tu tad dhiraṇyaṃ yathāvidhi |
imaṃ nigṛhya dāpyaḥ syād iti dharmasya dhāraṇā ||
[287]        nikṣiptasya dhanasyaivaṃ prītyopanihitasya ca |
kuryād vinirṇayaṃ rājā akṣiṇvan nyāsadhāriṇam || iti | (MDh 8.196)
akṣiṇvan atādayan | nikṣeptari mṛte pratyanantaradhane na doṣa ity āha nāradaḥ |
svayam eva tu yo dadyān mṛtasya pratyanantare |
na sa rājñā niyoktavyo na nikṣeptus tu bandhubhiḥ || iti |
yadā tu svayam eva na dadāti tadā pratyanantaraṃ praty āha |
acchalenaiva vānviṣyet tam arthaṃ prītipūrvakam |
vicārya tasya vā vṛttaṃ sāmnaiva parisādhayet || iti |
nikṣepe ‘bhihitaṃ dharmaṃ yācitādiṣv atidiśati nāradaḥ |
eṣa eva vidhir dṛṣṭo yācitānvāhitādiṣu |
śilpiṣūpanidhau nyāse pratinyāse tathaiva ca || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
[288]        yācitānvāhitnyāsanikṣepādiṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti | (YDh 2.67)
yācitam utsavādiṣu parakīyam alaṃkārādyarthaṃ yācitvā svasamīpam ānītam | anvāhitaṃ svasmin sthitaṃ paradhanaṃ dhanikāntarasthatayā kṛtam | nyāsanikṣepau pūrvam evābhihitau | bṛhaspatir api |
anvāhite yācitake śilpinyāse sabandhake |
eṣa evodito dharmas tathā ca śaraṇāgate || iti |
śilpinyāso nāmāṅgulīyakādikaraṇāya svarṇakārādihaste nyastaṃ suvarṇādikam | anena yācitasya śilpihastanyastasya daivarājopaghātena vināśe svarṇakāraprabhṛtayaḥ tan na dāpyāḥ ity uktaṃ bhavati | atrāpavādam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
yaiś ca saṃskriyate nyāso divasaiḥ pariniścitaiḥ |
tadūrdhvaṃ sthāpayec chilpī dāpyo daivahate ‘pi tam || iti |
nairmālyādyarthaṃ rajakādihastanyastavastādiviṣaye ‘py āha sa eva |
nyāsadoṣād vināśaḥ syāc chilpinaṃ tan na dāpayet |
dāpayec chilpidoṣāt tat saṃskārārthaṃ yad arpitam ||
[289] iti | yatra tantvādikaṃ vastrārthaṃ kuvindādau nyastaṃ khaṇḍapaṭādidaśāyāṃ naṣṭaṃ paripūrṇadaśāyāṃ vā kuvindādinā dīyamānaṃ svāminā na gṛhītaṃ naṣṭaṃ ca tatrāpy āha sa eva |
śilpenāpi ca yat karma naṣṭaṃ yad bhṛtakasya tat |
paryāptaṃ ditsatas tasya vinaśyet tad agṛhṇataḥ || iti |
śilpena prācyavanādinā vikalaṃ naṣṭaṃ bhṛtakasya śilpina eva tan naṣṭam | punar vetanagrahaṇam antareṇaiva vānādikriyāṃ kuryad ity arthaḥ | yadi svāmī punas tantvādikaṃ nārpayati tadā punar vānādyabhāve ‘pi vetanaṃ śilpine dattaṃ dātā na labhate | paryāptaṃ paripūrṇaṃ vastrādikam aditsato dhṛtakasya yaḥ svāmī tasya dīyamānam agṛhṇatas tatparyāptaṃ vinaśyet | yācitakviṣaye viśeṣas tenaivoktaḥ |
yadi tatkaryam ādiśya kālaṃ pariniyamya vā |
yācito ‘rdhakṛte tasmin prāpte ca na tu dāpyate ||
[290] iti | yat kāryaṃ dīrghakālasādhyaṃ tat kāryārthaṃ yadi yācitaḥ, yadi vā saṃvatsaraparyantaṃ dīyatām ity evaṃ kālaṃ pariniyamya yāceta, tac ca kāryamadhye priniyatakālasya madhye vā pratiyācyamāno yācitakaṃ na dadāti, asau na sodayaṃ dāpyaḥ | yācitakamātram evāsau kṛte kārte pariniyatakālātyaye vā dadyāt | yadi tadāpi na dadāti tadā daivādito vināśe mūlyaṃ deyam ity āha sa eva |
prāptakāle kṛte kārye na dadyād yācito ‘pi san |
tasmin naṣṭe mṛte vāpi grahītā mūlyam āvahet || iti |
kvacid aprāpte ‘pi kāle yācitakaṃ dāpya ity āha sa eva |
atha kāryavipattis tu tasyaiva svāmino bhavet |
aprāpte vesa(caiva?)kāle tu dāpyas tv ardhakṛte ‘pi tat || iti |
iti nikṣepaprakaraṇam
atha asvāmivrikrayaḥ
tasya svarūpam āha nāradaḥ |
nikṣiptaṃ vā paradravyaṃ naṣṭaṃ labdhvāpahṛtya vā |
vikrīyate ‘samakṣaṃ yat sa jñeyo ‘svāmivikrayaḥ || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
[291]        nikṣepānvāhitanyāsahṛtayācitabandhakam |
upāṃśujanavikrītam asvāmī so ‘bhidhīyate || iti |
asvāminā kṛto vyavahāro nivartate ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
asvāmivikrayaṃ dānam ādhiṃ ca vinivartayet | iti |
nārado ‘pi |
asvāminā kṛto yas tu krayo vikraya eva vā |
akṛtaḥ sa tu vijñeyo vyavahāreṣu nityaśaḥ || iti |
tataś cāsvāmivikrītaṃ dravyaṃ svāmī gṛhṇīyāt iti | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
dravyam asvāmivikrītaṃ prāpya svāmī tad āpnuyāt | iti |
tadavāptiś ca rājñe nivedayitvaiva, anyathā daṇḍavidhānāt | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
hṛtaṃ pranaṣṭaṃ yo dravyaṃ parahastād avāpnuyāt |
anivedya nṛpe daṇḍyaḥ sa tu ṣaṇṇavatiṃ paṇān || (YDh 2.172)
[292] iti | yadā punaḥ parahastād avāptum asamarthaḥ svāmī, tadā tv āha sa eva |
naṣṭāpahṛtam āsādya hartāraṃ grāhayen naram |
deśakālātipattau vā gṛhītvā svayam arpayet || iti | (YDh 2.169)
vinaṣṭam apahṛtaṃ vā svakīyadravyam adhigantur apahartur vā haste dṛṣṭvā adhigantāram apahartāraṃ rājapuruṣādibhir grāhayet | rājapuruṣādyānayanārthaṃ deśakālātikramaś ced bhavati tadā svayam eva gṛhītvā rājñe samarpayed ity arthaḥ | yadā punaḥ krayād ūrdvam eva svakīyaṃ dravyaṃ kretur haste paśyati tadāpy āha sa eva |
svaṃ labhetānyavikrītaṃ kretur doṣo ‘prakāśite |
hīnād raho hīnamūlye velāhīne ca taskaraḥ || iti | (YDh 2.268)
svāmī svasaṃbandhi dravyam anyavikrītaṃ yadi paśyati tadā labheta gṛhṇīyāt | asvāmivikrayasya parasvatvahetutvābhāvāt | kretuḥ punar aprakāśite gopite kraye doṣo bhavati | hīnād dravyāgamopāyahīnāt, raha ekānte, hīnamūlye alpatareṇa dravyeṇa adhikamūlyakraye ca, velāhīne rātryādau kṛte kraye ca sa coro bhavati | taskaravad daṇḍyo bhavatīty arthaḥ | yathoktaṃ manunā |
[293]        dravyam asvāmivikrītaṃ prāpya svāmī tad āpnuyāt |
prakāśe krayataḥ siddhiḥ kretuḥ steyaṃ rahaḥ krayāt || iti |
bṛhaspatināpi ajñānato jñānataś ca kretur nirdoṣatā sadoṣatā ca darśitā |
yena krītaṃ tu mūlyena prāg rājñe viniveditam |
na tatra vidyate doṣaḥ stenaḥ syād upavikrayāt || iti |
yena kretrā hīnamūlyena krayāt prāg eva rājñe viniveditam | na tatra doṣaḥ | upavikrayaśabdasyārthas tenaiva darśitaḥ |
antargṛhe bahir grāmān niśāyām asato janāt |
hīnamūlyaṃ ca yat krītaṃ jñeyo ‘sāv upavikrayaḥ || iti |
asato janāc caṇḍālāder ity arthaḥ | asadgrahaṇaṃ svāmyananujñātadāsādyupalakṣaṇārtham | ata eva nāradaḥ |
[294]        asvāmyanumatād dāsād asataś ca janād rahaḥ |
hīnamūlyam avelāyāṃ krīṇaṃs taddoṣabhāg bhavet || iti |
taddoṣabhāk coradoṣabhāg ity arthaḥ | kathaṃ svāmī vikrītadravyavipratipattau pramāṇena sādhayed ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
nāṣṭikas tu prakurvīta taddhanaṃ jñātṛbhiḥ svakam | iti |
nāṣṭiko naṣṭadhanaḥ | taddhanaṃ naṣṭadhanam | jñātṛbhiḥ sākṣyādibhiḥ | svakam ātmīyaṃ prakurvīta sādhayed ity arthaḥ | kathaṃ sādhayed ity apekṣite āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
āgamenopabhogena naṣṭaṃ bhāvyam ato ‘nyathā |
pañcabandho damas tasya rājñe tenāvibhāvite || iti | (YDh 2.171)
āgamas tu smṛtyantarābhihito draṣṭavyaḥ |
labdhaṃ dānakrayaprāptaṃ śauryaṃ vaivāhikaṃ tathā |
[295]        bāndhavād aprajād yat tu ṣaḍvidhas tu dhanāgamaḥ || iti |
kṛtasvakīyasya dhanasya svakīyatvānapagatir api dānaprabhāvena sādhanīyety āha kātyāyanaḥ |
adattatyaktavikrītaṃ kṛtvā svaṃ labhate dhanam | iti |
athaitat dattaṃ vikrītaṃ ca na bhavatīti pramāṇaiḥ prasādhya svakīyaṃ dhanaṃ nāṣṭiko vikretrādeḥ sakāśāl labhata ity arthaḥ | atra viṣaye viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
pūrvasvāmī tu tad dravyaṃ yad āgatya vicārayet |
tatra mūlaṃ darśanīyaṃ kretuḥ śuddhis tato bhavet || iti |
mūlaṃ vikretā | vikretṛdarśanānantaraṃ vyāsaḥ |
mūle samāhṛte kretā nābhiyojyaḥ kathaṃcana |
mūlena saha vādas tu nāṣṭikasya tadā bhavet || iti |
yadā tu mūlatvena darśito vikretā na kiṃcid uttaraṃ dadāti tadā tv āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
vikretā darśito yatra hīyate vyavahārataḥ |
kretre rājñe mūlyadaṇḍau pradadyāt svāmine dhanam ||
[296] iti | nārado ‘pi |
vikretā svāmine yat svaṃ kretre mūlyaṃ ca yat kṛtam |
dadyād daṇḍaṃ tathā rājñe vidhir asvāmivikraye || iti |
yadā tu mūlabhūto vikretā deśāntaraṃ gataḥ tadā kātyāyana āha |
mūlānayanakālaś ca deyo yojanasaṃkhyayā |
prakāśaṃ ca krayaṃ kuryāt sākṣibhir jñātibhiḥ svakaiḥ ||
na tatrānyā kriyā proktā daivikī na ca mānuṣī |
prasādhite kraye rājñā vaktavyaḥ sa na kiṃcana || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – yas tu kretā kālavilambenāpi mūlaṃ darśayituṃ na śaknoti krayaprakāśanaṃ ca karoti sa narādhamaḥ | na sa rājñāṃ daṇḍyaḥ | tasmāc ca nāṣṭiko dhanaṃ labhata iti | tad uktaṃ manunā |
atha mūlam anāhāryaṃ prakāśakrayaśodhitam |
adaṇḍyo mucyate rājñā nāṣṭiko labhate dhanam || (MDh 8.202)
[297] iti | kretuḥ sakāśād dhanagrahaṇam ardhaṃ mūlyaṃ datvaiva | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
vaṇig vīthīparigataṃ vijñātaṃ rājapūruṣaiḥ |
avijñātāśrayāt krītaṃ vikretā yatra vā mṛtaḥ ||
svāmī datvārdhmūlyaṃ tu praghṇīyāt svakaṃ dhanam |
ardhaṃ dvayor apahṛtaṃ tatra syāt vyavahārataḥ || iti |
avijñātāśrayād avijñātasthānakād ity arthaḥ | krayaprakāśanamūlānayanapakṣayoḥ sati saṃbhave mūlānayanapakṣa eva grāhyaḥ | tad uktaṃ kātyāyanena |
yadā mūlam upanyasyet punar vādī krayaṃ vadet |
āharan mūlam evāsau na krayeṇa prayojanam ||
asamāhāryamūlas tu krayam eva viśodhayet || iti |
yadā mūladarśanaṃ krayaprakāśanaṃ vā na karoti tadā daṇḍya ity āha sa eva |
anupasthāpayan mūlaṃ krayaṃ vāpy aviśodhayan |
yathābhiyogaṃ dhanine dhanaṃ dāpyo damaṃ ca saḥ || iti |
nāṣṭikaviprayogam āha sa eva |
yadi svaṃ naiva kurute jñātibhir nāṣṭiko dhanam |
prasaṅgavinivṛttyarthaṃ coravad daṇḍam arhati ||
[298] iti | prasaṅgo ‘tiprasaṅgaḥ | daṇḍaparimāṇam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
āgamenopabhogena naṣṭaṃ bhāvyam ato ‘nyathā |
pañcabandho damas tatra rājñe tenāvibhāvite || iti | (YDh 2.171)
vivādyasya dhanasya pañcamo ‘ṃśo yasmin mūle badhyate sa pañcabandhaḥ | etad alpāparādhaviṣayam | mahāparādhe tu vyāsaḥ |
vādī conmārgagaṃ dravyaṃ sākṣibhir naiva bhāvayet |
dāpyaḥ syāt dviguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ kretā tad dravyam arhati || iti |
yat tu prakāśite kraye kretuḥ svatvapratipādakaṃ marīcivacanam,
vaṇig vīthīparigataṃ vijñātaṃ rājapuruṣaiḥ |
divā gṛhītaṃ yat kretrā sa śuddho labhate dhanam ||
iti, tad evaṃ nāṣṭikenāsādhitadravyaviṣayam, anyathā “atha mūlam anāhāryam” iti pratyudāhṛtamanuvacanavirodhaprasaṅgāt | yadā kretā [299] sākṣyādibhiḥ krayaṃ na vibhāvayati nāṣṭiko ‘pi svakīyatvaṃ tadā nirṇayam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
pramāṇahīnavāde tu puruṣāpekṣayā nṛpaḥ |
samanyūnādhikatvena svayaṃ kuryād vinirṇayam || iti |
puruṣayor nāṣṭikakretṛpuruṣayoḥ sādhutvādyapekṣayā vivādāspadaṃ dhanaṃ samanyūnādhikatven vibhajya idaṃ taveti rājā nirṇayaṃ kuryāt |
nanu – mānuṣapramāṇābhāve ‘pi divyasya vidyamānatvāt pramāṇahīno vāda eva na saṃbhavati – iti cet |
ucyate | asminn evāsvāmivikrayavivāde divyābhāvābhidhānāt | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
prakāśaṃ na krayaṃ kuryāt sādhubhir jñātibhiḥ svakaiḥ |
na tatrānyā kriyā proktā daivikī na ca mānuṣī || iti |
kiṃ ca,
[300]        abhiyoktā dhanaṃ kuryāt prathamaṃ jñātibhiḥ svakam |
paścād ātmaviśuddhyarthaṃ krayaṃ kretā svabandhubhiḥ ||
iti vacanena sākṣītarapramāṇābhāvo ‘vagamyate | yat tu kātyāyanenoktam,
alekhyāsākṣike daivīṃ vyavahāre vinirdiśet |
iti, tad divyaniṣedhakasmṛtyabhāvaviṣayam iti mantavyam | sapramāṇake ‘pi vāde kretṛnāṣṭikayoḥ arthahānim āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ardhaṃ dvayor apahṛtaṃ tatra syād vyavahārataḥ |
avijñātakrayo doṣas tathā vā paripālanam ||
etad dvayaṃ samākhyātaṃ dravyahānikaraṃ budhaiḥ |
avijñātsthānakṛtaṃ kretṛnāṣṭikayor dvayoḥ || iti |
avijñātasthānakāt krayo avijñātakrayaḥ | atha vā paramārthato ‘yaṃ svāmī ity avijñānāt krayo ‘vijñātakrayaḥ | marīcir api |
[301]        avijñātaniveśatvād yatra mūlaṃ na vidyate |
hānis tatra samā kalpyā kretṛnāṣṭikayor dvayoḥ || iti |
asvāmī vikretur iva svāmyadattam upabhuñjānasyāpi daṇḍam āha nāradaḥ |
uddiṣṭam eva bhoktavyaṃ strī paśur vasudhāpi vā |
anarpitaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte bhuktabhogaṃ pradāpayet ||
anirdiṣṭaṃ tu yad dravyaṃ vāsaḥ kṣetraṃ gṛhādikam |
svabalenaiva bhuñjānaś coravad daṇḍam arhati ||
anaḍvāhaṃ tathā dhenuṃ navaṃ dāsaṃ tathaiva ca |
anidiṣṭaṃ tu bhuñjāno dadyāt paṇacatuṣṭayam ||
dāsī naukā tathā dhuryo bandhakaṃ nopayujyate |
upabhoktā tu taṃ dravyaṃ paṇyenaiva viśodhayet ||
divase dvipaṇaṃ dāsīṃ dhenum aṣṭapaṇaṃ tathā |
trayodaśam anaḍvāham aśvaṃ bhūmiṃ ca ṣoḍaśa ||
naukām aśvaṃ ca dhanuṃ ca lāṅgalaṃ kārmikasya ca |
balātkāreṇa yo bhuṅkte dāpyaś cāṣṭapaṇaṃ dine ||
[302]        ulūkhale paṇārdhaṃ tu musalasya paṇadvayam |
śūrpasya ca paṇārdhaṃ tu dve paṇe munir abravīt || iti |
iti asvāmivikrayākhyaṃ padaṃ samāptam
atha saṃbhūyasamuttāhākhyaṃ padam ucyate
tasya svarūpaṃ nārada āha |
vaṇikprabhṛtayo yatra karma saṃbhūya kurvate |
tat saṃbhūyasamutthānaṃ vyavahārapadaṃ smṛtam || iti |
tatrādhikāriṇo darśayati bṛhaspatiḥ |
kulīnadakṣānalasaiḥ prājñair nāṇakavedibhiḥ |
āyavyayajñaiḥ śucibhiḥ śūraiḥ kuryāt sahakriyām || iti |
kriyāṃ kṛṣivāṇijyaśilpakratusaṅgītasainyātmikām | nāṇakavijñānī vāṇijyakriyāyām upayujyate | āyavyayajñānamātraṃ karṣakakriyāyām | saṅgītādiśilpakriyāyāṃ prājñatvam upayujyate | kratukriyāyāṃ tu kulīnatvaprājñatvaśucitvāni | sainyakriyāyāṃ śūratvamātram | dakṣatvānalasatve tu sarvatropayujete | ata evādakṣādīn niṣedhati sa eva |
[303]        asaktālasarogārtamandabhāgyanirāśrayaiḥ |
vāṇijyādyāḥ sahaitais tu kartavyā budhaiḥ kriyāḥ || iti |
ye tu saṃbhūyavāṇijyādikriyāṃ kurvanti te dravyānusāreṇa lābhabhājaḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
prayogaṃ kurvate ye tu hemadhānyarasādinā |
samanyūnādhikar aṃśair lābhas teṣāṃ tathāvidhaḥ || iti |
lābhavad eva vyavyādir api tathaivety āha sa eva |
samo nyūnādhiko vāṃśo yena kṣiptas tathaiva saḥ |
vyayaṃ dadyāt karma kuryāl lābhaṃ gṛhṇīta caiva hi || iti |
dravyānusāreṇa lābha ity asyāpavādam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
samavāyena vaṇijāṃ lābhārthaṃ karma kurvatām |
lābhālābhau yathādravyaṃ yathā vā saṃvidā kṛtau || iti | (YDh 2.259)
saṃvidā samayena | puruṣaviśeṣānusāreṇa kṛtau kalpitau [304] lābhālābau jñeyau | na tu dravyānusāreṇa ity arthaḥ | saṃbhūyakāriṇāṃ kartavyam āha vyāsaḥ|
samakṣam asamakṣaṃ vāvañcayantaḥ parasparam |
nānāpaṇyānusārāt te prakuryuḥ krayavikrayau ||
agopayanto bhāṇḍāni śulkaṃ dadyuś ca te ‘dhvani |
anyathā dviguṇaṃ dāpyāḥ śulkasthānāt bahiḥsthitāḥ || iti |
nārado ‘pi |
bhāṇḍapiṇḍavyayoddhārabhārasārādyavekṣaṇam |
kuryus te ‘vyabhicāreṇa samaye sve vyavasthitāḥ || iti |
saṃbhūyakāriṇāṃ parasparaṃ vivāde nirṇayaprakāram āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
[305]        parīkṣakāḥ sākṣiṇaś ca ta evoktāḥ parasparam |
saṃdigdhe ‘rthe ‘vañcanārthā na ced videveṣasaṃyutāḥ ||
yaḥ kaścid vañcakas teṣāṃ vijñātaḥ krayavikraye |
śapathaiḥ sa viśodhyaḥ syāt sarvavādeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |
daivarājakṛtadravyahāniviṣaye ‘py āha sa eva |
kṣayahānir yadā tatra daivarājakṛtād bhavet |
sarveṣām eva sā proktā kalpanīyā yathāṃśataḥ || iti |
kṣayāyaiva hāniḥ kṣayahāniḥ | a tūpacayārthaṃ vyayaḥ | prātisvikadoṣeṇa dravyanāśe sa evāha |
anirdiṣṭo vāryamāṇaḥ pramādād yas tu nāśayet |
tenaiva tad bhaved deyaṃ sarveṣāṃ samavāyinām || iti |
anirdiṣṭaḥ samavāyyananujñātaḥ | corādibhyaḥ | pālayitur lābhādhikyam astīty āha kātyāyanaḥ |
corataḥ salilād agner dravyaṃ yas tu samāharet |
tasyāṃśo daśamo deyaḥ sarvadravyeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |
samāharet svaśaktyā paripālayed ity arthaḥ | bṛhaspatir api |
[306]        daivarājabhayād yas tu svaśaktyā paripālayet |
tasyāṃśaṃ daśamaṃ datvā gṛhṇīyus te tataḥ param || iti |
yas tu samavāyibhiḥ prayuktaṃ dhanaṃ samavāyibhiḥ saha pratipādanādinā na sādhayati tasya lābhahānir iti | tad āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
samavetais tu yad dattaṃ prārthanīyaṃ tathaiva tat |
na yācate ca yaḥ kaścil lābhāt sa parihīyate || iti |
sarvānumataḥ sarveṣāṃ kāryaṃ eka eva kuryāt tad āha sa eva |
bahūnāṃ saṃmato yas tu dadyād eko dhanaṃ naraḥ |
karaṇaṃ kārayed vāpi sarvair eva kṛtaṃ bhavet || iti |
karaṇaṃ lekhyādikam | saṃbhūyakāriṇām ṛtvijāṃ kartavyam āha manuḥ |
ṛtvijaḥ samavetās tu yathā satre nimantritāḥ |
kuryur yathārhaṃ te karma gṛhṇīyur dakṣiṇāṃ tathā || iti |
tatheti karmānusāreṇa dakṣiṇāṃ gṛhṇīyur ity arthaḥ | tathā ca sa eva |
saṃbhūya svāni karmāṇi kurvadbhir iha mānavaiḥ |
anena vidhiyogena kartavyāṃśakalpanā || (MDh 8.211)
[307] iti | iyaṃ cāṃśakalpanā “tasya dvādaśaśataṃ dakṣiṇā” ity evaṃ kratusaṃbandhitvamātratayā vihitāyāṃ dakṣiṇāyām eva | nartvigviśeṣollekhena vihitāyām | ata evoktaṃ tenaiva |
rathaṃ hareta vādhvaryur brahmādhāne ca vājinam |
hotā vāti hared aśvam udgātā cāpy anaḥ kraye || iti | (MDh8.209)
dakṣiṇāṃśaprakalpanām āha sa eva |
sarveṣām ardhino mukhyās tadardhenārdhino ‘pare |
tṛtīyinas tṛtīyāṃśāś caturthāṃśās tu pādinaḥ || iti | (MDh 8.210)
sarveṣāṃ ṣoḍaśartvijāṃ madhye mukhyāś catvāro hotradhvaryubarhmodgātāras te gośatasya ardhinaḥ | sarveṣāṃ bhāgaparipūraṇopapattivaśāyātāṣṭacatvāriṃśadrūpārdhenārdhabhājaḥ | apare [308] maitrāvaruṇapratiprasthātṛbrāhmaṇācchaṃsiprastotāras tadardhinaḥ dhanamukhyāṃśasyārdhena caturviṃśatirūpeṇārdhabhājaḥ | ye punas tṛtīyino ‘chāvākaneṣṭāgnīdhraprahartāras te tṛtīyino mukhyāṃśasya ṣoḍaśabhāgarūpagṛtīyāṃśabhājaḥ | ye tu pādino grāvastotṛnetṛpotṛsubhrahmaṇyās te mukhyabhāgasya caturthāṃśena dvādaśagorūpeṇāṃśabhājaḥ | mukhyānāṃ caturṇāṃ mitho vibhāgaḥ samatvenaiva | evam eva tadanantarādīnāṃ api mitho vibhāgaḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanasūtram: “dvādaśa dvādaśadyebhyaḥ ṣaṭ ṣaṭ dvitīyebhyaś catasraś catasraḥ tṛtīyebhyas tisras tisra itarebhyaḥ” iti | svakīyakarmakalāpāṃśakartṛtvānusāreṇa bhāgo deya ity āha manuḥ |
ṛtvig yadi vṛto yajñe svakarma parihāpayet |
tasya karmānurūpeṇa deyo ‘ṃśaḥ sahakartṛbhiḥ || iti | (MDh 8.206)
sahakartṛbhiḥ saṃbhūyakāribhir ity arthaḥ | kṛtakarmāṃśānusāreṇa dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād ity uktaṃ tasya kvacid apavādam āha sa eva |
[309]        dakṣiṇāsu pradattāsu svakarma parihāpayan |
kṛtsnam eva labhetāṃśam anyenaiva ca kārayet || iti | (MDh 8.207)
anyena svasvagaṇavartināṃ madhye pratyāsannena | karmamadhye ṛtviṅmaraṇe nārada āha |
ṛtvijāṃ vyasane ‘py evam anyas tat karma vistaret |
labhate dakṣiṇābhāgaṃ sa tasmāt saṃprakalpitam || iti |
saṃbhūyakāriṇāṃ kṛṣikarāṇāṃ kartavyam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
vāhyavāhakabījādyaiḥ kṣetropakaraṇena ca |
ye samāḥ syus tu taiḥ sārdhaṃ kṛṣiḥ kāyā vijānatā || iti |
vāhyaṃ lāṅgalādiḥ | vāhako balivardādiḥ | kṣetreṣu varjanīyam āha sa eva |
parvate nagarābhyāśe tathā rājapathasya ca |
uṣaraṃ muṣikavyāptaṃ kṣetraṃ yatnena varjayet || iti |
vāhyeṣu vivarjanīyān āha sa eva |
[310]        kṛśātivṛddhaṃ kṣudraṃ ca rogiṇaṃ prapalāyinam |
kāṇaṃ khañjaṃ vinādadyāt vāhyaṃ prājñaḥ kṛṣīvalaḥ || iti |
prātisvikadoṣāt phalahānau viśeṣam āha sa eva |
vāhyabījātyayād yasya kṣetrahāniḥ prajāyate |
tenaiva sā pradātavyā sarveṣāṃ kṛṣijīvinām || iti |
vāhyabījagrahaṇaṃ kṛṣisādhanānām upalakṣaṇārtham | saṃbhūyakāriṇāṃ śilpināṃ lābhavibhāgam āha sa eva |
hemakārādayo yatra śilpaṃ saṃbhūya kurvate |
karmānurūpaṃ nirveśaṃ labheraṃs te yathāṃśataḥ || iti |
nirveśo bhṛtiḥ | kātyāyano ‘pi |
śilpākarajñakuśalā ācāryaś ceti śilpinaḥ |
ekadvitricaturbhāgān hareyus te yathāṃśataḥ || iti |
harmyādinirmāṇakṛtāṃ madhya mukhyabhāgam āha bṛhaspatiḥ|
harmyaṃ devagṛhaṃ vāpi dhārmiko yat purāṇi ca |
saṃbhūya kurvatāṃ teṣāṃ pramukho dvyaṃśam arhati || iti |
śilpināṃ svarūpam āha sa eva |
[311]        hiraṇyakupyasūtrāṇāṃ kāṣṭhapāṣāṇacarmaṇām |
saṃskartā tu kalābhijñaḥ śilpī prokto manīṣibhiḥ || iti |
kupyaṃ hemarūpyavyatiriktatrapusīsādikam | stenān pray āha sa eva |
svāmyājñayā tu yac coraiḥ paradeśāt samāhṛtam |
rājñe datvā tu ṣaḍbhāgaṃ bhajeyus te yathāṃśataḥ ||
caturo ‘ṃśān bhajen mukhyaḥ śūras tryaṃśam avāpnuyāt |
samarthas tu hared dvyaṃśaṃ śeṣās tv anye samāṃśinaḥ || iti |
paradeśād vairideśād ity arthaḥ | prabalavairideśād āhṛtadhanaviṣayam etat | durbalavairideśād āhṛtaviṣaye tv āha kātyāyanaḥ |
pararāṣṭrād dhanaṃ yasya coraiś ced ājñayāhṛtam |
rājñe daśāṃśam uddhṛtya vibhajeran yathāvidhi ||
caurāṇṃ mukhyabhūtas tu caturo ‘ṃśāṃs tathā haret |
śūro ‘ṃśāṃs trīn samartho dvau śeṣās tv ekaikam eva ca || iti |
hānir apy evam evety āha sa eva |
teṣāṃ cet prasṛtāṃ yo yo grahaṇaṃ samavāpnuyāt |
tanmokṣaṇārthaṃ yad dattaṃ vaheyus te yathāṃśataḥ || iti
nartakānāṃ bhāgakalpanāprakāram āha sa eva |
[312]        nartakānām eṣa eva dharmaḥ sadbhir udāhṛtaḥ |
tālajño labhate hy ardhaṃ gāyanās tu samāṃśinaḥ ||
pramukhā dvyaṃśam arhanti so ‘yaṃ saṃbhūyakurvatām || iti |
śilpināṃ mukhyaḥ proktaḥ | sa eṣa dharmo bhāgadvayarūpo nartakādīnāṃ saṃbhūyakarvatāṃ madhye mukhyasyodāhṛtaḥ | ayam eva vibhāgaḥ pūrvam akṛtasamavāyānāṃ saṃbhūyakāriṇāṃ draṣtavyaḥ | tathā ca sa eva |
vaṇijāṃ karṣaṇānāṃ ca corāṇāṃ śilpināṃ tathā |
aninyamyāṃśakartṝṇāṃ sarveṣām eva nirṇayaḥ || iti |
iti saṃbhūyasamuttānākhyaṃ padaṃ samāptam
atha saṃbhūyasamutthānopasaṃhārapūrvakaṃ dattāpradānikākhyaṃ
vyavahārapadam ucyate
tatra bṛhasptiḥ |
eṣākhilenābhihitā saṃbhūyottānaniṣkṛtiḥ |
adeyadeyadattānām adattānāṃ ca kathyate || iti |
adeyaṃ dānānarham, deyaṃ dānārham, dattaṃ sthiram aniviartanīyam, adattam anivartanīyam | ity evaṃ caturvidhānām adeyādīnāṃ svarūpaṃ kathyata ity arthaḥ | ata eva nāradaḥ |
datvā dravyam asamyag yaḥ punar adātum icchati |
dattāpradānikaṃ nāma tad vivādapadaṃ smṛtam ||
[313]        adeyam atha deyaṃ ca dattaṃ cādattam eva ca |
vyavahāreṣu vijñeyo dānamārgaś caturvidhaḥ || iti |
adeyasvarūpabhedān āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
sāmānyaṃ putradārādi sarvasvaṃ nyāsayācitam |
pratiśrutam athānyasya na deyaṃ tv aṣṭadhā smṛtam || iti |
sāmānyam anekasvatantrasvāmikaṃ rathādi | nārado ‘pi |
anvāhitaṃ yācitakam ādhiḥ sādhāraṇaṃ ca yat |
nikṣepaṃ putradāraṃ ca sarvasvaṃ cānvaye sati ||
āpatsv api hi kaṣṭāsu vartamānena dehinā |
adeyāny āhur ācāryā yac cānyasmai pratiśrutam || iti |
anvāhitādivat strīdhanam apy adeyam | ata eva dakṣaḥ |
sāmānyaṃ yācitaṃ nyāsa ādhir dārāś ca tad dhanam |
anvāhitaṃ ca nikṣepaṃ sarvasvaṃ cānvaye sati ||
āpatsv api na deyāni navavastūni paṇḍitaiḥ |
yo dadāti sa mūḍhātmā prāyaścittīyate naraḥ || iti |
adeyāni pratigrahe ca daṇḍo manunā ‘bhihitaḥ |
[314]        adeyaṃ yaś ca gṛhṇāti yaś cādeyaṃ prayacchati |
tāv ubhau coravac chāsyau daṇḍyau cottamasāhasam || iti |
adeyagrahaṇam adattasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | ata eva nāradaḥ |
gṛhṇāty adattaṃ yo lobhād yaś cādeyaṃ prayacchati |
daṇḍanīyāv ubhāv etau dharmajñena mahīkṣitā || iti |
kiṃ tarhi deyam ity apekṣite sa evāha |
kuṭumbabharaṇād dravyaṃ yat kiṃcid atiricyate |
tad deyam uparuddhyan yat adad doṣam avāpnuyāt || iti |
bhartavyaṃ kuṭumbam uparuddhyety arthaḥ | kātyāyano ‘pi |
sarvasvaṃ gṛhavarjaṃ tu kuṭumbabharaṇādhikam |
yad dravyaṃ tat svakaṃ deyam adeyaṃ syād ato ‘nyathā || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
svaṃ kuṭumbāvirodhena deyaṃ dārasutād ṛte | (YDh 2.175)
[315] iti | atra sutasyādeyatvam ekaputraviṣayam | tasyāpi dāne kṛte santānavicchedāpatteḥ | ata evaikasya putrasya dānaṃ niṣedhati vasiṣṭhaḥ: “na tv ekaṃ putraṃ dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyād vā | sa hi santānāya pūrveṣām” iti (VaDh 15.3–4) | tathā kātyāyano ‘pi |
ataś ca sutadārāṇāṃ vaśitvaṃ tv anuśāsane |
vikraye caiva dāne ca vaśitvaṃ na sute pituḥ || iti |
evamādīnī sutrasyādeyatvapratipādakāni vacanāny ekaputraviṣayāṇīty avagamyate | anekaputreṣ api mātāpitṛviyoga sahanakṣama eva deyaḥ,
vikrayaṃ caiva dānaṃ ca na neyāḥ syur anicchavaḥ |
dārāḥ putrāś ca sarvasvam ātmany eva tu yojayet ||
iti kātyāyanasmaraṇāt | na neyāḥ syur anicchava ity anāpadviṣayam,
āpatkāle ‘pi kartavyaṃ dānaṃ vikraya eva vā |
anyathā na pravarteta iti śāstraviniścayaḥ ||
iti tān evādhikṛtya tenaivoktatvāt | putrasya pratigrahaprakāraviśeṣo vasiṣṭhena darśitaḥ: [316] “putraṃ pratigrahīṣyan bandhūn āhūya rājani ca nivedya niveśanasya madhye vyāhṛtibhir hutvādūrabāndhavam asaṃnikṛṣṭam eva gṛhṇīyāt” iti (VaDh 15.6) | adūrabāndhavaṃ saṃnikṛṣṭamātulādibāndhavam | asaṃnikṛṣṭasaṃnikṛṣṭabhātṛputrādivyatiriktam eva | sthāvaraviṣaye deyaṃ dravyam āha prajāpatiḥ |
saptāgamād gṛhakṣetrād yad yat kṣetraṃ pracīyate |
pitryaṃ vātha svayaṃ prāptaṃ tad dātavyaṃ vivakṣitam || iti |
saptabhya āgamebhyo yat pracīyate samadhikaṃ syāt tad dātavyatvena vivakṣitam iti | svayaṃ prāptaṃ dravyam avibhaktadhanair bhrātṛbhir ananujñātam ap deyam “svecchadeyaṃ svayaṃ prāptam” iti bṛhaspativacanāt | yat tu tenaivoktam,
vibhaktā vāvibhaktā vā dāyādāḥ sthāvare samāḥ |
eko ‘py anīśaḥ sarvatra dānādhamanvikraye ||
[317] iti | tad avibhaktasthāvaraviṣayam saptānadhikasthāvaraviṣayaṃ vā, saptādhikasyaiva deyatvenābhidhānāt | kiṃcid bhartrā bhāryayānujñātam eva deyam | kiṃcin mukhyenāpi bhrātrādyanujñātam eva deyam | kiṃcid dāsena svārjitam api svāmyanujñātam eva deyam | tathā ca sa eva |
saudāyikaṃ kramāyātaṃ śauryaprāptaṃ ca yad bhavet |
strījñātisvāmyanujñātaṃ dattaṃ siddhim avāpnuyāt || iti |
saudāyikaṃ vivāhalabdham | kramāyātaṃ pitāmahādikramāyātam | strījñātyanumataṃ sāvaśeṣaṃ deyam,
vaivāhike kramāyāte sarvaṃ dānaṃ na vidyate |
iti tenaivoktatvāt |
itthaṃ deyādeyasvarūpaṃ nirūpitam
atha dattādattayos tu svarūpaṃ nirūpyate
tatra dattaṃ saptavidham adattaṃ ṣoḍaśātmakam | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
dattaṃ saptavidhaṃ proktam adattaṃ ṣoḍaśātmakam ||
paṇyamūlyaṃ bhṛtis tuṣṭyā snehāt pratyupakārataḥ |
strīśukānugrahārthaṃ ca dattaṃ dānavido viduḥ ||
adattaṃ tu kṣayakrodhaśokāvegaruganvitaiḥ |
tathotkocaparīhāsavyatyāsachalayogataḥ ||
[318]        bālamūḍhāsvatantrārtamattonmattāpavarjitam |
kartā mamāyaṃ karmeti pratilābheccayā ca yat ||
apātre pātram ity ukte kārye cādharmasaṃhite |
yad dattaṃ syād avijñānād adattam iti tat smṛtam || iti |
paṇyasya krītadravyasya mūlyam | bhṛtir vetanaṃ kṛtakarmaṇe dattam | tuṣṭyā bandicāraṇādibhyo dattam | snehād duhitrādibhyo dattam | pratyupakārataḥ upakṛtavate pratyupakārarūpeṇa dattam | strīśulkaṃ pariṇayanārthaṃ kanyājñātibhyo dattam | anugrahārtham adṛṣṭārthe dattam | tad etat paṇyamūlyādisaptavidhaṃ dattam eva na pratyāharaṇīyam | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
deyaṃ pratiśrutaṃ caiva datvā nāpaharet punaḥ | iti | (YDh 2.176)
bhayena bandigrahādibhyo dattam | krodhena putrādiviṣayakopaniryātanāyānyasmai dattam | putraviyogādinimittaśokāveśena dattam | ruganvitaiḥ asādhyarogapīḍitaiś ca yad dattam | utkocena kāryapratibandhanirāsārtham adhikṛtebhyo dattam | parihārenopahāsena dattam | dravyavyatyāsena dattam ekaḥ svaṃ dravyam anyasmai dadāti anyo ‘pi tasmai dadāti iti [319] dānavyatyāsena dattam | anyasmai dātavyam anyasmai dānam | chalayogataḥ śatadānam abhisaṃdhāya sahasram iti paribhāvya dattam | bālenāprāptaṣoḍaśavarṣeṇa dattam | mūḍhena lokavedānabhijñena dattam | asvatantreṇa putradāsādinā dattam | unmattena vātikādyunmādagrastena apavarjitaṃ dattam | ayaṃ madīyam idaṃ karma kariṣyatīti pratilābhecchayā pratilābham akurvāṇāya dattam | ayogyāya yogyoktimātreṇa yathā acaturvedāya caturvedo 'ham ity ukte dattam | yajñaṃ kariṣyāmīti dhanaṃ labdhvā dyūtādau viniyuñjānāya dattam | evaṃ ṣoḍaśaprakāram api dattaṃ punaḥ pratyāharaṇīyatvād adattam ity ucyate | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
kāmakrodhāsvatantrārtaklībonmattapramohitaiḥ |
vyatyāsaparihāsāc ca yad dattaṃ tat punar haret ||
yā tu kāryasya siddhyartham utkocā syāt pratiśrutā |
tasminn api prasiddhe ‘rthe na deyā syāt kathaṃcana ||
atha prāg eva dattā syāt pratidāpyaḥ sa tāṃ balāt |
daṇḍaṃ caikādaśaguṇam āhur gārgīyamānavāḥ || iti |
utkocasvarūpam āha sa eva |
[320]        stenasāhasikodṛttapāradārikasaṃgamāt |
darśanodvṛttanaṣṭasya tathāsatyapravartanāt ||
prāptam etais tu yat kiṃcid utkocākhyaṃ tad ucyate |
na dātā tatra daṇḍyaḥ syān madhyasthaś caiva doṣabhāg || iti |
madhyastha uktānuvādakaḥ | cakārād grāhakaḥ samuccīyate | tāv ubhau doṣabhājau daṇḍanīyāv ity arthaḥ | ārtadattasya dattatvaṃ dharmakāryavyatiriktaviṣayam | tathā ca sa eva |
svasthenārtena vā dattaṃ śrāvitaṃ dharmakāraṇāt |
adatvā tu mṛte dāpyas tatsuto nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti |
manur api sopādhikadānāder nivartanīyatām āha |
yogādhamanavikrītaṃ yogadānapratigraham |
yasya vāpy upadhiṃ paśyet tat sarvaṃ vinivartayet || iti | (MDh 8.165)
yoga upadhiḥ | adeyadānatatpratigrahayor daṇḍo nāradenoktaḥ |
[321]        gṛhṇāty adattaṃ yo lobhād yaś cādeyaṃ prayacchati |
adeyadāyako daṇḍyas tathādattapratīcchakaḥ || iti |
iti dattāpradānikam
atha vetanasyānapākarmākhyaṃ vivādapadam ucyate
tasya svarūpam āha nāradaḥ |
bhṛtyānāṃ vetansyokto dānādānavidhikramaḥ |
vetanasyānapākarma tad vivādapadaṃ smṛtam || iti |
vetanaṃ karmamūlyam | tasyānapākarma bhṛtyāyāsamarpaṇaṃ samarpitasya parāvartanaṃ vā | tatra samarpaṇe viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
bhṛtyāya vetanaṃ dadyāt karmasvāmī yathākramam |
ādau madhye ‘vasāne ca karmaṇo yad viniścitam || iti |
etāvad ahaṃ tava karmakaraṇāya dāsyāmīti bhāṣāyā abhāve viśeṣam āha sa eva |
bhṛtāv aniścitāyāṃ tu daśabhāgam avāpnuyuḥ |
lābhaṃ govīryaśasyānāṃ vaṇijo ‘tha kṛṣīvalāḥ ||
[322] iti | govīryaṃ pālyamānagavādiprabhavaṃ payaḥprabhṛti | yadi karmasvāmī bhṛtyāya daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ na prayacchati tadāsau rājñā dāpya ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
dāpyas tu daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ vāṇijyapaśuśasyataḥ |
aniścitya bhṛtiṃ yas tu kārayet sa mahīkṣitā || iti | (YDh 2.194)
yat tu bṛhaspatinoktam,
tribhāgaṃ pañcabhāgaṃ vā gṛhṇīyāt sīravāhakaḥ |
iti, tad āyāsasādhyākṛṣṭakṣetrakartṛviṣayam | tatrāpi tribhāgapañcabhāgau vyavasthayā vikalpitau veditavyau | tathā ca sa eva |
bhaktācchādabhṛtaḥ sīrād bhāgaṃ gṛhṇīta pañcakam |
jataśasyo tribhāgaṃ tu pragṛhṇīyāt tathābhṛtaḥ || iti |
aśanācchādānābyāṃ bhṛtaḥ kṛṣīvalaḥ kṣetrajātaśasyāt pañcamaṃ bhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | tābhyām abhṛtas tṛtīyaṃ bhāgam ity arthaḥ | etāvad dāsyāmīti paribhāṣāyāṃ satyām api kvacit tato nyūnaṃ svāmibuddhiparikalpitaṃ vetanam deyam | kvacit tato ‘py adhikaṃ deyam | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[323]        deśaṃ kālaṃ ca yo ‘tīyāl lābhaṃ kuryāc ca yo ‘nyathā |
tadā tu svāminaḥ chando ‘dhikaṃ deyam kṛta ‘dhike || iti | (YDh 2.195)
yaḥ svāmyājñām antareṇa vāṇijyādilābhasādhanadeśakālātikramaṃ karoti lābhaṃ ca bahutaravyayakaraṇād alpaṃ karoti tasmai svāmī svecchānusāreṇa kiṃcid dadyāt | yas tu svātantryeṇa bahulābhaṃ karoti tasmai paribhāṣitamūlyād adhikaṃ deyam ity arthaḥ | anekabhṛtyakartṛkarmaṇi vetanārpaṇaprakāram āha sa eva |
yo yāvat kurute karma tāvat tasya tu vetanam |
ubhayor apy asādhyaṃ cet sādhye kuryād yathāśrutam || iti | (YDh 2.196)
yadā punar ekaṃ karma niyatavetanam ubhābhyāṃ bahibhir vā kriyamāṇam ubhayor apy asādhyaṃ ced ubhābhyām evāparisamāpitam tadā yo yāvat karma karoti tasmai tatkarmānusāreṇa madhyasthakalpitaṃ vetanaṃ deyam | na punaḥ samam | sādhye ubhābhyāṃ karmaṇi parisamāpite tu yathāśrutaṃ yathāvat paribhāṣitaṃ tāvad ubhābhyāṃ deyam | na punaḥ pratyekaṃ kṛtsnaṃ vetanaṃ deyam | nāpi karmānurūpaṃ parikalpya deyam | bhṛtyānāṃ kartṛtvam āha nāradaḥ |
[324]        karmopakaraṇaṃ teṣāṃ kriyāṃ prati yathāhitam |
āptabhāvena tad rakṣyaṃ na jaihmyena kadācana || iti |
teṣāṃ karmasvāmināṃ karmopakaraṇaṃ lāṅgalādi | kriyām uddiśya yasmin bhṛtye nihitaṃ tena sarvadā niḥśāṭhyena rakṣyam ity arthaḥ | bṛhaspatir api |
bhṛtakas tu na kurvīta svāminā śāṭhyam anv api |
bhṛtihāniṃ samāpnoti tadā vādaḥ pravartate || iti |
yas tu bhṛtiṃ svīkrtya karma na karoti taṃ praty āha sa eva |
gṛhītavetanaḥ karma na karoti yadā mṛtaḥ |
samarthaś ced damaṃ dāpyo dviguṇaṃ tac ca vetanam || iti |
agṛhītavetanaviṣaye yājñavalkya āha |
agṛhīte samaṃ dāpyo bhṛtyai rakṣya upaskaraḥ || (YDh 2.193)
[325] iti | samaṃ yāvatā vetanena bhṛtyatvam aṅgīkṛtaṃ tāvad eva svāmine dadyān na tu rājñe daṇḍam ity arthaḥ | yad vā atrāṅgīkṛtavetanaṃ datvā balāt kārayitavyaḥ | tad āha nāradaḥ |
karmākurvan pratiśrutya kāryo datvā bhṛtiṃ balāt |
bhṛtiṃ gṛhītvākurvāṇaḥ dviguṇāṃ bhṛtim āpnuyāt || iti |
pratiśrutyeti prārambasyāpy upalakṣaṇārtham | tad āha kātyāyanaḥ |
karmārambhaṃ tu yaḥ kṛtvā sarvaṃ naiva tu kārayet |
balāt kārayitavyo ‘sāv akurvan daṇḍam arhati || iti |
daṇḍaparimāṇam āha vṛddhamanuḥ |
pratiśrutya na kuryād yaḥ sa kāryaḥ syād balād api |
sa cen na kuryat tat karma prāpnuyād dviśataṃ damam || iti |
dviśataṃ kārṣāpaṇadviśatam ity arthaḥ | yat tu manuvacanam,
[326]         bhṛtyo ‘nārto na kuryād yo darpāt karma yathoditam |
sa daṇḍyaḥ kṛṣṇalāny aṣṭau na deyaṃ tasya vetanam || (MDh 8.215)
iti, tad ardhāvaśeṣitaviṣayam | kiṃcinmātrāvaśeṣe tu daṇḍavarjavetanādānam | tad āha sa eva |
yathoktam ārtaḥ svastho vā yas tu karma na kārayet |
na tasya vetanaṃ deyam alponasyāpi karmaṇaḥ || iti | (MDh 8.17)
yas tu kālaviśeṣāvadhikaṃ karma pratijñāya kālāt pūrvam eva karma tyajati taṃ praty āha nāradaḥ |
kāle ‘pūrṇe tyajan karma bhṛter nāśam avāpnuyāt |
svāmidoṣād akaraṇe yāvad bhṛtim avāpnuyāt || iti |
svāmidoṣāt pāruṣyakaraṇādisvāmidoṣāt | nāradaḥ |
[327]         bhāṇḍaṃ vyasan āgacched yadi vāhakadoṣataḥ |
dāpyo yat tatra naṣṭaṃ syād daivarājakṛtād ṛte || iti |
vāhakadoṣataḥ bhṛtakadoṣataḥ | vṛddhamanuḥ |
pramādān nāśitaṃ dāpyaḥ samaṃ dvir drohanāśitam |
na tu dāpyo hṛtaṃ corair dagdham ūḍhaṃ jalena vā || iti |
drohanāśitaṃ tīvraprahārādinā droheṇa nāśitam | dvir dāpyo dviguṇaṃ dāpya ity arthaḥ | prasthānavighnakaṃ bhṛtyaṃ praty āha katyāyanaḥ |
vighnayan vāhako dāpyaḥ prasthāne (?) dviguṇām bhṛtim | iti |
vṛddhamanuḥ |
yaḥ karmakāle saṃprāpte na kuryād vighnam ācaret |
uddhṛtvyānyas tu kāryaḥ syāt sa dāpyo dviguṇāṃ bhṛtim || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
[328]         arājadaivikaṃ naṣṭaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ dāpyas tu vāhakaḥ |
prasthānavighankṛc caiva pradāpyo dviguṇāṃ bhṛtim ||
prakrānte saptamaṃ bhāgaṃ caturthaṃ pathi saṃtyajan |
bhṛtim arthapathe sarvāṃ pradāpyas tyājako ‘pi ca || iti | (YDh 2.197–98)
arājadaivikaṃ na vidyate rājadaivikaṃ yasya bhāṇḍasya tad yadi prajñāhīnatayā vahakena nāśitaṃ tadā tanmūlyānusāreṇa tadbhāṇḍaṃ dāpanīyaḥ | yas tu prasthānalagnasamaya eva svābhyupāttaṃ karma tyajan prasthānavighnaṃ karoti tadāsau dviguṇāṃ bhṛtiṃ dāpyaḥ | yas tu bhṛtyāntaropādānāvasarasaṃbhave svāṅgīkṛtaṃ karma tyajati asau bhṛtyaḥ saptamaṃ bhāgaṃ dāpyaḥ | yaḥ punaḥ pathi prakrānte gamane vartamāne sati karma tyajati sa bhṛteś caturthaṃ bhāgaṃ dāpyaḥ | ardhapathe tyajan sarvāṃ bhṛtiṃ dāpanīyaḥ | yas tu svāmī bhṛtyaṃ svayam [329] eva karma tyājayati pūrvoktapradeśeṣu asāv api pūrvoktasaptamabhāgādikaṃ dāpanīyaḥ | etac cāvyādhitādiviṣayam, vyādhitasyāparādhābhāvāt | yadā punar vyādhito vyādhyapagame ‘ntaritadivasān parigaṇayya pūrayati tadā labhetaiva sarvāṃ bhṛtim | tad āha manuḥ |
ārtas tu kuryāt svasthaḥ san yathābhāṣitam āditaḥ |
sudīrghasyāpi kālasya tal labhetaiva vetanam || iti | (MDh 8.216)
tyājakasya svāminaś caturthabhāgādidāpanam avikrītabhāṇḍaviṣayam | vikrīte tu bhāṇḍe viśeṣo vṛddhamanunābhihitaḥ |
pathi vikrīya tad bhāṇḍaṃ vaṇig bhṛtyaṃ tyajed yadi |
atha tasyāpi deyaṃ syād bhṛter ardhaṃ labheta saḥ || iti |
āsedhena pratibaddhabhāṇḍaviṣaye rājādyapahṛtabhāṇḍaviṣaye cāha kātyāyanaḥ |
yathā ca pathi tad bhāṇḍam āsiddhyeta hriteta vā |
yāvān adhvā gatas tena prāpnuyāt tāvato bhṛtim || iti |
bhāṭakasvīkṛtena yānādinā bhāṇḍanetāraṃ praty āha nāradaḥ |
[330]         anayan bhāṭayitvā tu bhāṇḍavān yānavāhane |
dāpyo bhṛteś caturbhāgaṃ sarvām ardhapathe tyajan ||
anayan vāhako ‘py evaṃ bhrtihānim avāpnuyāt || iti |
yaḥ śakaṭādikaṃ bhāṭayitvā tad evopakāraśūnyam ādāya deśāntaraṃ gacchati bhāṭakaṃ ca na prayacchati taṃ praty āha vṛddhamanuḥ |
yo bhāṭayitvā śakaṭaṃ nītvā cānyatra gacchati |
bhāṇḍaṃ na dadyāt parato dāpyaḥ syāt so ‘pi bhāṭakam || iti |
bhāṭakakāryopādhiparikrayamūlyam ārūḍhasyāpi akṛtopakārasyāpi svakṛte kṛtye samanantaram anarpitahastyaśvādeḥ samarpaṇaparyanaṃ bhāṭakānivṛttir ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
hastyaśvagokharoṣṭrādīn gṛhītvā bhāṭakena yaḥ |
niryayet kṛtakṛtyaḥ san strīvat dāpyaḥ sa bhāṭakam ||
[331]         gṛhavāryārpaṇādīni gṛhītvā bhāṭakena yaḥ |
svāmino nārpayed yāvat tāvad dāpyaḥ sa bhāṭakam || iti |
vāriśabdena tadādhāro lakṣyate | upakāraśabdena tadādhāro lakṣyate | parabhūmau gṛhanirmāṇāya bhāṭakadātāraṃ praty āha nāradaḥ |
parabhūmau gṛhaṃ kṛtvā stomaṃ datvā vaset tu yaḥ |
sa tad gṛhītvā nirgacchet tṛṇakāṣṭhāni ceṣṭakām || iti |
tathā stomo bhāṭakam | stomāpradāne prāha sa eva |
stomād vinā vasitvā tu parabhūmāv aniścitaḥ |
nirgacchaṃs tṛṇakāṣṭhādi na gṛhṇīyāt kathaṃcana ||
yāny eva tṛṇakāṣṭhāni tv iṣṭakāviniveśitāḥ |
vinirgacchaṃs tu tat sarvaṃ bhūmisvāmini vedayet ||
[332] iti | aniścitatṛṇakāṣṭhādigrahaṇaparibhāṣāyām ity arthaḥ | paribhāṣite tu yathā paribhāṣā tatheti | vedayet nivedayed ity arthaḥ | bhāṭakaṃ datvā darvyādyarpaṇārthaṃ gṛhītamaṇikādipātrabhedanādāv apy āha sa eva |
stomavāhīni bhāṇḍāni pūrṇakālāny upānayet |
grahītur āvahed bhagnaṃ naṣṭaṃ vānyatra saṃplavāt || iti |
saṃplavaḥ parasparasaṃgharṣaḥ | tenālpakena kārtsnyena vā bhinnaṃ pūrvavat kṛtvā bhāṇḍaṃ vā tanmūlyaṃ vā svāmine deyam | saṃplavād anyatra bhede tu bhāṭakagrahītur eva tad ity arthaḥ | kṛtakarmaṇe bhṛtyāya vetanādātāraṃ praty āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
kṛte karmaṇi yaḥ svāmī na dadyād vetanaṃ bhṛte |
rājñā dāpayitavyaḥ syād vinayaṃ cānurūpataḥ || iti |
rugṇaṃ bhṛtyaṃ pathi tyajato daṇḍam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
tyajet pathi sahāyaṃ yo śrāntaṃ rogārtam eva ca |
prāpnuyāt sāhasaṃ pūrvaṃ grāme tryaham apālayan || iti |
paṇyastrī tadupabhoktṛviṣaye tv āha nāradaḥ |
[333]         śulkaṃ gṛhītvā paṇyastrī necchantī dviguṇaṃ vahet |
anicchan śulkadātāpi śulkahānim avāpnuyāt || iti |
etad avyādhitādiviṣayam | vyādhitaviṣaye tu smṛtyantaram |
vyādhitā saṃbhramā vyagrā rājadharmaparāyaṇā |
āmantritā ca nāgacched avācyā vaḍavā smṛtā || iti |
atyantāvaśyakeṣu kāryeṣu jātasaṃbhramā saṃbhramapadena uktā | tatraiva vyākulā vyagrā | vaḍavā dāsī | dāsīgrahaṇam atra paṇyastrīpradarśanārtham | upabhoktāraṃ praty āha nāradaḥ |
aprayacchan tathā śulkam anubhūya pumān striyam |
akrameṇa tu saṃgacched ghātayed vā nakhādibhiḥ ||
ayonau yaḥ samākrāmed bahubhir vā vivāsayet |
śulkaṃ tv aṣṭaguṇaṃ dāpyo vinayaṃ tāvad eva tu || iti |
paṇyastriyās tv aparādhe daṇḍādikaṃ matsyapurāṇe ‘bhihitam |
[334]         gṛhītvā vetanaṃ veśyā lobhād anyatra gacchati |
tāṃ damaṃ dāpayed dadyād itarasyāpi bhāṭakam || iti | (MatsPu 23.7)
atra nirṇayam āha nāradaḥ |
veśyāpradhānā ye tatra kāmukās tadgṛhoṣitāḥ |
tatsamuttheṣu kāryeṣu nirṇayaṃ saṃśaye viduḥ || iti |
itthaṃ abhyetyāśruśrūṣākhyavivādapadāntargatavetanasyānapākarmākhyaṃ vivādapadaṃ manūktoddeśakramānusāreṇābhihitam |
iti vetanasyānapākarmākhyaṃ vivādapadam abhihitam
athedānīm abhyuteyāśuśrūṣākhyaṃ vivādapadam ahibhīyate
tasya svarūpaṃ nārada āha |
abhyupetya ca śuśrūṣāṃ yas tāṃ na pratipadyate |
aśuśrūṣābhyupetyaitad vivādapadam ucyate || iti |
ājñākaraṇaṃ śuśrūṣā | śuśrūṣakaś ca pañcaprakāraḥ | tathā ca sa eva |
[335]         śuśrūṣakaḥ pañcavidhaḥ śāstre dṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ |
caturvidhaḥ karmakaraḥ śeṣā dāsās tripañcakāḥ ||
śiṣyāntevāsibhṛtakāḥ caturthas tv adhikarmakṛt |
ete karmakarā jñeyā dāsās tu gṛhajādayaḥ ||
sāmānyam asvatantratvaṃ teṣām āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
jātakarmakaras tūkto viśeṣo vṛttitas tathā ||
karmāpi dvividhaṃ jñeyam aśubhaṃ śubham eva ca |
aśubhaṃ dāsakarmoktaṃ śubhaṃ karmakare smṛtam ||
gṛhadvārāśucisthānarathyāvaskaraśodhanam |
guhyāṅgasparśanocchiṣṭaviṇmūtragrahaṇojjhanam ||
icchataḥ svāminaś cāṅgair upasthānam athāntataḥ |
aśubhaṃ karma vijñeyaṃ śubham anyad ataḥ param || iti |
tatra śiṣyo vedavidyārthī | antevāsī śilpaśikṣārthī | mūlyena yaḥ karma karoti sa bhṛtakaḥ | adhikarmakṛt [336] karmakurvatām adhiṣṭhātā | aśucisthānam ucchiṣṭaprakṣepārthaṃ gartādikam | avaskaraḥ gṛhasaṃmārjitapāṃsvādinicayatyāgasthānam | bhṛtakaś ca trividhaḥ | tad uktaṃ tenaiva |
uttamaḥ kārykartā ca madhyamas tu kṛṣīvalaḥ |
adhamo bhāravāhī syād ity evaṃ trividho bhṛtaḥ || iti |
dāsasvarūpam api tenaiva darśitam |
gṛhajātas tathā krīto labdho dāyād upāgataḥ |
annākālabhṛtas tadvad āhitaḥ svāminā ca yaḥ ||
mokṣito mahataś carṇād yuddhe prāptaḥ paṇe jitaḥ |
tavāham ity upagataḥ pravrajyāvasitaḥ kṛtaḥ ||
bhaktadāsaś ca vijñeyas tathaiva vaḍavāhṛtaḥ |
vikretā cātmanaḥ śāstre dāsāḥ pañcadaś smṛtāḥ || iti |
gṛhajātaḥ svagṛhe dāsyāṃ jātaḥ | krīto mūlyena | labdha pratigrahādinā | dāyāgato rikthagrāhitvena prāptaḥ | annākālabhṛtaḥ durbhikṣe dāsatvāya poṣitaḥ | āhitaḥ svāminā [337] dhanagrahaṇenādhīnatāṃ nītaḥ | ṛṇamokṣitaḥ ṛṇamocanapratyupakāratayā dāsatvam abhyupagataḥ | yuddhaprāptaḥ samare vijitya gṛhītaḥ | paṇe vijitaḥ dāsatvapaṇake dyūtādau jitaḥ | tavāham ity upagataḥ tava dāso ‘smīti svayam evāgataḥ | pravrajyāvasitaḥ pravrajyātaś cyutaḥ | kṛtaḥ kṛtakālaḥ etāvatkālaṃ tvaṃ maddāsa ity upagata iti | bhaktadāsaḥ sarvakālaṃ bhaktārthe eva dāsatvam abhyupagataḥ | vaḍavayā gṛhadāsyā āhṛtaḥ tallobhena tām udvāhya dāsatvena praviṣṭaḥ | yaḥ ātmānaṃ vikrīṇīte asāv ātmavikretā | evaṃ pañcadaśaprakārāḥ | yat tu manunoktam,
dhvajāhṛto bhaktadāso gṛhajaḥ krītadattrimau |
paitṛko daṇḍadāsaś ca saptaite dāsayonayaḥ || (MDh 8.415)
iti, tat teṣāṃ dāsatvapratipādanārtham | na tu parisaṃkhyārtham | atra śiṣyāṇāṃ karmakṛtau viśeṣo nāradenoktaḥ |
ā vidyāgrahaṇāc chiṣyaḥ śuśrūṣet prayato gurum |
tadvṛttir gurudāreṣu guruputre tathaiva ca || iti |
vidyā cātra trayī | tad uktaṃ bṛhaspatinā |
[338]         vidyā trayī samākhyātā ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇā |
tadarthaṃ guruśuśrūṣāṃ prakuryāc ca pracoditām || iti |
antevāsinām api karmakṛtau viśeṣas tenaivoktaḥ |
vijñānam ucyate śilpaṃ hemarūpyādisaṃskṛtiḥ |
nṛtyādikaṃ ca tatprāptaṃ kuryāt karma guror gṛhe || iti |
nārado ‘pi |
svaṃ śilpam icchan vā hartuṃ bāndhavānām anujñayā |
ācāryasya vased ante kālaṃ kṛtvā suniścitam || iti |
ācāryasyāpi kartavyam āha sa eva |
ācāryaḥ śikṣayed enaṃ svagṛhe dattabhojanam |
na cānyat kārayet karma putravac cainam ācaret || iti |
anyakarmakārakam ācāryaṃ praty āha kātyāyanaḥ |
yas tu na grāhayec chilpaṃ karmāṇy anyāni kārayet |
prāpnuyāt sāhasaṃ pūrvaṃ tasmāc chiṣyo nivartate || iti |
praibhāṣitakālāt prāg eva vidyāprāptāv api tāvat kālaṃ vased ity āha nāradaḥ |
[339]         śikṣito ‘pi kṛtaṃ kālam antevāsī samāpayet |
tatra karma ca yat kuryād ācāryasyaiva tatphalam || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
kṛtaśilpo ‘pi nivaset kṛtakālaṃ guror gṛhe |
antevāsī guruprāptabhojanas tatphalapradaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.184)
duṣṭaṃ praty āha nāradaḥ |
śikṣayan tam aduṣṭaṃ ca yas tv ācāryaṃ parityajet |
balād vāsayitavyaḥ syād vadhabandhaṃ ca so ‘rhati || iti |
vadho ‘tra tāḍanādiḥ | paribhāṣitakālasaṃpūrtau kartavyam āha nāradaḥ |
gṛhītaśilpaḥ samaye kṛtvācāryaṃ pradakṣiṇam |
śaktitaś cānumānyainam antevāsī nivartate || iti |
bhṛtakānām api bhṛtikṛtaḥ kālakṛtaś ca viśeṣo bṛhaspatinā darśitaḥ |
yo bhuṅkte paradāsīṃ tu sa jñeyo vaḍavābhṛtaḥ |
karma tatsvāminaḥ kryād yathānnena bhṛto naraḥ ||
bahudhārthabhṛtaḥ. proktas tathā bhāgabhṛto ‘paraḥ |
hīnamadhyottamatvaṃ ca sarveṣām eva coditam ||
[340]         dinamāsārdhaṣaṇmāsatrimāsābdabhṛtas tathā |
karma kuryāt pratijñātaṃ labhate paribhāṣitam || iti |
arthabhṛtasya bahudhātvaṃ samarthālpatvamahatvābhyāṃ draṣṭavyam | te cālpatvamahate śaktyanusārato draṣṭavye | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
bhṛtyas tu trividho jñeyaḥ uttamo madhyamo ‘dhamaḥ |
śaktibhaktānusārābhyāṃ teṣāṃ karmāśrayā bhṛtiḥ || iti |
bhāgabhṛtasya dvaividhyam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
dviprakāro bhāgabhṛtaḥ kṛṣaṇo jīvitaḥ smṛtaḥ |
jātasasyāt tathā kṣīrāl labhate tu na saṃśayaḥ || iti |
adhikarmakṛtasya svarūpam āha nāradaḥ |
artheṣv adhikṛto yaḥ syāt kuṭumbasya tathopari |
so ‘dhikarmakṛto jñeyaḥ sa na kauṭumbikaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
evaṃ nirūpitebhyaḥ śiṣyāntevāsibhyaḥ bhṛtakādikarma karebhyo dāsānāṃ bhedaṃ dāsaśabdavyutpattidarśanamukhenāha kātyāyanaḥ |
svatantrasyātmano dānād dāsatvaṃ dāravad bhṛguḥ |
[341] iti | ayam arthaḥ | yathā bhartuḥ saṃbhogārthaṃ svaśarīradānād dāratvaṃ tathā svatantrasyātmano dānād dāsatvam – iti bhṛgur ācāryo manyate iti | tena cātyantapārārthyadarśanam āsādya śuśrūṣakāḥ dāsāḥ | pārārthyamātram āsādya śuśrūṣakāḥ karmakarā ity uktaṃ bhavati | dāsatvaṃ ca brāhmaṇavyatirikteṣv eva triṣu varṇeṣu vijñeyam, “dāsyaṃ viprasya na kvacit” iti tenaivoktatvāt | teṣv api dāsyam ānulomyenaivety āha sa eva |
varṇānām ānulomyena dāsyaṃ na pratilomataḥ |
rājanyavaiśyaśūdrāṇāṃ tyajatāṃ hi svatantarām || iti |
prātilomyena dāsatvapratiṣedhaḥ svadharmaparityāgibhyo patitebhyo ‘nyatra draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
varṇānāṃ prātilomyena dāsatvaṃ na vidhīyate |
svadharmatyāgino ‘nyatra dāravad dāsatā matā ||
[342] iti | dāravad dāsatā mateti vacanāt brāhmaṇasya savarṇaṃ prati dāsatvam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
asavarṇe tu viprasya dāsatvaṃ naiva kārayet | iti |
yadi brāhmaṇaḥ svecchayā dāsyaṃ bhajate tadāsau nāśubhaṃ karma kuryād ity āha sa eva |
śrutādhyayanasaṃpannaṃ tadūnaṃ karma kāmataḥ |
tatrāpi nāśubhaṃ kiṃcit prakurvīta dvijottamaḥ || iti |
ūnaṃ hīnam api karma kāmato vetanagrahaṇam antareṇa svecchayā prahitārtham | kṣatriyavaiśyaviṣaye svāminaḥ kartavyam āha manuḥ |
kṣatriyaṃ caiva vaiśyaṃ ca brāhmaṇo ‘vṛttikarśitau |
bibhṛyād ānṛśaṃsyena svāni karmāṇi kārayet || iti | (MDh 8.411)
yas tu dvijātiṃ balād dāsyaṃ karma kārayati tasya daṇḍam āha sa eva |
[343]         dāsyaṃ tu kārayen mohād brāhmaṇaḥ saṃskṛtān dvijān |
anicchataḥ prābhavatvād rājñā daṇḍyaḥ śatāni ṣaṭ || iti | (MDh 8.412)
prabhavato bhāvaḥ prābhavatvaṃ tasmād iti | śūdraṃ tu yathākatham api dāsyaṃ kārayed ity āha sa eva |
śūdraṃ tu kārayed dāsyaṃ krītam akrītam eva ca |
dāsyāyaiva hi sṛṣṭo ‘sau svayam eva svayaṃbhuvā || iti | (MDh 9.413)
pañcadaśaprakārāṇāṃ dāsānāṃ madhye gṛhajātakrītalabdhadāyāgatānāṃ caturṇāṃ dāsatvaṃ svāmiprasādād eva mucyate, nānyathety āha nāradaḥ |
tatra pūrvaś caturvargo dāsatvān na vimucyate |
prasādāt svāmino ‘nyatra dāsyam eṣāṃ kramāgatam || iti |
ātmavikretur api dāsatvaṃ svāmiprasādād anyato nāpaitīty āha nāradaḥ |
[344]         vikrīṇīte ‘svatantraḥ san ya ātmānaṃ narādhamaḥ |
sa jaghanyatamas teṣāṃ so ‘pi dāsyān na mucyate || iti |
pravrajyāvasitasyāpi dāsyamokṣo nāstīty āha sa eva |
rājña eva hi dāsaḥ syāt pravrajyāvasito naraḥ |
na tasya pratimokṣo ‘sti na viśuddhiḥ kathaṃcana || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
pravrajyāvasito rājño dāsa āmaraṇāntikam | iti | (YDh 2.183)
pravrajāvasitasya dāsatvaṃ brāhmaṇetaraviṣayam | brāhmaṇas tu nirvāsya ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
[345]         pravrajyāvasitā ye tu trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ |
nirvāsaṃ kārayed vipraṃ dāsatvaṃ kṣatriyaṃ viśaḥ || iti |
nirvāsanaprakāram āha nāradaḥ |
pārivrajyaṃ gṛhitvā tu yaḥ svadharme na tiṣṭhati |
śvapadenāṅkayitvā taṃ rājā śīghraṃ pravāsayet || iti |
pravrajyāvasitātmavikretṛvyatirikānām annākālabhṛtādīnāṃ dāsyāpanayanaprakāram āha sa eva |
annākālabhṛto dāsyān mucyate godvayaṃ dadat |
tadbhakṣitaṃ yad durbhikṣe na tat śudhyeta karmaṇā ||
bhaktasyotkṣepaṇenaiva bhaktadāsaḥ pramucyate |
āhito ‘pi dhanaṃ datvā svāmī yady enam uddharet ||
[346]         svaparyāptam ṛṇaṃ datvā tadṛṇāt sa vimucyate |
tavāham ity upagato yuddhe prāptaḥ paṇe jitaḥ ||
pratiśīrṣapradānena mucyeraṃs tulyakarmaṇā |
kṛtakālavyapagamāt kṛtako ‘pi vimucyate ||
nigrahād vaḍavāyās tu mucyate vaḍavāhṛtaḥ || iti |
svāminaḥ prāṇarakṣaṇād api gṛhajātādayaḥ sarve dāsyān mucyanta ity āha nāradaḥ |
yaś caiṣāṃ svāminaṃ kaścin mocayet prāṇasaṃśayāt |
dāsatvāt sa vimucyeta putrabhāgaṃ labheta ca || iti |
dāsābhāsānāṃ mocanam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
balād dāsīkṛtaś corair vikrītaś cāpi mucyate | iti | (YDh 2.182)
cakārād āhito dattaś ca gṛhyate | nārado ‘pi |
corāpahṛtavikrītā ye ca dāsīkṛtā balāt |
rājñā mocayitavyās te dāsatvaṃ teṣu niṣyate || iti |
yat tv ekasya pūrvaṃ dāsyam aṅgīkṛtya parasyāpi dāsatvam aṅgīkaroti asāv apareṇa visarjanīya ity āha sa eva |
tavāham iti vātmānaṃ yo ‘svatantraḥ prayacchati |
na sa taṃ prāpnuyāt kāmaṃ pūrvasvāmī labheta tam || iti |
dāsavimokṣaṇeti kartavyatām āha sa eva |
svadāsam icched yaḥ kartum dāsaṃ prītamānasaḥ |
skandhād ādāya tasyāsau bhindyāt kumbhaṃ sahāmbhasā ||
sākṣatābhiḥ sapuṣpābhir mūrdhany adbhir avākiret |
adāsa iti coktvā triḥ prāṅmukhas tu tathotsṛjet ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vaktavyaḥ svāmyanugrahapālitaḥ |
bhojyānno ‘tha pratigrāhyo bhavaty abhimataḥ satām || iti |
ity abhyupetyāśruśrūṣākhyaṃ vivādapadaṃ samāptam
[348]
atha saṃvidvyatikramākhyavivādapadasya vidhir ucyate
tasya lakṣaṇaṃ nāradena vyatirekamukhena darśitam |
pāṣaṇḍanaigamādīnāṃ sthitiḥ samaya ucyate |
samayasyānapākarma tad vivādapadaṃ smṛtam || iti |
samayasyānapākarma avyatikarmaḥ samayaparipālanam | tadvytikramamāṇaṃ vivādapadaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | tadupayoginam artham āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
vedavidyāvido viprān śrotriyāṃś cāgnihotriṇaḥ |
āhṛtya sthāpayet tatra teṣāṃ vṛttiṃ prakalpayet || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
rājā kṛtvā pure sthānaṃ brāhmaṇān nyasya tatra tu |
traividyān vṛttimad brūyāt svadharmaḥ pālyatām iti || iti | (YDh 2.185)
brāhmaṇān traividyān vedatrayasaṃpannān vṛttimat bhūri hiraṇyādisaṃpannaṃ kṛtvā svadharmo varṇāśramaśrutismṛtivihito bhagavadbhir anuṣṭhīyatām iti tān brūyāt | vṛttisaṃpattiś ca bṛhaspatinā darśitā |
[349]        anācchedyakarās tebhyaḥ pradadyād gṛhabhūmayaḥ |
bhuktabhāvyāś ca nṛpatir lekhayitvā svaśāsane || iti |
anācchedyakarā agrāhyakarāḥ bhuktā bhāvyās tu na viśrutakarāḥ | āgāminṛpatibhir agrāhyakarā yā bhūmayas tāḥ svaśāsane likhitvā tebhyo dadyād ity arthaḥ |
teṣāṃ kartavyam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ tathā |
paurāṇāṃ karma kuryus te saṃdigdhe ‘rthe ca nirṇayam || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
nijadharmāvirodhena yas tu sāmayiko bhavet |
so ‘pi yatnena saṃrakṣyo dharmo rājakṛtaś ca yaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.186)
śrautasmārtadharmānupamardena gotrācārekṣaṇadevagṛhapālanādirūpo yo dharmaḥ samayān niṣpanno bhavet so ‘pi yatnena pālanīyaḥ | tathā rājñā ca nijadharmāvirodhenaiva yāvat pathikabhojanaṃ deyam | asmadarātimaṇḍalaṃ turaṅgādayo na prasthāpanīyā [350] ity evaṃrūpaḥ samayaniṣpannaḥ so ‘pi rakṣaṇīyaḥ | evaṃ rājaniyuktasamudāyaviśeṣasya kartavyaviśeṣo ‘bhihitaḥ | grāmādisarvasamudāyānāṃ tu sādāraṇakāryam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
grāmaśreṇīgaṇānāṃ ca saṅketaḥ samayakriyā |
bādhākāle tu sā kāryā dharmakārye tathaiva ca ||
vāṭadoramaye bhādhā sarvasādhāraṇī smṛtā |
tatropaśamanaṃ kāryaṃ sarveṇaikena kenacit || iti |
cakāreṇa pāṣaṇḍanaigamādīnāṃ copasaṃgrahaḥ | tataś ca grāmaśreṇīgaṇapāṣaṇḍanaigamādīnām upadravakāle dharmakārye ca yāṃ pāribhāṣikīṃ samayakriyāṃ vinā upadravo duḥpariharaḥ, dharmakāryaṃ ca duḥsādhyaṃ sā pāribhāṣikī samayakriyā sarvair militaiḥ kāryā | vāṭo vṛkaḥ | vāṭacorebhyo bhaye prāpte tadā coropaśamanaṃ sarvaiḥ saṃbhūya kartavyam ity arthaḥ | dharmakārye tu viśeṣaḥ tenaivoktaḥ |
sabhā prapā devagṛhaṃ taḍāgārām asaṃskṛtiḥ |
tathānāthadaridrāṇāṃ saṃskāro yajanakriyā ||
kulāyanaṃ nirodhaś ca kāryam asmābhir aṃśataḥ |
yat tv evaṃ likhitaṃ patraṃ dharmyā sā samayakriyā ||
[351]         pālanīyā samastais tu yaḥ samartho visaṃvadet |
sarvasvaharaṇaṃ daṇḍaḥ tasya nirvāsanaṃ purāt || iti |
yajanakriyā somayāgādikartṛbhyo dānam | kulāyanaṃ durbhikṣādipīḍitavṛthāgamanam | tasmin gate sati yat saṃvidhānaṃ vidheyaṃ tad eva tacchabdenocyate | nirodhaḥ durbhikṣādyapagamaparyantaṃ dhāraṇam | aṃśataḥ gṛhakṣetrapuruṣādiprayuktasaṃgṛhītadhanenāḍhyakatvena vā sthitena kāryam iti | evaṃ kṛtā samayakriyā na kevalaṃ samudāyibhiḥ pālanīyā | kiṃ tu rājñāpīty āha nāradaḥ |
pāṣaṇḍanaigamaśreṇipūgavrātagaṇādiṣu |
saṃrakṣet samayaṃ rājā durge janapade tathā || iti |
pāṣaṇḍā vedabāhyā vedoktaliṅgadāriṇo atiriktā vā sarve liṅginaḥ | teṣām madhye abhicaraṇādyāḥ samayāḥ santi | naigamāḥ sārthikā vaṇigprabhṛtayaḥ | teṣu [352] saṃcukasaṃdeśaharapuruṣatiraskāriṇo daṇḍyā ity evamādayo bahavaḥ samayā vidyante | atha vā naigamā āptapraṇītatvena vedaprāmāṇyam icchanti ye pāśupatādayaḥ, paṇyaśilpopajīvinaḥ kuviṃdādayaḥ tāsv iyam anayaiva śreṇyā vikreyam ityādikāḥ samayāḥ santi yugā hastyaśvārohakādayaḥ | vrātagaṇaśabdayor arthaḥ kātyāyanena darśitaḥ |
nānāyudhadharā vrātāḥ samavetās tu kīrtitāḥ |
kulānāṃ tu samūho yaḥ gaṇaḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ || iti |
pūge vrāte cānyonyam utsṛjya samare na gantavyam ityādayaḥ santi samayāḥ | gaṇe ca pañcame ‘hani pañcame vā abde karṇavedhaḥ kartavya ityādisamayāḥ | durge dhānyadikaṃ gṛhītvā anyatra yāsyatā na tad vikreyam ity āste samayaḥ | janapade tu kvacid vikretur haste kvacit kretṛhaste śulkagrahaṇam ityādiko ‘sty anekavidhaḥ samayaḥ | tat samayajātaṃ yathā na bhraśyati na ca vyativartera tathā rājā kuryād ity arthaḥ | samudāye tu puruṣaviṣaye viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
[353]         kośena lekhyakriyayā madhyasthair vā parasparam |
viśvāsaṃ prathamaṃ kṛtvā kuryuḥ kāryāṇy anantaram || iti |
madhyasthaiḥ pratibhūbhiḥ | kāryāṇi samūhakāryāṇi | kātyāyano ‘pi |
samūhānāṃ tu yo dharmas tena dharmeṇa te sadā |
prakuryuḥ sarvakarmāṇi svadharmeṣu vyavasthitāḥ || iti |
samūhakāryakāriṣu heyopādeyān vibhajati bṛhaspatiḥ |
vidveṣiṇo vyasaninaḥ śālīnālasabhīravaḥ |
vṛddhā lubdhāś ca bālāś ca na kāryāḥ kāryacintakāḥ ||
śucayo vedadharmajñā dakṣā dāntāḥ kulodbhavāḥ |
sarvakāryapravīṇāś ca kartavyās tu mahattamāḥ || iti |
te ca kiyantaḥ kartavyā ity apekṣite sa evāha |
[354]         dvau trayaḥ pañca vā kāryāḥ samūhahitavādinaḥ |
kartavyaṃ vacanaṃ teṣāṃ grāmaśreṇigaṇādibhiḥ || iti |
yaḥ samūhahitavādināṃ vacanātikramaṃ karoti tasya daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
kartavyaṃ vacanaṃ sarvaiḥ samūhahitavādinām |
yas tatra viparītaḥ syāt sa dāpyaḥ prathamaṃ damam || iti | (YDh 2.188)
kātyāyano ‘pi |
yuktiyuktaṃ ca yo hanyād yaḥ kāryānavakāśadaḥ |
ayuktaṃ caiva yo brūyāt sa dāpyaḥ pūrvasāhasam || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
yas tu sādhāraṇaṃ hiṃsyāt kṣipet traividyam eva vā |
saṃvitkriyāṃ vihanyāc ca sa nirvāsyaḥ tataḥ purāt || iti |
samūhinām apy adharmeṇa dveṣādinā kāryakaraṇe daṇḍam āha sa eva |
[355]         bādhāṃ kuryur yadaikasya saṃbhūtā dveṣasaṃyuāḥ |
rājñā sarve gṛhītārthāḥ śāsyāś caivānubandhataḥ || iti |
anubandhato ‘nusārataḥ na yathā samayaṃ jahyuḥ | mukhyānām auddaṇḍyaṃ rājā nivārayed ity āha sa eva |
mukhyaiḥ sahasamūhānāṃ visaṃvādo yadā bhavet |
tadā vicārayed rājā svamārge sthāpayec ca tān || iti |
yas tu mukhyaḥ samūhadravyādikam apaharati tasya daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
gaṇadravyaṃ hared yas tu saṃvidaṃ laṅghayet tu yaḥ |
sarvasvaharaṇaṃ kṛtvā taṃ rāṣṭrād vipravāsayet || iti | (YDh 2.187)
marmodghāṭakādīnāṃ purān nirvāsanam eva daṇḍam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
aruntudaḥ sūcakaś ca bhedakṛt sāhasī tathā |
śreṇipūganṛpadveṣṭā kṣipraṃ nirvāsyate tadā ||
[356]         puraśreṇigaṇādhyakṣāḥ puradurganivāsinaḥ |
vāgdigdamaṃ parityāgaṃ prakuryuḥ pāpakāriṇaḥ ||
taiḥ kṛtaṃ yat svadharmeṇa nigrahānugrahaṃ nṛṇām |
tad rājñāpy anumantavyaṃ nisṛṣṭārthā hi te smṛtāḥ || iti |
nisṛṣṭārthā anujñātakāryā ity arthaḥ | pāṣaṇḍyādisarvasamūheṣu yathā rājñā vartitavyaṃ tad āha nāradaḥ |
yo dharmaḥ karma ya caiṣām upasthānavidhiś ca yaḥ |
yac caiṣāṃ vṛtyupādānam anumanyet tat tathā ||
pratikūlaṃ ca yad rājñaḥ prakṛtyavamataṃ ca yat |
doṣavat karaṇaṃ yat syād anāmnāyaprakalpitam |
pravṛttam api tad rājā śreyaskāmo nivartayet || iti |
dharmo jaṭāvatvādi | karma prātaḥparyuṣitabhikṣāṭanādi | upasthānavidhiḥ samūhakāryārthe paṭahādidhvanim ākarṇya maṇḍapādau melanam | vṛtyupādānaṃ jīvanārthaṃ tāpasaveṣaparigrahaḥ | [357] rājñaḥ pratikūlam ādhikārikaśūdrakartṛkaṃ traivarṇaikavivāde dharmavivecanam | tasya ca pratikūlatvam uktaṃ smṛtyantareṇa |
yasya rājñaḥ tu kurute śūdro dharmavivecanam |
tasya prakṣubhyate rāṣṭraṃ balaṃ koṣaṃ ca naśyati || iti |
prakṛtyavamataṃ svabhāvata eva yad ananujñātaṃ pāṣaṇḍyādiṣu tāmbūlabhakṣaṇādyarthānāṃ bādhakaṃ rasavādādikam | mithaḥ saṅghātakaraṇam upasthānavidheḥ pūrvaṃ pṛthak pṛthag avāntarastomakaraṇam āhitakalaśādikam | parasparopatāpaḥ rājapuruṣāśrayaṇenānyonyam arthāpaharaṇādiḥ | doṣavat karaṇaṃ śrutismṛtiviruddhaṃ vidhavādau veśyātvādikaṃ pāṣāṇḍyādibhiḥ prakalpitam | saṃvillaṅghane daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
yo grāmadeśasaṃghānāṃ kṛtvā satyena saṃvidam |
visaṃvaden naro lobhāt taṃ rāṣṭād vipravāsayet ||
vigṛhya dāpayed enaṃ samayavyabhicāriṇam |
catuḥsuvarṇān ṣaṇṇiṣkān śatamānaṃ ca rājatam ||
[358]         evaṃ daṇḍavidhiṃ kuryād dhārmikaḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
grāmajātisamūheṣu samayavyabhicāriṇām || iti | (MDh 8.219–21)
satyena śapathena | eteṣāṃ nirvāsanacatuḥsuvarṇaṣaṭniṣkaśatamānarūpāṇāṃ jātividyāguṇādyapekṣayā vyavasthā kalpanīyā | samūhapūjārthaṃ rājñā samarpitaṃ dravyaṃ samūhāya yo na dadāti taṃ praty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
samūhakārya āyātān kṛtakāryān visarjayet |
sadānamānasatkāraiḥ pūjayitvā mahīpatiḥ ||
samūhakaryaprahito yal labheta tad arpayet |
ekādaśaguṇaṃ dāpyo yady asmai nārpayet svayam || iti | (YDh 2.189–90)
samūhakāryaprahitenārpitaṃ ca dhanaṃ sarve samūhino vibhajya gṛhṇīyuḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
tato labhyeta yat kiṃcit sarveṣām eva tat samam | iti |
vibhajya grahaṇam aṇudravyaviṣayam | yas tatpakṣaviṣaye sa evāha |
[359]         ṣāṇmāsikaṃ vatsaraṃ vā vibhaktavyaṃ yathāṃśataḥ |
deyaṃ badhiravṛddhāndhastrībālāturarogiṣu ||
sāntānikādiṣu tathā dharma eṣa sanātanaḥ || iti |
rājñaḥ prasādalabdhaṃ vaṃṭaṇam api sarveṣāṃ samam ity āha sa eva |
yat taiḥ prāptaṃ rakṣitaṃ vā gaṇārthe vā ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
rājñaḥ prasādalabdhaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ tat samāhitam || iti |
etad abhakṣitaviṣayam | bhakṣite tu kātyāyana āha |
gaṇam uddiśya yat kiṃcit kṛtvarṇaṃ bhakṣitaṃ bhavet |
ātmārthaṃ viniyuktaṃ vā deyaṃ tair eva tad bhavet || iti |
ye tu samudāyaṃ prasādya tadantargatā ye ca samudāyakṣobhādinā tato bahirbhūtāḥ tān praty āha sa eva |
gaṇināṃ śilpivargāṇāṃ gatāḥ syur ye tu madhyatām |
prāktanasyādhamarṇasya samāṃśāḥ sarva eva te ||
[360]         tathaiva bhojanair bhāvyaṃ dānadharmakriyāsu ca |
samūhastho ‘ṃśabhāgī syāt pragatas tv aṃśabhāg abhāk || iti |
saṃvidvyatikramākhyaṃ vivādapadam
ath krītānuśayaḥ kathyate
tatsvarūpaṃ nāradenoktam |
krītvā mūlyena yaḥ paṇyaṃ kretā na bahu manyate |
krītānuśaya ity etad vivādapadam ucyate || iti |
krītvānuśayānutpattyarthaṃ kretā krayāt prāg eva samyak parīkṣeta | tathā ca sa eva |
kretā paṇyaṃ parīkṣeta prāk svayaṃ guṇadoṣataḥ |
parīkṣābhimataṃ krītaṃ vikretur na bhavet punaḥ || iti |
parīkṣābhimataṃ krītaṃ taddoṣadarśane ‘pi grahītur eva bhavati na vikretuḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
parīkṣitaṃ bahumataṃ gṛhītvā na punas tyajet |
[361] iti | tatkālaparīkṣitasya punar arpaṇābhāvaḥ sāvadhiviṣayaḥ | tatra sadyaḥ parīkṣaṇasya vihitatvāt | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
carmakāṣṭheṣṭakāsūtradhānyāsavarasasya ca |
vastrarūpyahiraṇyānāṃ sadya eva parīkṣaṇam || iti |
krītānāṃ paṇyānāṃ dravyaviśeṣeṇa parīkṣaṇakālāvadhim āha sa eva |
tryahād dohyaṃ parīkṣeta pañcāhād vāhyam eva tu |
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ saptāhāt syāt parīkṣaṇam |
dvipadām ardhamāsaṃ syāt puṃsāṃ taddviguṇaṃ striyāḥ ||
daśāhāt sarvabījānām ekāhāl lohavāsasām |
ato ‘rvāk paṇyadoṣas tu yadi saṃjñāyate kvacit |
vikretuḥ pratideyaṃ tat kretā mūlyam avāpnuyāt || iti |
yathoktaparīkṣākālātikrame tu na pratideyam ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
avijñātaṃ tu yat krītaṃ duṣṭaṃ paścād vibhāvitam |
krītaṃ tat svāmine deyaṃ paṇyaṃ kāle ‘nyathā na tu ||
[362] iti | avijñātaṃ parīkṣayā tattvato ‘parijñātaṃ yasya dravyasya tad yāvat parīkṣākālaḥ uktaḥ tasmin kāle pratideyam | anyathā tatkālātikrame duṣṭatayā parijñātam api krītaṃ tatsvāmine na deyam ity arthaḥ | paṇyānāṃ deśakālavaśād upacayāpacayau prathamato jñātavyāv ity āha nāradaḥ |
kṣayaṃ vṛddhiṃ ca jānīyāt paṇyānām āgamaṃ tathā | iti |
aśvādipaṇyānām asmin kāle asmin deśe ca vṛddhir bhaviṣyatīti jāniyāt tathā āgamanaṃ kulīnatvādijñānārtham utpādakajanmabhūyādikaṃ ca jānīyād ity arthaḥ | evaṃ samyak parīkṣya guṇadoṣadarśanādikālam antareṇa nānuśayaḥ kārya ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
kṣayaṃ vṛddhiṃ ca vaṇijā paṇyānām avijānatā |
krītvā nānuśayaḥ kāryaḥ kurvan ṣaḍbhāgadaṇḍabhāgk || (YDh 2.258)
[363] iti | parīkṣitapaṇyānāṃ krayakālottarakālam | parimāṇato ‘rghakṛtāṃ vṛddhim apaśyatā kretrānuśayo na kāryaḥ | vikretrā ca mahārghanibandhanaṃ paṇyakṣayam apaśyatā nānuśayitavyam | kṣayavṛddhikālaparijñāne punaḥ kretur vikretur anuśayo bhavatīti vyatirekād uktaṃ bhavati | paṇyadoṣatadvṛddhikṣayakāraṇatritayābhāve ‘nuśayakālābhyantare ‘pi yady anuśayaṃ karoti tadā paṇyaṣaḍbhāgaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | anuśayakāraṇasadbhāve ‘py anuśayakālātikrameṇa yo ‘nuśayaṃ karoti so ‘py evaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ |
etac copabhogāvinaśvaravastuviṣayam | upabhogena vinaśvaravastiviṣaye pratyarpaṇe ‘vadhim āha nāradaḥ |
krītvā mūlyena yaḥ paṇyaṃ duṣkrītaṃ manyate krayī |
vikretuḥ pratideyaṃ tat tasminn evāhny avikṣatam || iti |
dvitīyādidivasapratyarpaṇe tu viśeṣas tenaivoktaḥ |
dvitīye ‘hni dadat kretā mūlyāt triṃśāṃśam āvahet |
dviguṇaṃ tu tṛtīye ‘hni parataḥ ketur eva ca || iti |
parato ‘nuśayo na kartavya ity arthaḥ | yat tu punar manunoktam,
[364]         krītvā vikrīya vā kiṃcid yasyehānuśayo bhavet |
so ‘ntardaśāhāt tad dravyaṃ dadyāc caivādadīta vā ||
iti, tad upabhogenāvinaśvaragṛhakṣetrayānaśayanādiviṣayam, tatraiva daśāhāder uktatvāt | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
bhūmer daśāhe vikretur āyas tatkretur eva ca |
dvādaśāhaḥ sapiṇḍānām api cālpam ataḥ param || iti |
avinaśvareṣu sthirārgheṣu anuśayakālātikrameṇānuśayaṃ kurvato manunokto daṇḍo veditavyaḥ |
pareṇa tu daśāhasya na dadyān nāp dāpayet |
ādadāno dadac caiva rājñā daṇḍyaḥ śatāni ṣaṭ || iti |
bījādivyatirktopabhogaḥ | vāsoviṣaye ‘pi nāradaḥ |
paribhuktaṃ ca yad vāsaḥ kliṣṭarūpaṃ malīmasam |
sadoṣam api tat krītaṃ vikretur na bhavet punaḥ || iti |
iti krītānuśayaḥ
[365]
atha vikrīyāsaṃpradānam
tasya svarūpaṃ nāradenoktam |
vikrīya paṇyaṃ mūlyena kretre yan na pradīyate |
vikrīyāsaṃpradānaṃ tad vivādapadam ucyate || iti |
paṇyadvaividhyam uktaṃ tenaiva |
loke ‘smin dvividhaṃ dravyaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ tathā |
krayavikrayadharmeṣu sarvaṃ tat paṇyam ucyate ||
ṣaḍvidhas tasya tu budhaiḥ dānādānavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
gaṇimaṃ tulimaṃ meyaṃ kriyayā rūpataḥ śriyā || iti |
gaṇimaṃ saṃkhyeyaṃ kamukaphalādi | tulimaṃ tulayādhāpyaṃ hemacandanādi | meyaṃ vrīhyādi | kriyayā vāhanadohanādikriyopalakṣitam aśvamahiṣyādi | rūpataḥ paṇyāṅganādi | śriyā padmarāgādi | tad etat ṣaṭprakāram api paṇyaṃ vikrīyāprayacchan sodayaṃ dāpya ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
gṛhītamūlyaṃ yaḥ paṇyaṃ kretur naiva prayacchati |
sodayaṃ tasya dāpyo ‘sau diglābhām vā digāgate || (YDh 2.254)
[366] iti | gṛhītamūlyaṃ paṇyaṃ vikretā yadi prārthayamānāya svadeśavaṇije kretre na samarpayati tac ca paṇyaṃ yadi krayakāle bahumūlyaṃ sat kālāntare svalpamūlyenaiva labhyate tadā sodayaṃ vṛddhyā sahitaṃ vikrektā kretre dāpanīyaḥ | yadā mūlyahrāsakṛtaḥ paṇyasyodayo nāsti kiṃ tu krayakāle yavad eva yato mūlyasya yat paṇyam iti pratipannaṃ tāvad eva tadā tat paṇyam ādāya tasmin deśe vikrīṇānasya  yo lābhas tenodayena sahitaṃ dvikaṃ trikam ityādinā pratipāditavṛddhirūpodayena sahitaṃ dāpanīyaḥ | yathāha nāradaḥ |
arghaś ced avahīyeta sodayaṃ paṇyam āvahet |
sthāyinām eṣa niyamo diglābho digvicāriṇām || iti |
yadā tv arghamahatvena paṇyasya nyūnabhāvas tadā tasmin paṇye vastragṛhādike ya upabhogaḥ tadācchādanamukhanivāsādirūpo vikretuḥ tatsahitaṃ paṇyasamau dāpyaḥ | yathāha nāradaḥ |
[367]         vikrīya paṇyaṃ mūlyena kretre yan na prayacchati |
sthāvarasyodayaṃ dāpyo jaṅgamasya kriyāphalam || iti |
kṣayaśabdena gatabhoga uktaḥ | yady api tasya dānam aśakyaṃ tathāpi tadanuguṇadravyaṃ deyam | jaṅgamānāṃ tu tatkarmanimittaṃ mūlyaṃ dāpyaḥ | yadā tv asau kretā deśāntarāt paṇyagrahaṇārtham āgatas tadā tatpaṇyam ādāya deśāntare vikrītasya yo lābhas tena sahitaṃ paṇyaṃ vikretā kretre dāpanīyaḥ | viṣṇus tu vikretur daṇḍam apy āha: “gṛhītamūlyaṃ yaḥ paṇyaṃ kretur naiva dadyāt tasyāsau sodayaṃ dāpyo rājñā cāpi paṇaśataṃ daṇḍyaḥ” iti | yas tu vikrīyānuśayavaśān na samarpayati yaś ca krītvānuśyavaśān na gṛhṇāti taṃ praty āha kātyāyanaḥ |
krītvā prāptaṃ na gṛhṇīyād yo na dadyād adūṣitam |
sa mūlyād daśamabhāgaṃ tu datvā svaṃ dravyam āpnuyāt ||
aprāpte ‘rthe kṛcchrakāle kṛte naiva pradāpayet |
evaṃ dharmo daśāhāt tu parato ‘nuśayo na tu || iti |
adūṣitaṃ jalādineti śaṣaḥ | dohyavāhyādipaṇyasya dohanādineti śeṣaḥ | dohyavāhyādipaṇasya dohanādikālo [368] ‘rthakriyākālaḥ | tasmin prāpte sati agrahaṇe adāne vā kṛtī daśamabhāgaṃ pradāpayet | kiṃ tu tam adatvaiva svaṃ dravyam avāpnuyāt | eṣa dharmo daśāhāt prāg veditavyaḥ | tataḥ param anuśayo na kartavyaḥ | vikrīyāsaṃprayacchato ‘pi vikrītaṃ paṇyaṃ vikretṛpārśve sthitaṃ tasya yadi daivādinā nāśaḥ syāt tadā vikretur eva hānir ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
daivarājopaghātena paṇyae doṣam upāgate |
hānir vikretur evāsau yācitasyāprayacchataḥ || iti | (YDh 2.256)
yācitasyeti viśeṣaṇena ayācite na vikretur hānir ity arthād avagamyate | nārado ‘pi |
upahanyeta vā paṇyaṃ dahyetāpahriyeta vā |
vikretur eva so ‘nartho vikrīyāsaṃprayacchataḥ ||
[369] iti | yathā yācitasyāprayacchato vikretur hāniḥ tathā dīyamānapaṇyam agṛhṇataḥ kretur apīty āha sa eva |
dīyamānaṃ na gṛhṇāti krītaṃ paṇyaṃ ca yaḥ krayī |
sa evāsya bhaved doṣo vikretur yo ‘prayacchataḥ ||
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
vikrītam api vikreyaṃ pūrvaṃ kretary agṛhṇati |
hāniś cet kretṛdoṣeṇa kretur eva hi sā bhavet || iti | (YDh 2.255)
yas tu nirdoṣaṃ paṇyaṃ darśayitvā sadoṣaṃ vikrīṇīte yaś cānyahaste vikrīya tad anyasmai tat prayacchati tayoḥ samāno daṇḍa ity āha nāradaḥ |
nirdoṣaṃ darśayitvā tu sadoṣaṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
mūlyaṃ taddviguṇaṃ dāpyo vinayaṃ tāvad eva ca ||
anyahaste ca vikrīya tathānyasmai prayacchati |
so ‘pi taddviguṇaṃ dāpyo vinayaṃ tāvad eva tu || iti |
etad buddhipūrvakaviṣayam.
[370]        jñātvā sadoṣaṃ paṇyaṃ yo vikrīṇīte ‘vicakṣaṇaḥ |
tad eva dviguṇaṃ dāpyas tatsamaṃ vinayaṃ tathā ||
iti bṛhaspatiṇoktatvāt | abuddhipūrvake tu kretuḥ aparāvartanam eva | ata eva evaṃvidhakrayaparāvartaṇaṃ sa evāha |
mattonmattena vikrītaṃ hīnamūlyaṃ bhayena vā |
asvatantreṇa mūḍhena tyājyaṃ tasya punar bhavet || iti |
ata evaṃvidhaniyamo dattamūlye kraye draṣṭavyaḥ | adattamūlye punaḥ paṇye vāṅmātrakraye kretṛvikretroḥ samayā ṛte pravṛttau vā na kaścid doṣaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
dattamūlyasya paṇyasya vidhir eṣa prakīrtitaḥ |
adatte ‘nyatre samayān na vikretur atikramaḥ || iti |
yatra punar vāṅmātreṇa krayo mā bhūd iti vikretṛhaste kretrā yat kiṃcid dravyaṃ dattaṃ tatra kretur doṣavaśena krayāsiddhau āha vyāsaḥ |
satyaṃkāraṃ ca yo datvā yathākālaṃ na dṛśyate |
paṇyam eva nisṛṣṭaṃ tad dīyamānam agṛhṇataḥ ||
[371] iti | atra paṇyadravyasyotsargaḥ satyaṃkāradravyasyotsargo ‘bhimataḥ | asminn eva viṣaye vikretṛdoṣavaśena krayāsiddhau āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
satyaṃkārakṛtaṃ dravyaṃ dviguṇaṃ pratidāpayet | iti |
krītvānuśayānutpattyarthaṃ katipayapaṇyānāṃ vikrayānarhatvam āha manuḥ |
nānyad anyena saṃsṛṣṭarūpaṃ vikrayam arhati |
na cāsāraṃ na ca nyūnaṃ na dūre na tirohitam || iti | (MDh 8.203)
iti krayavikrayānuśayākhyaṃ vivādapadam
[372]
atha svāmipālavivādavidhiḥ
tatra tu tadabhidhānapratijñā manunā kṛtā |
paśuṣu svāmināṃ caiva pālānāṃ ca vyatikrame |
vivādaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yathāvad dharmatattvataḥ || iti | (MDh 8.229)
vivādaṃ vivādāpanodam ity arthaḥ | svāmipālayoḥ kartavyam āha nāradaḥ |
upānayed gāḥ gopālaḥ pratyahaṃ rajanīkṣaye |
cīrṇā pītāś ca tā gāvāḥ sāyāhne pratyupānayet || iti |
gograhaṇaṃ paśumātropalakṣaṇārtham | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
yathārpitān paśūn gopaḥ sāyaṃ pratyarpayet tathā | iti | (YDh 2.164)
yāvantaḥ prātaḥ samarptiāḥ tāvantaḥ sāyaṃ pratyarpaṇīyā ity arthaḥ | gavādiparipālakasya bhṛtiparimāṇam āha nāradaḥ |
[373]         gavāṃ śatād vatsatarī dhenuḥ syād dviśatād bhṛtiḥ |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ gope saṃdoho vāṣṭame ‘hani || iti |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ vatsatarī dvihāyanī gauḥ bhṛtiḥ bhṛtake kalpanīyā | dviśate tu savatsā gauḥ | aṣṭame divase dohaś ca bhṛtitvena kalpanīya ity arthaḥ | saṃdohaḥ sarvadohaḥ,
tayā dhenubhṛtaḥ kṣīraṃ labhetaivāṣṭame ‘khilam |
iti bṛhaspatismaraṇāt | iyaṃ ca bhṛtikalpanā paribhāṣitabhṛtiviśeṣābhāviviṣaye | paribhāṣite tu bhṛtiviśeṣe sa eva deyaḥ | manus tu prakārāntareṇa bhṛtim āha |
gopaḥ kṣīrabhṛto yas tu sa duhyād daśato varām |
gosvāmyanumate bhṛtyaḥ sā syāt pāle ‘bhṛte bhṛtiḥ || iti | (MDh 8.231)
daśato daśadogdhrīṇāṃ madhe varām utkṛṣṭaāṃ gāṃ svīkṛtya tatkṣīraṃ kṣīrabhṛto gṛhṇīyāt | kṣīraśūnyānāṃ tu kṣīramūlyato bhṛtiḥ kalpanīyā | yady asau dravyāntareṇa bhṛtaḥ na tatraiṣā bhṛtir ity arthaḥ | yas tv evaṃ parikalpitaṃ vetanaṃ gṛhītvā paśūn [374] pālayan bhṛtyaḥ svadoṣeṇa paśūn māreyed vināśayati vā taṃ praty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pramādamṛtanaṣṭāṃś ca pradāpyaḥ kṛtavetanaḥ | iti | (YDh 2.164)
pramādagrahaṇaṃ pālakadoṣopalakṣaṇārtham | pramādaś ca manunā spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ |
naṣṭaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ kṛmibhiḥ śvahastaṃ viṣame mṛtam |
hīnaṃ puruṣakāreṇa pradadyāt pāla eva tu || iti | (MDh 8.232)
prasahya corair apahṛto na dāpyaḥ | tathā ca sa eva |
vighuṣya tu hṛtaṃ caurair na pālo dātum arhati |
yadi deśe ca kāle ca svāminaḥ svasya śaṃsati || iti | (MDh 8.233)
vyāso ‘pi |
pālagrahe grāmaghāte tathā rāṣṭrasya viplave |
yat paṇaṣṭaṃ hṛtaṃ vā syān na pāleṣv atra kilbiṣam || iti |
etat puruṣakārakaraṇe veditavyam | puruṣakārākaraṇe tu bhavaty eva kilbiṣī | puruṣakārasya svarūpaṃ nāradena darśitam |
[375]        kṛmicoravyāghrabhayād darīśvabhrāc ca pālayet |
vyāyacchec chaktitaḥ krośet svāmine tu nivedayet || | iti |
vyāyacchet nivāraṇārthaṃ prayatetety arthaḥ | yaḥ prastutārthaṃ na yatate taṃ praty āha sa eva |
avyāyacchann avikrośan svāmine vānivedayan |
voḍhum arhati gopas tāṃ vinayaṃ caiva rājani || iti |
vinayapramāṇam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pāladoṣavināśe tu pāle daṇḍo vidhīyate |
ardhatrayodaśapaṇaḥ svāmino dravyam eva ca || iti | (YDh 2.165)
ardhatrayodaśapaṇaḥ sārdhadvādaśakārṣāpaṇaḥ | pāladoṣam āha manuḥ |
[376]         ajāvike tu saṃruddhe vṛkaiḥ pāle tv anāyati |
yāṃ prasahya vṛko hanyāt pāle tat kilbiṣaṃ bhavet || iti | (MDh 8.235)
anāyati, upadravanirākaraṇāya anāgacchatīty arthaḥ | yām ajāvikajātīyām | etat sugamasthalasthaviṣayam | durgamasthalaviṣaye tu na doṣa ity āha sa eva |
tāsāṃ ced avaruddhānāṃ carantīnāṃ mitho vane |
yām utplutya vṛko hanyān na pālas tatra kilbiṣī || iti | (MDh 8.236)
avaruddhānāṃ pālakena ekatra sthāpitānām ity arthaḥ | daivamṛtānāṃ punaḥ karṇādikaṃ darśanīyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
karṇau carma ca vālāṃś ca bastyasthisnāyurocanam |
paśuṣu svāmināṃ dadyān mṛteṣv aṅgāni darśayet || (MDh 8.234)
[377] iti | smṛtyantaram api |
karṇau carma ca vālāṃś ca śṛṅgasnāyvasthirocanam |
paśusvāmiṣu dadyāt tu mṛteṣv aṅgāni darśayet || iti |
gopracārabhūmim āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
grāmecchayā gopracāro bhūmī rājecchayāpi vā | iti | (YDh 2.116)
grāmecchayā grāmālpatvamahatvāpekṣayā rājecchayā vā gavāṃ tṛṇādibhakṣaṇārthaṃ kuyān api bhūbhāgo ‘kṛṣṭaḥ kalpanīyaḥ | gavāṃ pracārasthānāsanasaukaryārthaṃ grāmakṣetrayor antaram āha sa eva |
dhanuḥśataṃ parīṇāho grāme ketrāntaraṃ bhavet |
dve śate kharvaṭasya syān nagarasya catuḥśatam || iti | (YDh 2.167)
grāmakṣetrayor antaraṃ dhanuḥśataparimitam | sarvadaivavidhinā śasyaṃ kāryam | kharvaṭasya pracurakaṇṭakasantānasya grāmasya dve śate antare śasyam | nagarasya ca bahujanasaṃkīrṇasya catuḥśataparimite antare śasyaṃ kāryam iti | [378] tatra paśunivāraṇāya vṛtir api kalpanīyety āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ājāteṣv eva śasyeṣu kuryād āvaraṇaṃ sadā |
duḥkhena vinivāryante labdhasvādurasā mṛgāḥ || iti |
nārado ‘pi |
pathi kṣetre vṛtiḥ kāryā yām uṣṭro nāvalokayet |
na laṅghayet paśur vāśvo na bhindyāt yāṃ ca sūkaraḥ || iti |
evaṃ ca paśunivāraṇe kṛte ‘pi tām atikramya śasyādi vināśe sati manur āha |
pathi kṣetre parivṛte grāmāntīye ‘tha vā punaḥ |
sapālaḥ śatadaṇḍārho vipālān vārayet paśūn | iti |
pathi kṣetre parivṛte sati tāṃ vṛtim atikramya śatyaghāte sapālaḥ paśukārye paṇaśatadaṇḍārthaḥ | evaṃ grāmāntīye grāmasamīpavartini kṣetre parivṛte sati tāṃ vṛtim atikramya śasyaghāte sa pālaḥ [379] śatapaṇadaṇḍārhaḥ | tatpaśupālakaḥ śatadaṇḍena daṇḍanīya ity arthaḥ | vipālaṃ pālakarathitaṃ paśuṃ kṣetriko laguḍādinā nivārayet | nārado ‘pi |
utkramya tu vṛtiṃ yatra sasyaghāto gavādibhiḥ |
pālaḥ śāsyo bhavet tatra na cec chaktyā nivārayet || iti |
anāvṛtakṣetraghātaviṣaye sa evāha |
grāmopānte ca yat kṣetraṃ vivītānte mahāpathe |
anāvṛtaṃ cet tannāśe na pālasya vyatikramaḥ || iti |
vivītaśabdena tṛṇakāṣṭhasmṛddhaḥ paraparigṛhītaḥ pradeśa ucyate | vyatikramaniṣedhenārthād daṇḍābhāvaḥ sūcitaḥ | tad anena aparivṛte pālasya daṇḍābhāvaḥ sūcitaḥ | manus tu sākṣād daṇḍaṃ viṣedhati |
[380]        tatrāprivṛtaṃ dhānyaṃ vihiṃsyuḥ paśavo yadi |
na tatra praṇayed daṇḍaṃ nṛpatiḥ paśurakṣiṇām || iti | (MDh 8.238)
etad adīrghakālapracāraviṣayam | dīrghakālapracāre tu daṇḍam arhati | ata evālpakālapracāre doṣābhāvam āha viṣṇuḥ: “pathi grāmaprānte ca na doṣo ‘lpakālam” iti (ViDh 3.15) | daṇḍaparimāṇaṃ tu paśuviśeṣeṇa darśitaṃ yājñavalkyena |
māṣān aṣṭau tu mahiṣī śasyaghātasya kāriṇī |
daṇḍanīyā tadarthaṃ tu gaus tadardham ajāvikam ||
bhakṣayitvopaviṣṭānāṃ yathoktān dviguṇo damaḥ |
samam eṣāṃ vivīte ‘pi kharoṣṭraṃ mahiṣīsama || iti | (YDh 2.159–60)
paraśasyaghātakārimahiṣīsvāmī pratimahiṣy aṣṭau māṣān daṇḍanīyaḥ | caturo māṣān gosvāmī | meṣasvāmī dvau dvau māṣau | eṣām eva paśūnāṃ śasyabhakṣaṇād ārabhya yāvac chayanam anivāritānāṃ svāmī yathoktadaṇḍān dviguṇaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ |
[381] vivītasya tṛṇādyupaghāte paśusvāmī śasyakṣetropaghātavad daṇḍanīyaḥ | kharoṣṭrasvāmī mahiṣīsvāmivad daṇḍanīyaḥ | etad ajñānaviṣayam |
paṇasya pādau dvau gāṃ tu dviguṇaṃ mahiṣīṃ tathā |
tathājāvikavatsānāṃ pādo daṇḍaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
iti smṛtyantaroktaṃ veditavyam | māṣāś cātra tāmrikapaṇasya viṃśatitamo bhāgaḥ,
māṣo viṃśatitamo bhāgaḥ paṇasya parikīrtitaḥ \
iti nāradasmaraṇāt | bhakṣayitvopaviṣṭasavatsaviṣaye yathoktāc caturguṇo daṇḍaḥ | tad uktaṃ smṛtyantare |
vatsānāṃ dviguṇaḥ proktaḥ savatsānāṃ caturguṇaḥ | iti |
yat punar nāradenoktam,
māṣaṃ gāṃ dāpayed daṇḍaṃ dvau māṣau mahiṣaṃ tathā |
tathājāvikavatsānāṃ daṇḍaḥ syād ardhamāṣikaḥ ||
[382] iti tanmuhūrtamātrabhakṣaṇaviṣayam | ata evāhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: “rātrau carantī gauḥ pañca māṣān | divā trīn | muhūrte māṣam | daṇḍaṃ grāse” iti | āturapaśuviṣaye tu na daṇḍa ity āha nāradaḥ |
rājagrahagṛhīto vā vajrāśanihato ‘pi vā |
api sarpeṇa vā daṣṭo vṛkṣād vā patito bhavet ||
vyāghrādibhis hato vāpi vyādhibhir vāpy upadrutaḥ |
na tatra doṣaḥ pālasya na ca doṣo ‘sti gominām || iti |
anātureṣv api keṣucit paśuṣu daṇḍābhāvam āha sa eva |
gauḥ prasūtā daśāhaṃ ca mahokṣo vājikuñjarau |
nivaryāḥ syuḥ prayatnena teṣāṃ svāmī na daṇḍabhāk ||
[383] iti | manur api |
anirdaśāhāṃ gāṃ sutāṃ vṛṣān devapaśūṃs tathā |
sapālān vā vipālān vā na daṇḍyān manur abravīt || iti | (MDh 8.242)
vṛṣā mahokṣāḥ | atha vā vṛṣotsargavidhānenotsṛṣṭāḥ | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
mahokṣotsṛṣṭapaśavaḥ sūtikāgantukādayaḥ |
pālo yeṣāṃ na te mocyā daivarājapariplutāḥ || iti | (YDh 2.166)
āgantukaḥ svayūthāt paribhraṣṭaḥ | ādiśabdena mṛtavatsādayo gṛhyante | ata eva uśanāḥ |
[384]         adaṇḍyā mṛtavatsā ca khaṃjā rogavatī kṛśā |
adaṇḍyāgantukī gauś ca sūtikā cābhisāriṇī ||
adaṇḍyāś cotsave gāvaḥ śrāddhakāle tathaiva ca || iti |
paraśasyavināśe na kevalaṃ svāmī daṇḍanīyaḥ api tu śasyam api dāpanīyaḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
śasyān nivārayed gās tu cīrṇe doṣadvayaṃ bhavet |
svāmī śatadamaṃ dāpyaḥ pālas tāḍanam arhati ||
śadaś ca sadamaṃ cīrṇe samūle kārṣabhakṣite || iti |
ata eva nāradaḥ |
samūlaśasyanāśe tu tatsvāmī prāpnuyāc chatam |
vadhena gopo mucyeta daṇḍaṃ svāmini pātayet || iti |
tatsvāmī śasyasvāmī śadaś ca sāmantādibhiḥ parikalpito deyaḥ | tathā ca sa eva |
[385]         gobhis tu bhakṣitaṃ śasyaṃ yo naraḥ pratiyācate |
sāmantānumate deyaṃ dhānyaṃ yat tatra bhakṣitam || iti |
yas tu uśanasā śadayācananiṣedho ‘rthāt kṛtaḥ |
gobhir vināśitaṃ dhānyaṃ yo naraḥ pratiyācate |
pitaras tasya nāśnanti nāśnanti tridivaukasaḥ || iti |
sa grāmādisamīpasthānāvṛtakṣetraviṣayaḥ |
iti svāmipālākhyaṃ vivādapadam
atha sīmāvivādanirṇayaḥ
tatra tāvat sīmā caturvidhā | janapadasīmā grāmasīmā gṛhasīmā kṣetrasīmā ca iti | sā ca yathākramaṃ pañcalakṣaṇā | tad uktaṃ nāradena |
dhvajinī matsyanī caiva naidhānī bhayavarjitā |
rājaśāsananītā ca sīmā pañcavidhā smṛtā || iti |
dvajinī vṛkṣādilakṣitā | matsyinī jalaliṅgānvitā | naidhānī nikhātatuṣāṅgārādimatī | bhavarjitā [386] sarthipratyarthiparasparaviṣayāpattinirmitā | rājaśāsananītā jñātṛcihnādyabhāve rājecchayā nirmitā | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
grāmayor ubhayoḥ sīmni vṛkṣā yatra samunnatāḥ |
samucchirtā dhvajākārā dhvajinī sā prakīrtitā ||
svacchandagā bahujalā matsyakūrmasamanvitā |
pratyakpravāhinī yatra sā sīmā matsyanī matā ||
tuṣāṅgārakapālais kumbhair āyatanais tathā |
sīmātra cihnitā kāryā naidhānī sā nigadyate || iti |
vṛkṣāś ca nyagrodhādayaḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
[387]         sīmāvṛkṣāṃs tu kurvīta nyagrodhāśvatthakiṃśukān |
śālmalīsālatālāṃś ca kṣīriṇaś caiva pādapān || iti | (MDh 8.246)
pratyakpravāhinīty anena vāpyādīni prakāśacihnāny upalakṣyante | tāni ca bṛhaspatinā darśitāni |
vāpīkūpataḍāgāni caityārāmasurālayāḥ |
sthalanimnanadīsrotaḥśaragulmanagādayaḥ ||
prakāśacihnāny etāni sīmāyāṃ kārayet sadā || iti |
tuṣāṅgārakapālair iti karīṣādīnāṃ guptaliṅgānām apy upalakṣaṇam | tāni ca tenaiva darśitāni |
karīṣāsthituṣāṅgāraśarkarāśmakapālikāḥ |
sikateṣṭakagovālakārpāsāsthīni bhasma ca ||
prakṣipya kumbheṣv etāni sīmānteṣu nidhāpayet || iti |
tāni ca sīmāliṅgāni sthavirair bālānāṃ darśanīyāni | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
[388]         tataḥ paugaṇḍabālānāṃ prayatnena pradarśayet |
vārdhike ca śiśūnāṃ te darśayeyus tathaiva ca ||
evaṃ paramparājñāte sīmābhrāntir na jāyate || iti |
