evaṃ nirūpitair liṅgair sīmāvivādanirṇayaṃ kuryād ity āha manuḥ |
etair liṅgair nayet sīmāṃ rājā vivadamānayoḥ |
pūrvabhuktyā ca satatam udakasyāgamena ca ||
yadi saṃśaya eva syāl liṅgānām api darśane |
sākṣipratyaya eva syāt sīmāvādavinirṇayaḥ ||
sākṣyabhāve tu catvāro grāmāḥ sāmantavāinaḥ |
sīmāvinirṇayaṃ kuryuḥ prayatā rājasaṃnidhau || iti | (MDh 8.252–53, 258)
prathamaṃ tāvad arthipratyarthipradarśitaiḥ liṅgaiḥ sīmāvivādanirṇayaḥ | athātrāpy aviśvāsas tadā liṅgaviṣayakāt sīmāviṣayakād vā sākṣipratyayān nirṇayaḥ | yadā sākṣinām abhāvas tadā sāmantair vinirṇaya ity arthaḥ, [389] “teṣām abhāve sāmantāḥ” iti kātyāyanenoktatvāt | ke punaḥ sāmantāḥ ity apekṣite sa evāha |
saṃsaktakās tu sāmantās tatsaṃsaktās tathottarāḥ |
saṃsaktasaktasaṃsaktāḥ padmākārāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || iti |
vipratipannasīmakasya kṣetrasya catasṛṣu dikṣu saṃnihitagrāmādibhoktāraḥ saṃsaktāḥ | eta eva sāmantaśabdābhidheyāḥ | yadā punar aduṣṭasaṃsaktakā na santi tadā saṃsaktasaṃsaktatatsaṃsaktaiḥ nirṇayaḥ kāryaḥ | tad āha sa eva |
svārthasiddhau ca duṣṭeṣu sāmānteṣv arthagauravāt |
tatsaṃsaktais tu kartavya uddhāro nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
saṃsaktasaktadoṣe tu tatsaṃsaktāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
kartavyās tv aviduṣṭās tu rājñā dharmaṃ vijānatā ||
[390]         teṣām abhāve sāmantā maulavṛddhoddhṛtādayaḥ |
sthāvare ṣaṭprakāre ‘pi kāryā nātra vicāraṇā || iti |
vṛddhādilakṣaṇaṃ tenaivoktam |
nibadhyamānaṃ yair dṛṣṭaṃ tat kāryaṃ suguṇānvitaiḥ |
vṛddhā vā yadi vāvṛddhās te ca vṛddhāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
ye tatra pūrvaṃ sāmantāḥ paścād diśāntaraṃ gatāḥ |
tanmūlatvāt tu te maulā ṛṣibhiḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
upaśravaṇasaṃbhogabhayasthānopacihnitāḥ |
uddharanti punar yasmād uddhṛtās te tataḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
sākṣiprabhṛtyuddhṛtaparyantānām abhāve manur āha |
sāmantānām abhāve tu maulānāṃ sīmasākṣiṇām |
imān apy anuyuñjīta puruṣān vanagocarān ||
[391]         vyādhān śākunikān gopān kaivartān mūlakhānakān |
vyālagrāhān uñchavṛttīn anyāṃś ca vanagocarān || iti | (MDh 8.259–60)
anyāṃś ceti cakāreṇa sīmākarṣakā upalakṣyante | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
sīmnop vivāde kṣetrasya sāmantāḥ sthavirādayaḥ |
gopāḥ sīmākṛṣāṇā ye sarve ca vacagocarāḥ ||
nayeyur ete sīmānaṃ sthalāṅgāratuṣadrumaiḥ |
setuvalmīkanimnāsthicaityādyair upalakṣitām || iti | (YDh 2.150–51)
nārado ‘pi |
grāmasīmāsu ca bahir ye ca syuḥ kṛṣijīvinaḥ |
gopāḥ śākunikā vyādhā ye cānye vanagocarāḥ || iti |
te ca śapathaiḥ śāpitā eva nirṇayaṃ brūyuḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
[392]         śāpitāḥ śapathaiḥ svaiḥ svair brūyuḥ sīmāvinirṇayam |
darśayeyuś ca liṅgāni tat pramāṇam iti sthitiḥ || iti |
svaiḥ svair iti,
satyena śāpayed vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ |
ityādilokavyavasthayā pratipāditaiḥ ity arthaḥ | manur api |
grāmīyakakulānāṃ ca samakṣaṃ sīmni sākṣiṇaḥ |
praṣṭavyāḥ sīmaliṅgāni tayoś caiva vivādinoḥ ||
te pṛṣṭās tu yathā brūyuḥ samastāḥ sīmni niścayam |
nibadhnīyāt tathā sīmāṃ sarvāṃs tāṃś caiva nāmataḥ || iti | (MDh 8.254–55)
sīmāsākṣiṇāṃ tu lakṣaṇam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
āgamaṃ ca pramāṇaṃ ca bhogaṃ kāmaṃ ca nāma ca |
bhūbhāgalakṣaṇaṃ caiva ye vidus te ‘tra sākṣiṇaḥ || iti |
yadā punaś cihnāni na santi vidyamānāni vā liṅgāliṅgatayā saṃdigdhāni tadā nirṇayopāyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[393]         sāmantā vā samagrāmāś catvāro ‘ṣṭau daśāpi vā |
raktasragvasanāḥ sīmāṃ nayayuḥ kṣitidhāriṇaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.152)
sāmantā veti vikalpābhidhānaṃ smṛtyantaroktasākṣyādyabhiprāyam | samagrāmāḥ pratyāsannagrāmīṇāḥ catvāro ‘ṣṭau daśa vety eva samasaṃkhyākā raktasragviṇo raktāmbaradharā mūrdhny āropitakṣitikhaṇḍāḥ sīmāṃ pradarśayeyuḥ | svaiḥ svaiḥ śapathaiḥ śāpitāḥ santaḥ sīmāṃ nayeyuḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
śirobhis te gṛhītvorvīṃ sragviṇo raktavāsasaḥ |
sukṛtaiḥ śāpitāḥ svaiḥ svair nayeyus te samañjasam || iti | (MDh 8.256)
nayeyur iti bahuvacanam api avivakṣitam, ekasyāpi sīmāpradarśakasya bṛhaspatinā darśitatvāt |
jñātṛcihnair vinā sādhur eko ‘py ubhayasaṃmataḥ |
raktamālyāmbaradharo mṛdam ādāya mūrdhani ||
satyavrataḥ sopavāsaḥ sīmānaṃ darśayen naraḥ || iti |
yat tu nāredenoktam,
[394]        naikaḥ samunnayet sīmāṃ naraḥ pratyayavān api |
gurutvād asya kāryasya kriyaiṣā bahuṣu sthitā || iti |
tad ubhayānumatadharmavidvyatiriktaviṣayam | sthalādicihnābhāve ‘pi sākṣisāmantādīnāṃ sīmājñāna upāyaviśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
nimnagāpahṛtotsṛṣṭanaṣṭacihnāu bhūmiṣu |
tatpradeśānumānāc ca pramāṇād bhogadarśanāt || iti |
nimnagayā nadyā apahāreṇotsṛṣṭāni naṣṭāni svasthānāc cyutāni yāsu maryādābhūmiṣu tatra tatra deśānumānāt dṛṣṭasvacihnānāṃ prācīnapradeśānubhāvāt  prayogāt grāmād ārabhya sahasradaṇḍaparimitaṃ kṣetram asya grāmasya paścimo bhāga ity evaṃvidhāt parimāṇāt, bhogadarśanāt pratyarthisamakṣam avipratipannāyā asmārtakālopalakṣitabhukter vā niścinuyur ity arthaḥ | eteṣāṃ sākṣisāmantaprabhṛtīnāṃ sīmāsaṅkramaṇadinād ārabhya yāvat tripakṣaṃ yadi [395] rājadaivikavyasanaṃ notpadyate tadā tatpardarśanāt sīmānirṇayaḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
sīmācaṅkramaṇe kośe pādasparśe tathaiva ca |
tripakṣapañcasaptāhaṃ daivarājakam iṣyate || iti |
yas tatra niṣedhaḥ smṛtyantare ‘bhihitaḥ,
vākpāruṣye mahīvāde divyāni parivarjayet |
iti, sa uktalakṣaṇapuruṣābhāvaviṣaya ity avirodhaḥ | kathaṃ tarhy atra nirṇaya ity apekṣite āha nāradaḥ |
yadā tu na syur jñātāraḥ sīmāyāś ca na lakṣaṇam |
tadā rājā dvayoḥ sīmām unnayed iṣṭataḥ svayam || iti |
iṣṭataḥ icchātaḥ | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
abhāve jñātṛcihnānāṃ rājā sīmnaḥ pravartitā | iti | (YDh 2.153)
jñātṝṇāṃ sāmantādīnāṃ cihnānāṃ vṛkṣādīnām abhāve rājaiva sīmnaḥ pravartayitā | grāmadvayamadhyavartinīṃ vivādāspadībhūtāṃ bhuvaṃ samaṃ pravibhajya ubhayor grāmayoḥ samarpya tanmadhye sīmāliṅgāni kārayet | tadā tasyā bhūmer yatraivopakārātiśayo dṛśyate tadā tasyaiva grāmasya sakalā bhūḥ samarpaṇīyā | tathāha manuḥ |
[396]         sīmāyām aviṣahyāyāṃ svayaṃ rājaiva dharmavit |
pradiśed bhūmim ekeṣām upakārād iti sthitiḥ || iti | (MDh 8.265)
aviṣahyāyāṃ jñātṛjñāpakaśūnyāyām ity arthaḥ | ṛṇādinirṇayavat sīmānirṇayo nāvedanānantaram eva kāryaḥ | kiṃ tu prakāśiteṣu setvādiṣu | tad āha sa eva |
sīmāṃ prati samutpanne vivāde grāmayor dvayoḥ |
jyeṣṭhe māse nayet sīmāṃ saprakāśeṣu setuṣu || iti | (MDh 8.245)
grāmagrahaṇaṃ nagarāder apy upalakṣaṇārtham | ata eva kātyāyanaḥ |
[397]         sīmāntavāsisāmantaiḥ kuryāt kṣetrādinirṇayam |
grāmasīmādiṣu tathā tadvan nagaradeśayoḥ || iti |
yadā rāgalobhādivaśāt sīmāsākṣiṇo nirṇayaṃ na kuryuḥ tadā daṇḍanīyā ity āha sa eva |
bahūnāṃ tu gṛhītānāṃ na sīmānirṇayaṃ yadi |
kuryur bhayād vā lobhād vā dāpyās tūttamasāhasam || iti |
etaj jñānaviṣayam | ajñānaviṣaye tu nāradaḥ |
atha ced anṛtaṃ brūyuḥ sāmantāḥ sīmanirṇaye |
sarve pṛthak pṛthak daṇḍyā rājñā madhyamasāhasam ||
sāmantāt parato ye syus tatsaṃsaktā mṛṣoditaiḥ |
saṃsaktasaktasaktās tu vineyāḥ pūrvasāhasam ||
maulavṛddhādayas tv anye daṇḍaṃ dāpyāḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
vineyāḥ prathamenaiva sāhasena vyavasthitāḥ || iti |
sākṣiṇāṃ mitho vaimatyābhidhāne daṇḍam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
kīrtite yadi bhedaḥ syād daṇḍyās tūttamasāhasam | iti |
sīmācaṅkramaṇakartṝṇām api daṇḍam āha sa eva |
[398]         yathoktena nayantas te pūyante satyasākṣiṇaḥ |
viparītaṃ nayantas tu dāpyās tu dviśataṃ damam || iti |
ājñānād anṛtavacane sākṣyādīn daṇḍayitvā punar vicāraḥ pravartayitavyaḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
ajñānoktān daṇḍayitvā punaḥ sīmāṃ vicārayet |
tyaktvā duṣṭāṃs tu sāmantān anyān maulādibhiḥ saha |
saṃmīlya kārayet sīmām evaṃ dharmavido vidhuḥ || iti |
nadyutsṛṣṭakṣetraviṣaye nirṇayam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
anyagrāmāt samāhṛtya dattāny asya yadā mahī |
mahānadyātha vā rājñā kathaṃ tatra vicāraṇā ||
nadyotsṛṣṭā rājadattā yasya tasyaiva sā mahī |
anyathā na bhavel lābho narāṇāṃ rājadaivakaḥ ||
kṣayodayau jīvanaṃ ca daivarājavaśān nṛṇām |
tasmāt sarveṣu kāryeṣu tatkṛtaṃ na vicālayet ||
grāmayor ubhayor yatra maryādā kalpitā nadī |
kurute dānaharaṇaṃ bhāgyābhāgyavaśān nṛṇām ||
[399]         ekatra kūlapātaṃ tu bhūmer anyatra saṃsthitim |
nadītīre prakurute tasya tāṃ na vicālayet || iti |
etad avyuptaśasyatīraviṣayam | uptaśasyaviṣaye tu sa evāha |
kṣetraśasyaṃ samullaṅghya bhūmicchinnā yadā bhavet |
nadīsrotaḥpravāheṇa pūrvasvāmī labhec ca tām || iti |
tāṃ saśasyāṃ bhūmiṃ pūrvasvāmī yāvad uptaśasyaphalaprāptis tāval labhetety arthaḥ | phalaprāpter ūrdhvaṃ tu pūrvavacanaviṣayasamānatā | rājadattaviṣaye kvacid apavādam āha sa eva |
yā rājñā krodhalobhena chalānyāyena vā hṛtā |
pradattānyasya tuṣṭena na sā siddhim avāpnuyāt || iti |
etac ca svatvahetupramāṇavat kṣetraviṣayam | pramāṇābhāve tu sa evāha |
pramāṇarahitāṃ bhūmiṃ bhuñjāno yasya yā hṛtā |
guṇādhikāya vā dattā tasya tāṃ na vicālayet || iti |
gṛhādiviṣaye nirṇayas tenaiva darśitaḥ |
niveśakālād ārabhya gṛhavāryāpaṇādikam |
yena yāvad yathā bhuktaṃ tasya tan na vicālayet ||
[400]         vātāyanaṃ praṇālīś ca tathā niryūhavedikāḥ |
catuḥśālaṃ syandinikāṃ prāṅniviṣṭāṃ na cālayet || iti |
vātāyanaṃ gavākṣaḥ | praṇālī kāṣṭādimayo jalanirgamopāyaḥ | niryūho gṛhakoṇo nāgadantako vā | vedikā rathyādipradeśasaṃskṛtā unnatā bhūmiḥ | catasṛṇāṃ śālānāṃ samāhāraś catuḥśālam | tasmāt syandanikā vṛṣṭijalanipātaḥ |
niveśakālād ūrdhvaṃ prātiveśyāniṣṭakāri vātāyanādikaṃ na kāryam ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
niveśasamayād ūrdhvaṃ naite yojyāḥ kadācana |
dṛṣṭipātaṃ praṇālīṃ ca na kuryāt paraveśmasu || iti |
dṛṣṭipāto gavākṣaḥ | paraveśmasu paraveśamābhimukhyena | bṛhaspatir api |
varcaḥsthānaṃ vahnicayaṃ gartocchiṣṭāmbusecanam |
atyārāt parakuḍyasya na kartavyaṃ kathaṃcana || iti |
aratnidvayam utsṛjya kartavyam ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
viṇmūtrodakacakraṃ ca vahiniśvabhraniveśanam |
aratnidvayam utsṛjya parakuḍyān niveśayet || iti |
avaskārādibhiś catuṣpathādikaṃ na rodhayed ity āha nāradaḥ |
[401]         avaskarasthalaśvabhrabhramasyandanikādibhiḥ |
catuṣpathasurasthānarājamārgān na rodhayet || iti |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
yānty āyānti janā yena paśavaś cānivāritāḥ |
tad ucyate saṃsaraṇaṃ na roddhavyaṃ tu kenacit || iti |
yas tu saṃsaraṇe svabhrādikaṃ karoti tasya daṇḍam āha sa eva |
yas tatra saṃsare śvabhraṃ vṛkṣāropaṇam eva vā |
kāmāt purīṣaṃ kuryāc cet tasya daṇḍas tu māṣakaḥ || iti |
rājamārge tu purīṣakartur daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
samutsṛjed rājamārge yas tv amedhyam anāpadi |
sa dvau kārṣāpanau dadyād amedhyaṃ cāśu śodhayet ||
[402]         āpadgato ‘tha vā vṛddho garbhiṇī bālaeva vā |
paribhāṣaṇam arhanti tac c śodhyam iti sthitiḥ || iti | (MDh 9.282–83)
amedhyādinā taḍāgādiṣu doṣaṃ kurvatāṃ daṇḍam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
taḍāgodyānatīrthāni yo ‘medhyena vināśayet |
amedhyaṃ śodhayitvā tu daṇḍayet pūrvasāhasam ||
dūṣayan siddhatīrthāni sthāpitāni mahātmabhiḥ |
puṇyāni pāvanīyāni prāpnuyāt pūrvasāhasam || iti |
maryādābhedanādau daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
maryādāyāḥ prabhede ca sīmātikramaṇe tathā |
kṣetrasya haraṇe daṇḍā adhamottamamadhyamāḥ || iti | (YDh 2.155)
anekakṣetravyavacchedikā sādhāraṇī bhūr maryādā | tasyāḥ prakarṣeṇa bhedane sīmānam atilaṅghya karṣaṇe kṣetrasya tathā ninditapradarśanena haraṇe yathākrameṇadhottamamadhyamasāhasā [403] daṇḍā veditavyāḥ | kṣetragrahaṇaṃ gṛhārāmādyupalakṣaṇārtham | ajñānāt kṣetrādiharaṇe adhama eva daṇḍo veditavyaḥ | ata eva manuḥ |
gṛhaṃ taḍāgam ārāmaṃ kṣetraṃ vā bhīṣayā haran |
śatāni pañca daṇḍyaḥ syād ajñānād dviśato damaḥ || (MDh 8.264)
[404] iti | ye tu balād apahriyamāṇakṣetrādibhūmayas teṣām uttamo daṇḍaḥ prayoktavyaḥ | uttamasāhasaṃ yathā,
vadhaḥ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ purān nirvāsanāṅkane |
tadaṅgaccheda ity eko daṇḍa uttamasāhasaḥ ||
iti smaraṇāt | yat tu śaṅkhalikhitābhyāṃ sīmātikramaṇe daṇḍādhikyam uktam “sīmāvyatikrame tv aṣṭasahasram” iti, tat samagrasīmātikramaviṣayam | sīmāsaṃdhiṣūtpannavṛkṣātiviṣaye kātyāyanaḥ |
sīmāmadhye tu jātānāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ kṣetrayor dvayoḥ |
phalaṃ puṣpaṃ ca sāmānyaṃ kṣetrasvāmiṣu nirdiśet || iti |
anyakṣetre jātavṛkṣādiviṣaye sa eva |
anyakṣetre tu jātānāṃ śākhā yatrānyasaṃsthitā |
svāminaṃ taṃ vijānīyād yasya kṣetre tu saṃsthitā || iti |
parakṣetre prārthanayā kriyamāṇasetukūpādikaṃ kṣetrasvāminā na niṣeddhavyam | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[405]         na niṣedhyo ‘lpabādhas tu setuḥ kalyāṇakārakaḥ |
parabhūmiṃ haran kūpaḥ svalapkṣetro bahūdakaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.156]
yat svalpabādhakaṃ mahopakārakaṃ kṣetrādikaṃ ca bhavati tat kṣetraṃ svāminā na niṣeddhavyam | yat punar bahubādhakaṃ svalpopakārakaṃ ca tan niṣeddhavyaṃ bhavati | nārado ‘pi |
parakṣetrasya madhye tu setur na pratibadhyate |
mahāguṇo ‘lpabādhaś ced vṛddhir iṣṭā kṣaye sati || iti |
setuś ca dvividhaḥ | tathā ca sa evāha |
[406]        setus tu dvividho jñeyaḥ kheyo bandhyas tathaiva ca |
toyapravartanāt kheyo bandhyaḥ syāt tannivartanāt || iti |
setvādisaṃskāraviṣaye nāradaḥ |
pūrvapravṛttam utsannam apṛṣṭvā svāminaṃ tu yaḥ |
setuṃ pravartayet kaścin na sa tatphalabhāg bhavet ||
mṛte tu svāmini punas tadvaṃśye vāpi mānave |
rājānam āmantrya tataḥ prakuryāt setukarma tat || iti |
kṣetrasvāminam anabhyupagamya tadabhāve rājānaṃ vā setvādipravartane yājñavalkyaḥ |
svāmine yo ‘nivedyaiva kṣetre setuṃ pravartayet |
utpanne svāmino bhogaḥ tadabhāve mahīpateḥ || iti | (YDh 2.157)
prārthanārthadānena vā labdhānujñaḥ sann eva parakṣetre setuṃ pravartayed ity asya tātparyam | na tu setupravartakasya  [407] phalabhāktvaniṣedhe tātparyam, tasyāprasaktatvāt | atha vā dṛṣṭalābhaphalabhoktṛtvaniṣedhe tātparyam astu | kātyāyano ‘pi |
asvāmyanumatenaiva saṃskāraṃ kurute tu yaḥ |
gṛhodyānataḍāgānāṃ saṃskartā labhate na tu ||
vyayaṃ svāmini cāyāte na nivedya nṛpe yadi |
athāvedya prayuktas tu tadgataṃ labhate vyayam || iti |
kṣetrasvāmipārśve kṣetram idam ahaṃ karṣāmītīdam aṅgīkṛtya paścād yo na karṣati anyena vā na karṣayati taṃ praty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
phālāhatam api kṣetraṃ na kuryād yo na kārayet |
sa pradāpyaḥ kṛṣṭaphalaṃ kṣetram anyena kārayet || iti | (YDh 2.158)
yady api phālāhatam īṣad dhalena vidāritaṃ na samyag bījāvāpārhaṃ tathāpy akṛṣṭakṣetrasya phalaṃ yāvad yatrotpattyarhaṃ sāmantādikalpitaṃ [408] tāvad asau svāmine dāpanīyaḥ | tac ca kṣetraṃ pūrvakarṣakād ācchidyānyena tat kārayet | bṛhaspatir api |
kṣetraṃ gṛhītvā yaḥ kaścin na kuryān na ca kārayet |
svāmine sa śadaṃ dāpyo rājñe daṇḍaṃ ca tatsamam || iti |
svāmine kiyān śado deya ity apekṣite sa evāha |
cirāvasanne daśamaṃ kṛṣyamāṇe tathāṣṭamam |
susaṃskṛte tu ṣaṣṭhaṃ syāt parikalpya yathāvidhi || iti |
cirāvasanne cirakālam akṛṣṭe kṣetre karṣāmīti svīkṛtyopekṣite yāvatphalm anupekṣite labhyate tasya daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ dāpyaḥ | kṛṣyamāṇe sucirāvasanne svīkṛtyopekṣite tv aṣṭamaṃ bhāgaṃ dāpyaḥ | susaṃskṛte kṣetre upekṣite tu ṣaṣṭhaṃ bhāgaṃ dāpya ity arthaḥ | aśaktapretanaṣṭakṣetraviṣaye nāradaḥ |
aśaktapretanaṣṭeṣu kṣetrikeṣv anivāritaḥ |
kṣetraṃ ced vikṛṣet kaścid aśnuvīta sa tatphalam ||
[409]         kṛṣyamāṇeṣu kṣetreṣu kṣetrikaḥ punar āvrajet |
khilopacāraṃ tat sarvaṃ datvā kṣetram avāpnuyāt || iti |
khilopacāraḥ khilamañjanārtho vyayaḥ | tasyeyattāvadhāraṇārthaṃ vicāraṃ tasyāha sa eva |
saṃvatsareṇārdhākhilaṃ khilaṃ syād vatsarais tribhiḥ |
pañcavarṣāvasannaṃ tu kṣetraṃ syād aṭavīsamam || iti |
yadā punaḥ khilopacāraṃ svāmī na dadāti tadāpy āha kātyāyanaḥ |
aśaktito na dadyāc cet khilārthe yaḥ kṛto vyayaḥ |
tadaṣṭabhāgahīnaṃ tu karṣakaḥ phalam āpnuyāt ||
varṣān aṣṭau sa bhoktā syāt parataḥ svāmine tu tat || iti \
iti sīmāvivādanirṇayaḥ samāptaḥ
atha daṇḍapāruṣyam
tatsvarūpaṃ nāradenoktam |
paragātreṣv abhidroho hastapādāyudhādibhiḥ |
bhasmādīnām upakṣepair daṇḍapāruṣyam ucyate ||
[410] iti | paragātreṣu sthāvarajaṅgamāder anekadravyeṣu | hastapādāyudhādibhir ity ādigrahaṇād grāvādibhiḥ | droho hiṃsanam | tathā bhasmādibhiḥ bhasmarajaḥpaṅkapurīṣādyaiḥ upaghātaḥ saṃsparśarūpaṃ manoduḥkhotpādanam | tad ubhayaṃ daṇḍapāruṣyam | tasya traividhyam āha sa eva |
tasyāpi dṛṣṭaṃ traividhyaṃ hīnamadhyottamakramāt |
avagūraṇaniḥśaṅkhapātanakṣatadarśanaiḥ ||
hīnamadhyottamānāṃ tu dravyāṇām anatikramāt |
trīṇy eva sāhasāny āhus tatra kaṇṭakaśodhanam || iti |
niḥśaṅkapātanaṃ niḥśaṅkapraharaṇam | trīṇy eva sāhasāni sāhasīkṛtāni daṇḍapāruṣyāṇīty arthaḥ | daṇḍapāruṣye pañca prakārā vidhayas tenaivoktāḥ |
vidhiḥ pañcavidhas tūktaḥ etayor ubhayor api |
pāruṣye sati saṃrambhād utpanne kṣubdhayor dvayoḥ ||
[411]         sa mānyate yaḥ kṣmate daṇḍabhāg yo ‘tivartate |
pūrvam ākṣārayed yas tu niyataṃ syāt sa doṣabhāk ||
paścād yaḥ so ‘py asatkārī pūrve tu vinayo guruḥ |
dvayor āpannayos tulyam anubadhnāti yo ‘dhikam ||
sa tayor daṇḍam āpnoti pūrvo vā yadi vottaraḥ |
pāruṣyadoṣāvṛtayor yugapat saṃpravṛttayoḥ ||
viśeṣaś cen na lakṣyeta vinayaḥ syāt samastayoḥ |
śvapākaṣaṇḍhapāṣaṇḍavyaṅgeṣu badhireṣu ca ||
hastipavrātyadāseṣu gurvācāryāntikeṣu ca |
maryādātikrame sadyo ghāta evānuśāsanam ||
yam eva hy ativarteta nīcaḥ santaṃ janaṃ nṛṣu |
sa eva vinayaṃ kuryān na tadvinayabhāṅ nṛpaḥ ||
[412]         malā hy ete manuṣyāṇāṃ dhanam eṣāṃ malātmakam |
atas tān ghātayed rājā nārthadaṇḍena daṇḍayet || iti |
yas tu paścāt pravṛttasyāparādhābhāvo bṛhaspatinā darśitaḥ |
ākṛṣṭas tu samākrośān tāḍitaḥ pratitāḍayan |
hatvāparādhinaṃ caiva nāparādhī bhaven naraḥ || iti |
yo ‘pi paścāt pravṛttasya daṇḍaḥ kātyāyanena darśitaḥ |
abhīṣaṇena daṇḍena prahared yas tu mānavaḥ |
pūrvaṃ vā pīḍito vātha sa daṇḍyaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || iti |
so ‘pi pūrvpravṛttadaṇḍād alpadaṇḍārthaḥ | daṇḍapāruṣyasaṃdehe nirṇayakāraṇam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
asākṣīkahate cihnair yuktibhiś cāgamena ca |
draṣṭavyo vyavahāras tu kūṭacihnakṛto bhayāt || iti | (Ydh 2.212)
yadā kaścid rahasy anenāhaṃ tāḍitaḥ iti rājñe nivedayati tadā cihnais tadgātragataśramādibhiḥ [413] kāraṇaprayojanaparyālocanarūpābhir yuktibhir āgamena janapravādena ca śabdād divyena ca kūṭacihnakaraṇasaṃbhāvanābhayāt parīkṣā kāryety arthaḥ | rājaśāsanadravyaviśeṣeṇa daṇḍaviśeṣam āha sa eva |
bhasmapaṅkarajaḥsparśe daṇḍo daśapaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
amedhyapārṣṇiniṣṭhyūtasparśane dviguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
sameṣv evaṃ parastrīṣu dviguṇas tūttameṣu ca |
hīneṣv ardhadamo mohamadādibhir adaṇḍanam || iti | (MDh 2.213–14)
amedhaśabdena śleṣmanakhakarṇādidūṣikāśukrocchiṣhādikaṃ gṛhate | purīṣādisparśe kātyāyanaḥ |
chardimūtrapurīṣādyaiḥ pādādau ca caturguṇaḥ |
ṣaḍguṇaḥ kāyamadhye tu mūrdhni tv aṣṭaguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
[414] iti | ādiśabdena vasāśukrāsṛṅmajjāno gṛhyante | tāḍanārthaṃ hastodyamane tāḍane ca daṇḍam āha sa eva |
udgūrṇe tu hastasya karyo dvādaśako damaḥ |
sa eva dviguṇaḥ proktaḥ pātaneṣu svajātiṣu || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
udgūrṇe hastapāde tu daśaviṃśatikau damau |
parasparaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ śastre madhyamasāhasaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.216)
haste pāde vā tāḍanārtham udyate sati yathākramaṃ daśaviṃśatipaṇakau damau | parasparavadhārthaṃ śastre udyate sati sarveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ madhyamasāhaso daṇḍa ity arthaḥ | kāṣṭhādibhis tāḍane sa eva |
[415]         śoṇitena vinā duḥkhaṃ kurvan kāṣṭhādibhir naraḥ |
dvātriṃśataṃ paṇān daṇḍyo dviguṇaṃ darśane ‘sṛjaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.218)
tvagādibhede daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
tvagbhedakaḥ śataṃ daṇḍyo lohitasya ca darśakaḥ |
māṃsacchede śataṃ niṣkān pravāsyas tv asthibhedakaḥ || iti | (MDh 8.284)
pādādyākarṣaṇādau yājñavalkyaḥ |
pādakeśāṃśukakarolluñcaneṣu paṇān daśa |
piṇḍākarṣāṃśukāveṣṭapādādhyāse śataṃ damaḥ ||
karapadadato bhaṅge chedane karṇanasayoḥ |
madhyo daṇḍo vraṇodbhede mṛtakalpahate tathā ||
[416]         ceṣṭābhojanavāgrodhe netrādipratibhedane |
kandharābāhusakthnāṃ ca bhaṅge madhyamasāhasaḥ ||
ekaṃ ghnatāṃ bahūnāṃ ca yathoktād dviguṇo damaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.217–21)
avamatya keśaṃ gṛhītvā yo jhaṭity ākarṣati asau daśapaṇaṃ daṇḍyaḥ syāt | yaḥ punar aṃśukenāveṣṭya gāḍham āpīḍyākṛṣya pādena ghaṭṭayati asau śatapaṇān daṇḍyaḥ | karapādadantānāṃ pratyekaṃ bhaṅge karṇanāsikayoś ca chedane mṛtakalpahate ca madhyamasāhaso daṇḍaḥ | gamanabhojanabhāṣaṇanirodhe netrapratibhedane grīvāhastavraṇabhaṅge madhyamasāhaso daṇḍaḥ | militvaikasyāṅgabhaṅgaṃ kurvatāṃ bahūnām ekasyāparādhe yo daṇḍa uktaḥ tatra tasmād dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ pratyekaṃ veditavyaḥ ity arthaḥ | kātyāyano ‘pi |
[417]         karṇoṣṭhaghrāṇapādādijihvānāsākarasya ca |
chedane cottamo daṇḍo bhedane madhyamo bhṛguḥ ||
manuṣyāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ ca duḥkhāya prahate sati |
yathā yathā bhaved duḥkhaṃ daṇḍaṃ kuryāt tathā tathā || iti |
prātilomyena prahāre daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
viprapīḍākaraṃ chedyam aṅgam abrāhmaṇasya tu |
udgūrṇe prathamo daṇḍaḥ saṃsparśe tu tadardhikaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.215)
brāhmaṇapīḍākaram abrāhmaṇasya kṣatriyāder aṅgaṃ karacaraṇḍikaṃ chettavyam | brāhmaṇagrahaṇam uttamavarṇopalakṣaṇārtham | ata eva manuḥ |
yena kenacid aṅgena hiṃsyād cec chreṣṭham antyajaḥ |
chettavyaṃ tat tad evāsya tan manor anuśāsanam || (MDh 8.279)
[418] iti | udgūrṇe vadhārtham udyate śastrādike prathamasāhaso veditavyaḥ | śūdrasya tatrāpi chedanam eva hastādeḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
pāṇim udyamya daṇḍaṃ vā pāṇicchedanam arhati |
pādena praharan kopāt pādachedanam arhati || iti | (MDh 8.280)
udgūraṇārthaṃ śastrādisaṃsparśe prathamasāhasās ardhadaṇḍo veditavyaḥ | bhasmādisparśane tu punaḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ prātilomyāpavādeṣu dviguṇo damaḥ | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
prātilomyāpavādeṣu dviguṇatriguṇā damāḥ |
varṇānām ānulomyena tasmād ardhārdhahānitaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.207)
vākpāruṣyoktanyāyena daṇḍaḥ kalpanīyaḥ | kātyāyanaḥ |
vākpāruṣye yathaivoktaḥ pratilomānulomataḥ |
tathaiva daṇḍapāruṣye pātyo daṇḍo yathākramam || iti |
tatrāpi śūdraviṣaye viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
avaniṣṭhīvato darpāt dvāv oṣṭhau chedayen nṛpaḥ |
avamūtrayato meḍhram avaśardhayato gudam ||
[419]         keśeṣu gṛhṇato hastau chedayed avicārayan |
pādayor dāḍhikāyāṃ ca grīvāyāṃ ca vṛṣaṇeṣu ca ||
sahāsanam abhiprepsur utkṛṣṭasyāpakṛṣṭajaḥ |
kaṭyāṃ kṛtāṅko nirvāsyaḥ sphicaṃ cāsyāvakartayet || iti | (MDh 8.281–83)
aṅgachedanādau viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
dehendriyavināśe tu yadā daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet |
tadā tuṣṭikaraṃ deyaṃ samutthānaṃ ca paṇḍitaiḥ || iti |
tuṣṭikaraṃ vraṇatuṣṭikaram | samutthānaṃ vraṇāropaṇam | tannimittakaś ca vyayo vraṇagurutvānusāreṇa paṇḍitair auṣadhārthaṃ vyayārthaṃ ca kalpitamānaṃ vraṇāropaṇaṃ deyam,
[420]        samutthānaṃ vyayaṃ cāsau dadyād āvraṇaropaṇam |
iti tenaivoktatvāt | bṛhaspatir api |
aṅgāvapīḍane caiva chedane pīḍane tathā |
samutthānavyayaṃ dāpyaḥ kalahāpahṛtaṃ ca yat || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
kalahāpahṛtaṃ deyaṃ daṇḍaś ca dviguṇs tathā |
duḥkham utpadayed yas tu sa samutthānakaṃ vyayam |
dāpyo daṇḍaṃ ca yo yasmin kalahe samudāhṛtaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.221–22)
grāmyapaśupīḍāyāṃ daṇḍam āha viṣṇuḥ: “grāmyapaśughāte kārṣāpaṇaṃ daṇḍyaḥ | paśusvāmine tu tanmūlyaṃ dadyāt” iti | mūlyadānaṃ tu mṛtapaśuviṣayam | maraṇābhāve tu samutthānavyayaṃ dadyāt | tathā ca sa eva: “sarve ca puruṣapīḍākarāḥ samutthānavyayaṃ dāpyā grāmyapaśupīḍākarāś ca” [421] iti | prāṇighātanimittako daṇḍaḥ kvacid aśakyapratikāraviṣaye nāstīty āha manuḥ |
chinnanāsya bhagnayuge tiryakpratimukhāgate |
akṣabhaṅge ca yānasya cakrabhaṅge tathaiva ca ||
chedane caiva yantrāṇāṃ yoktraraśmyos tathaiva ca |
ākrande cāpy apaihīti na daṇḍaṃ manur abravīt || iti | (MDh 8.291–92)
śakyapratikāropekṣakasya daṇḍam āha sa eva |
yatrāpavartate yugyaṃ vaiguṇyāt prājakasya tu |
tatra svāmī bhaved daṇḍyo hiṃsāyāṃ dviśataṃ damam || (MDh 8.293)
[422] iti | prājakaḥ śakaṭādinetā | vaiguṇyaṃ nāma vaikalyaṃ vetanalāghavārthaṃ svāmyanumatam | yatra samarthaprājakadoṣeṇa prāṇihiṃsā tatra na svāmino daṇḍaḥ kiṃ tu prājakasyaivety āha sa eva |
prājakaś ced bhaved āptaḥ prājako daṇḍam arhati | iti | (MDh 8.294)
āptaḥ samartha ity arthaḥ | paśvabhidrohe daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
duḥkhe ca śoṇitotpāde śākhāṅgachedane tathā |
daṇḍaḥ kṣudrapaśūnāṃ tu dvipaṇaprabhṛtiḥ kramāt ||
liṅgasya chedane mṛtyau madhyamo mūlyam eva ca |
mahāpaśūnām eteṣu sthāneṣu dviguṇo damaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.225–26)
kṣudrapaśūnām ajāviprabhṛtīnāṃ duḥkhotpādane śoṇitotpādane | śākhāśabdena śṛṅgādikaṃ lakṣyate | aṅgāni karacaraṇādīni | teṣāṃ chede vā yathākramaṃ dvipaṇaprabhṛtir daṇḍaḥ | [423] dvipanacatuṣpaṇaṣaṭpaṇāṣtapaṇa ityādirūpaḥ | teṣāṃ liṅgachedane mṛtyukaraṇe vā madhyamasāhaso daṇḍaḥ mūlyadānaṃ ca | mahāpaśūnāṃ gogajavājiprabhṛtīnām eteṣu sthāneṣu pūrvoktadaṇād dviguṇadaṇdo veditavyaḥ ity arthaḥ | kārṣāpaṇaśatadaṇḍa ity anuvṛttau viṣṇur api: “paśūnāṃ puṃstvopaghātakārī tathā gajāśvoṣṭragoghāte ‘py ekapādaḥ | māṃsavikrayī ca paśumūlyaṃ dadyāt | āraṇyapaśughātī pañcāśataṃ kārṣāpaṇāt | pakṣighātī matsyaghātī ca daśa kārṣāpaṇāt | kīṭopaghātī kārṣāpaṇam” iti | kātyāyano ‘pi |
[424]         dvipaṇo dvādaśapaṇo vadhe tu mṛgapakṣiṇām |
sarpamārjāranakulaśvasūkaravadhe nṛṇām || iti |
manur api |
gokumārīdevapaśūn akṣamaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ tathā |
vāhayan sāhasaṃ pūrvaṃ prāpnuyād uttamaṃ vadhe ||
manuṣyamāraṇe kṣipraṃ cauravat kilbiṣaṃ bhavet |
prāṇabhṛtsu mahatsv ardhaṃ gogajoṣṭrahayādiṣu ||
kṣudrakāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ tu hiṃsāyāṃ dviśato damaḥ |
pañcāsat tu bhaved daṇḍaḥ śubheṣu mṛgapakṣiṣu ||
[425]        gardabhājāvikānāṃ tu daṇḍaḥ syāt pañcamāṣikaḥ |
māṣikas tu bhaved daṇḍaḥ śvasūkaranipātane || iti | (MDh 8.296–98)
rājño daṇḍadānavat svāminaḥ pratirūpakaṃ mūlyaṃ vā dadyād ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
pramāpaṇe prāṇabhṛtāṃ dadyāt tatpratirūpakam |
tasyānurūpaṃ mūlyaṃ vā dadyād ity abravīn manuḥ || iti |
sthāvaraprāṇipīḍākāriṇāṃ daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
vanaspatīnāṃ sarveṣām upabhogo yatha yathā |
tathā tathā damaḥ kāryo hiṃsāyām iti dhāraṇā || (MDh 8.285)
[426] iti | phalapuṣpopabhogatāratamyānurodhenottamamadhyamādayo daṇḍāḥ kalpanīyāḥ | tathā ca daṇḍya ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ: “phalpabhogadrumachedī tūttamaṃ sāhasam | puṣpopabhogadrumachedī madhyamaṃ sāhasam | vallīgulmalatāchedī kārṣāpaṇaśatam | tṛṇachedy ekam | sarve ca tatsvāmināṃ tadutpattim” iti | phalapuṣpopabhogadrumachedakādayaḥ chinnadrumasvāmināṃ tadutpattiṃ punaḥ pratiropitadrumaphalādibhogakālaparyantaṃ dāpyā iti śeṣaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
prarohiśākhināṃ śākhāskandhasarvavidāraṇe |
upajīvyadrumāṇāṃ ca viṃśater dviguṇo damaḥ ||
[427]         caityaśmaśānasīmāsu puṇyasthāne surālaye |
jātadrumāṇāṃ dviguṇo damo vṛkṣe ‘tha viśrute ||
gulmagucchakṣupalatāpratānauṣadhivīrudhām |
pūrvasmṛtād ardhadaṇḍaḥ sthāneṣūkteṣu kartane || iti | (YDh 2.227––29)
prarohiśākhināṃ vaṭādīnāṃ śākhāchedane skandhachedane ca yathākramaṃ viṃśatipaṇād daṇḍād ārabhya pūrvasmāt pūrvasmād uttara uttaro daṇḍo dviguṇaḥ | viṃśatipaṇacatvāriṃśatpaṇāśītipaṇā ity evaṃrūpaḥ | aprarohiśākhinām āmrādīnām upajīvyadrumāṇāṃ pūrvokteṣu sthāneṣu caityādisthāneṣūtpannānāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ śākhādichedane aśvatthapalāśādīnāṃ śākhādichedane pūrvoktād daṇḍād dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ | gulmā mālatyādayaḥ | gucchāḥ kuraṇḍakādayaḥ | kṣupāḥ karavīrādayaḥ | latā drākṣātimuktādayaḥ | pratānāḥ kāṇḍapraroharahitāḥ | oṣadhyaḥ phalapākāntāḥ śāliprabhṛtayaḥ | vīrudho guḍūciprabhṛtayaḥ | eteṣu sthāneṣu vakartane pūrvoktād daṇḍād ardhadaṇḍo veditavyaḥ | kuḍyābhighāte gṛhe kaṇṭakādiprakṣepaṇe ca daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[428]         abhighāte tathā chede bhede kuḍyāvapātane |
paṇān dāpyaḥ pañca daśa viṃśatiṃ tadvyayaṃ tathā ||
duḥkhotpādi gṛhe dravyaṃ kṣipan prāṇaharaṃ tathā |
ṣoḍaśādyaḥ paṇān dāpyo dvitīyo madhyamaṃ damam || iti | (YDh 2.223–24)
mudgarādinā kuḍyasyābhighāte vidāraṇe dvaidhīkaraṇe yathākramaṃ pañcapaṇo daśapaṇo viṃśatipaṇaś ca daṇḍaḥ | avapātane punas trayo daṇḍāḥ samucchitāḥ | kuḍyasaṃpādanārthaṃ dhanam api deyam | paragṛhe kaṇṭakādiprakṣepaṇe ṣoḍaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ | viṣabhujaṅgādiprakṣepaṇe madhyamasāhaso daṇḍa ity arthaḥ |
iti daṇḍaparuṣyam
atha vākpāruṣyam
tasya lakṣaṇaṃ nāradenoktam |
deśajātikulādīnām ākrośanyaṅgasaṃyutam |
yad vacaḥ pratikūlārthaṃ vākpāruṣyaṃ tad ucyate || iti |
kalahapriyā gauḍā iti deśākrośaḥ | atilolupā brāhmaṇā iti jātyākrośaḥ | krūracittā vaiśvāmitrā iti kulākrośaḥ | [429] ākrośa uccair bhāṣaṇaṃ nyaṅgam avadyaṃ tadubhayayuktaṃ yad udvegajananarthaṃ vākyaṃ tad vākpāruṣyam ity arthaḥ | tasya traividhyam āha sa eva |
niṣṭurāślīlatīvratvāt tad api trividhaṃ smṛtam |
gauravānukramāt tasya daṇḍo ‘py atra kramād guruḥ ||
sākṣepaṃ niṣṭuraṃ jñeyam aślīlaṃ nyaṅgasaṃyutam |
pātanīyair upakrośais tīvram āhur manīṣiṇaḥ || iti |
kātyāyano ‘pi |
yas tv asatsaṃjñitair aṅgaiḥ parasyākṣipati kvacit |
amūlair vātha mūlair vā niṣṭhurā vāk smṛtā budhaiḥ ||
nyagbhāvakaraṇaṃ vācā krodhāt tu kurute yadā |
vṛtter deśakulānāṃ vāpy aślilā sā budhaiḥ smṛtā ||
mahāpātakayoktrī ca rāgadveṣakarī ca yā |
jātibhraṃśakarī vātha tīvrā sā prathitā tu vāk || iti |
prathamamadhyamottamabhedena traividhyam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
deśagrāmakulādīnāṃ kṣepaḥ pāpena yojanam |
iṣṭaṃ vinā tu prathamaṃ vākpāruṣyaṃ tad ucyate ||
[430]        bhaginīmātṛsaṃbandham upapātakaśaṃsanam |
pāruṣyaṃ madhyamaṃ proktaṃ vācikaṃ śāstravedibhiḥ ||
abhakṣyāpeyakathanaṃ mahāpātakadūṣaṇam |
pāruṣyam uttamaṃ proktaṃ tīvraṃ marmābhighaṭṭanam || iti |
niṣṭhurākrośe daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
satyāsatyānyathāstotrair nyūnāṅgendriyarogiṇām |
kṣepaṃ karoti ced daṇḍyaḥ paṇān ardhatrayodaśān || iti | (YDh 2.204)
satyenāsatyenānyathāstotreṇa nyūnāṅgādīnāṃ tarjanītarjanaṃ yaḥ karoti asāv aradhādhikadvādaśapaṇaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | etat samavarṇagunaviṣayam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
samajātiguṇānāṃ tu vākpāruṣye parasparam |
vinayo ‘bhihitaḥ śāstre paṇān ardhatrayodaśa || iti |
yat tu manuvacanam,
[431]        kāṇaṃ vāpy atha vā khañjam anyaṃ vāpi tathāvidham |
tathyenāpi bruvan dāpyo daṇḍaṃ kārṣāpaṇāvaram || (MDh 8.274)
iti, tad api durvṛttaviṣayam | mātrādyākṣepakaṃ praty āha manuḥ |
mātaraṃ pitaraṃ jāyāṃ bhrātaraṃ śvaśruraṃ gurum |
akṣārayan śataṃ dāpyaḥ panthānaṃ vādadad guroḥ || iti | (MDh 8.275)
etac cāparādhiṣu mātrādiṣu jāyāyāṃ vā niraparādhāyāṃ veditavyam | svasrādyākṣepe daṇḍam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
kṣipan svasrādikaṃ dadyāt pañcāśatpaṇikaṃ damam | iti |
prātilomyānulomyābhyām ākrośe daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
śataṃ brāhmaṇm ākruśya kṣatriyo daṇḍam arhati |
vaiśyo ‘py ardhaśataṃ dve vā śūdras tu vadham arhati ||
pañcāśad brāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ kṣatriyasyābhiśaṃsane |
vaiśye syād ardhapañcāśat śūdre dvādaśako damaḥ || (MDh 8.267–68)
[432] iti | bṛhaspatir api |
vaiśyasya kṣatriyākrośe daṇḍanīyaḥ prado bhavet |
tadardhaṃ kṣatriyo vaiśyam kṣipan vinayam arhati ||
śūdrākrośe kṣatriyasya pañcaviṃśatiko damaḥ |
bṛhattve dviguṇaṃ tatra śāstravidbhir udāhrtam ||
vaiśyam ākṣārayan śūdro dāpyaḥ syāt prathamaṃ damam |
kṣatriyaṃ madhyamaṃ caiva vipram uttamasāhasam || iti |
bāhvādichedananiṣṭhurādbhibhāṣaṇe yājñavalkyaḥ |
bāhugrīvānetrasakthivināśe vācike damaḥ |
śatyas tadardhikaḥ pādanāsākarṇakarādiṣu ||
aśaktas tu vadann evaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ paṇān daśa |
tathā śaktaḥ pratibhuvaṃ dāpyaḥ kṣemāya tasya tu || iti | (YDh 2.208–09)
bāhvādīnāṃ vināśe tava bāhū chnadmīty evaṃ vācā pratipādite pratyekaṃ śataparimito daṇḍaḥ | pādanāsādiṣu tu [433] vācike tadardhikaḥ pañcāśatpaṇādiko daṇḍaḥ | aśaktas tv evaṃ vadan daśapaṇān daṇḍanīyaḥ | śaktaḥ punaḥ kṣīṇaśaktiṃ evaṃ vadam śatapaṇādyātmakaṃ daṇḍaṃ datvā tasya kṣemāya pratibhuvaṃ dadyād ity arthaḥ | aślīlabhāṣaṇe daṇḍam āha sa eva |
abhigantāsmi bhaginīṃ mātaraṃ vā taveti ca |
śapantaṃ dāpayed rājā pañcaviṃśatikaṃ damam | iti | (YDh 2.205)
tīvrākrośe daṇḍam āha sa eva |
patanīyakṛte kṣepe daṇḍo madhyamasāhasaḥ |
upapātakayukte tu dāpyaḥ prathamasāhasam || iti | (YDh 2.210)
manur api |
pāpopapāpavaktāro mahāpātakaśaṃsakaḥ |
ādyamadhyottamān daṇḍān dadyus tv ete yathākramam || (MDh 8.269)
[434] iti | traividyādyadhikṣepe yājñavalkyaḥ |
traividyanṛpadevānāṃ kṣepa uttamasāhasaḥ |
madhyamo jātipūgānāṃ prathamo grāmadeśayoḥ || iti | (YDh 2.211)
jātayaḥ brāhmaṇādayaḥ | pūgāḥ saṃghāḥ | śūdram adhikṛtyāhatur manu-nāradau |
ekajātir dvijātīṃs tu vācā dāruṇayā kṣipan |
jihvāyāḥ prāpnuyāc chedaṃ jaghanyaprabhavo hi saḥ ||
madhyamo jātipūgānāṃ prathamo grāmadeśayoḥ |
nāmajātigrahaṃ caiṣām abhidroheṇa kurvataḥ |
nikheyo ‘yomayaḥ śaṅkur jvanaṇn ‘sye daśāṅgulaḥ || iti | (MDh 8.270–71)
bṛhaspatir api |
dharmopadeśaṃ dharmeṇa viprāṇām asya kurvataḥ |
taptam āsiñcayet tailaṃ vaktre śrotre ca pārthivaḥ || iti |
kvacid vākpāruṣyadaṇḍāpavādam āha sa eva |
sacchūdrasyāyam uddiṣṭo vinayo’naparādhinaḥ |
guṇahīnasya pāruṣye brāḥmaṇo nāpardhnuyāt || iti |
iti vākpāruṣyam
[435]
atha steyam
tallakṣaṇam āha manuḥ |
syāt sāhasaṃ tv anvayavat prasabhām karma yat kṛtam |
niranvayaṃ bhavet steyaṃ hṛtvāpavyayane yadi || iti | (MDh 8.332)
asyārthāh – dravyarakaṣaka rājādhyakṣādisamakṣaṃ balāvaṣṭambhena yat paradravyāpahārādikaṃ kriyate tat sāhasam | steyaṃ punar asamakṣaṃ vañcayitvā yat paradravyagrahaṇaṃ tad iti | yat tu rājādhyakṣādikam āhṛtya na mayediam apahṛtam iti bhayān nihnute tad api steyam bhavati | ata eva nāradaḥ |
upāyair vividhair eṣāṃ chelayitvāpakarṣaṇm |
suptamattapramattebhyā steyam āhur manīḍiṇaḥ || iti tad apy kṣudramadhyottamadravyā trividham | tad āha sa eva |
[436]         tad api trividhaṃ jñeyaṃ dravyāpekṣaṃ manīṣibhiḥ |
kṣudramadhyamamukhyānāṃ dravyāṇām apahārataḥ || iti |
kṣudramadhyottamadravyāny āha hārītaḥ |
mṛdbhāṇḍāsanakhaṭvāsthitantucarmatṛṇādi yat |
śamīdhānyaṃ kṛtānnaṃ ca kṣudraṃ dravyam udāhṛtam ||
vāsaḥ kauśeyavarjaṃ tu govarjaṃ paśavas tathā |
hiraṇyavarjaṃ lohaṃ ca madyavrīhiyavādikam ||
hiraṇyaratnakauśeyastrīpuṃsagajavājinaḥ |
devabrāhmaṇarājñāṃ ca vijñeyaṃ dravyam uttamam || iti |
taskarajñānopāyām āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
grāhakair gṛhyate coro loptreṇātha padena vā |
pūrvakarmāparādhī ca tathā cāśuddhavāsakaḥ ||
anye ‘pi śaṅkayā grāhyā jātināmādinihnavaiḥ |
dyūtastrīpānasaktāś ca śuṣkabhinnamukhasvarāḥ ||
paradravyagṛhāṇāṃ ca pracchakā gūḍhacāriṇaḥ |
nirāyā vyayavantaś ca vinaṣṭadravyavikrayāḥ || (YDh 2.266–68)
[437] iti | grāhakai rājapuruṣair loptreṇāpahṛtabhājanādinā cauryacihnena naṣṭadravyadeśād ārabhya corapādānusāreṇa vā coro grahītavyāḥ | pūrvakarmāparādhī prāk prakhyātacauryaḥ | aśuddhavāsaka aprajñātasthānavāsī | jātinihnavo nāhaṃ śūdra iti | nāmanihnavo nāhaṃ ḍittha iti | ādigrahaṇāt svadeśagrāmakulādy upalakṣyate | naṣṭadravyavikrayāḥ bhinnabhājanajīrṇavastrādyanirjñātasvāmikavikrayakāriṇaḥ | evaṃvidhaliṅgaiḥ puruṣān gṛhītvā coro bhavanti na vā iti samyak parīkṣeta | na tāvatā stenaṃ niścinuyāt | tad āha nāradaḥ |
anyahastāt paribhraṣṭam akāmād utthitaṃ bhuvi |
coreṇāpi parikṣiptaṃ loptraṃ yatnāt parīkṣayet ||
asatyāḥ satyasaṃkāśāḥ satyāś cāsatyasaṃnibhāḥ |
dṛśyante vividhā bhāvāḥ tasmād yuktaṃ parīkṣaṇam || iti |
taskaro ‘pi dvividhaḥ | tad āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
prakāśāś cāparkāśāś c taskarā dvividhāḥ smṛtāḥ |
prajñāsāmarthyamāyābhiḥ prabhinnās te sahasradhā ||
[438]         naigamā vaidyakitavāḥ sabhyotkocakavañcakāḥ |
daivotpātavido bhadrāḥ śilpajñāḥ pratirūpakāḥ ||
akriyākāriṇaś caiva madhyasthāḥ kūṭasākṣiṇaḥ |
prakāśataskarā hy ete tathā kuhakajīvinaḥ || iti |
pratirūpakāḥ pratirūpakārā ity arthaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
prakāśavañcakās tatra kūṭamānatulāśritāḥ |
utkocakāḥ sopadhikāḥ kitavāḥ paṇyayoṣitaḥ ||
pratirūpakarāś caiva maṅgalādeśavṛttayaḥ |
ity evamādayo jñeyāḥ prakāśās taskarā bhuvi || iti |
aprakāśataskarāṇāṃ svarūpam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
saṃdhichedaḥ pānthamuṣo dvicatuṣpadahāriṇaḥ |
utkṣepakāḥ sasyaharā jñeyāḥ pracchannataskarāḥ || iti |
vyāso ‘pi |
sādhanāṅgānvitā rātrau vicaranty avibhāvitāḥ |
avijñātanivāsāś ca jñeyāḥ pracchannataskarāḥ ||
utkṣepakaḥ saṃdhibhettā pānth-udgranthikādayaḥ |
strīpuṃsayoḥ paśusteyī corā navavidhāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
utkṣepako dhaninām navadhānam avadhārya taddhanam utkṛtya grāhakaḥ | saṃdhibettā gṛhayoḥ sandhau sthitvā tatratyabhittibhettā | [439] pānthaḥ yaḥ kāntārādau pathikānāṃ pratyakṣāpahārakaḥ | parīdhānādigrathitaṃ dhanaṃ grahītuṃ tadgranthiṃ mocayati sa udgranthikaḥ | prakāśataskarāṇāṃ naigamādīnāṃ daṇḍam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
saṃsargacihnarūpaiś ca vijñātā rājapūruṣaiḥ |
pradāpyāpahṛtaṃ daṇḍyā damaiḥ śāstrapracoditaiḥ ||
pracchādya doṣaṃ vyāmiśraṃ punaḥ saṃskṛtya vikrayī |
paṇyaṃ taddviguṇaṃ dāpyo vaṇig daṇḍyaś ca tatsamam ||
ajñātauṣadhimantras tu yaś ca vyādher atattvavit |
rogibhyo ‘rthaṃ samādatte sa daṇḍyaś coravad bhiṣak ||
kūṭākṣadevinaḥ kṣudrā rājabhāryāharāś ca ye |
gaṇakā vañcakāś caiva daṇḍyās te kitavāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
anyāyavādinaḥ sabhyās tathaivotkocajīvinaḥ |
viśvastavañcakāś caiva nirvāsyāḥ sarva eva te ||
jyotirjñānaṃ tathotpātam aviditvā tu ye nṛṇām |
śrāvayanty arthalobhena vineyās te prayatnataḥ ||
daṇḍajinādibhir yuktam ātmānaṃ darśayanti ye |
hiṃsantaś chadmanā nṝṇāṃ vadhyās te rājapūruṣaiḥ ||
alpamūlyaṃ tu saṃskṛtya nayanti bahumūlyatām |
strībālakān vañcayanti daṇḍyās te ‘rthānusārataḥ ||
[440]         hemaratnapravālādyān kṛtrimān kurvate tu ye |
kretur mūlyaṃ pradāpyās te rājñā taddviguṇaṃ damam ||
madhyasthā vañcayanty ekaṃ snehalobhādinā yadā |
sākṣiṇaś cānyathā brūyur dāpyās te dviguṇaṃ damam || iti |
aprakāśataskarāṇāṃ saṃdhichidāsikānāṃ ca daṇḍam āha sa eva |
saṃdhichedakṛto jñātvā śūlam āgrāhayet prabhuḥ |
tathā pānthamuṣo vṛkṣe galaṃ badhvāvalambayet ||
manuṣyahāriṇo rājñā dagdhavyās te kaṭāgninā |
gohartur nāsikāṃ chindyād badhvā vāmbhasi majjayet ||
utkṣepakas tu saṃdaṃśair bhettavyo rājapūruṣaiḥ |
dhānyahartā daśaguṇaṃ dāpyaḥ syād dviguṇaṃ damam || iti |
granthibhedakasya daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
aṅgulīr ganthibhedasya chedayet prathame grahe |
dvitīye hastacaraṇau tṛtīye vadham arhati || (MDh 9.277)
[441] iti | aṅgulīḥ tarjanyaṅguṣṭhau | ata eva nāradaḥ |
prathame granthibhedānām aṅgulyaṅguṣṭhayor vadhaḥ |
dvitīye caiva yac cheṣaṃ tṛtīye vadham arhati || iti |
bandigrāhīdānāṃ daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
bandigrāhān tathā vājikuñjarāṇāṃ ca hāriṇaḥ |
prasahya ghātakāṃś caiva śūlān āropayen naraḥ || iti | (YDh 2.273)
ayam aṅgulichedanādiprāṇāntiko daṇḍa uttamasāhasaprāptiyogyadravyaviṣayaḥ,
[442]         vadhaḥ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ purān nirvāsanāṅkane |
tadaṅgacheda ity uktaḥ daṇḍa uttamasāhase |
iti nāradasmaraṇāt | kṣudramadhyamottamadravyeṣu prathamamadhyamottamasāhasarūpa-daṇḍaniyamo nāradena darśitaḥ |
sāhaseṣu ya evāste triṣu daṇḍo manīṣibhiḥ |
sa eva daṇḍaḥ steye ‘pi dravyeṣu triṣv anukramāt || iti |
jātyādibhedena daṇḍatāratamyam āha manuḥ |
aṣṭāpādyaṃ tu śūdrasya steye bhavati kilbiṣam |
ṣoḍaśaiva tu vaiśyasya dvātriṃśat kṣatriyasya ca ||
brāhmaṇasya catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ pūrṇaṃ vāpi śataṃ bhavet |
dviguṇā vā catuḥṣaṣṭis taddoṣaguṇavid dhi saḥ || iti | (MDh 8.337–38)
dānyaṃ daśabhya kumbhebhyo harato ‘bhyadhikaṃ vadhaḥ |
śeṣe ‘py ekādaśaguṇaṃ dāpyas tasya ca tad dhanam ||
suvarṇarajatādīnām uttamānāṃ ca vāsasām |
ratnānāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ śatād abhyadhikaṃ vadhaḥ ||
[443]        pañcāśatas tv abhyadhike hastacchedanam iṣyate |
śeṣe tv ekādaśaguṇaṃ mūlyād daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet ||
puruṣāṇāṃ kulīnānāṃ haraṇe nārīṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
ratnānāṃ caiva mukhyānāṃ haraṇe vadham arhati || iti | (MDh 8.320–323)
yasminn apahāre yo daṇḍa uktaḥ sa śūdrakartṛke ‘ṣṭaguṇaḥ, vaiśyakartṛke ṣoḍaśaguṇaḥ, kṣatriyakartṛke dvātriṃśad guṇaḥ, brāhmaṇakartṛke catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇaḥ śataguṇo vā aṣṭāviṃśatyuttaraśataguṇo vā | śeṣeṣu svalpamūlyeṣu mūlyād ekādaśaguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ kalpayet | kṣudradravyāṇāṃ māṣāt nyūnamūlyānāṃ mūlyāt pañcaguṇo daṇḍaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
kāṣṭabhāṇḍatṛṇādīnāṃ mṛṇmayānāṃ tathaiva ca |
veṇuvaiṇavabhāṇḍānāṃ tathā snāyvasthicarmaṇām ||
śākānām ārdramūlānāṃ haraṇe phalamūlayoḥ |
pakvānnānāṃ kṛtānnānāṃ matsyānām āmiṣasya ca |
sarveṣām alpamūlyānāṃ mūlyāt pañcaguṇo damaḥ || iti |
yat punar manunoktam,
[444]        sūtrakārpāsakiṇvānāṃ gomayasya guḍasya ca |
dadhnaḥ kṣīrasya takrasya pānīyasya tṛṇasya ca ||
veṇuvaiṇavabhāṇḍānāṃ lavaṇānāṃ tathaiva ca |
veṇuvaiṇavabhāṇḍānāṃ lavaṇānāṃ tathaiva ca |
mṛnmayānāṃ ca haraṇe mṛdo bhasmana eva ca ||
ajānāṃ pakṣiṇāṃ caiva tailasya ca ghṛtasya ca |
māṃsasya madhunaś caiva yac cānyat paśusaṃbhavam ||
anyeṣāṃ caivamādīnāṃ madyānām odanasya ca |
pakvānnānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ tanmūlyād dviguṇo damaḥ || (MDh 8.326–29)
iti, tad alpaprayojanaviṣayam | svalpaprayojanadravyāpahāre pānthādīnāṃ na daṇḍa ity āha manuḥ |
dvijo ‘dhvagaḥ kṣīṇavṛttiḥ dvāv ikṣū dve ca mūlake |
ādadānaḥ parakṣetrān na daṇḍaṃ dātum arhati || (MDh 8.341)
caṇakavrīhigodhūmayavānāṃ mudgamāṣayoḥ |
aniṣiddhair grahītavyo muṣṭir ekā pathi sthitaiḥ ||
tathaiva saptame bhakte bhaktāni ṣaḍ anaśnatā |
aśvastanavidhānena hartavyaṃ hīnakarmaṇaḥ || iti |
mahāparādhe ‘pi brāhmaṇasya na vadhadaṇḍa ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[445]         sacihnaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kṛtvā svarāṣṭrād vipravāsayet |
mahāparādhinam api brāhmaṇaṃ naiva ghātayet || iti | (YDh 2.279)
api tu lalāṭe cihnaṃ kṛtvā svadeśān nirvāsayet | tathā ca manuḥ |
gurutalpe bhagaḥ kāryaḥ surāpāne surādhvajaḥ |
steye ca śvapadaṃ kāryaṃ brahmahaṇy aśirāḥ pumān || iti | (MDh 9.237)
etac cāṅkanādi prāyaścittam akurvatāṃ daṇḍottarakālam na tu prāyaścittaṃ cikīrṣatām | tathā ca manuḥ |
[446]         prāyaścittaṃ tu kurvāṇāḥ sarve varṇā yathoditam |
nāṅkyā rājñā lalāṭe syuḥ dāpyās tūttamasāhasam || iti | (MDh 9.240)
bhaktāvakāśādidānena coropakāriṇaṃ praty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhaktāvakāśāgnyudakaśastropakaraṇavyayān |
datvā caurasya vā hantur jānato dama uttamaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.276)
kātyāyano ‘pi |
corāṇāṃ bhaktadā ye syus tathāgnyudakadāyakāḥ |
kretāraś caiva bhāṇḍānāṃ pratigrāhiṇa eva ca ||
samadaṇḍāḥ smṛtā hy ete ye ca pracchadayanti tān |
[447] iti | coropekṣiṇaṃ pray āha nāradaḥ |
śaktāś ca ya upekṣante te ‘pi taddoṣabhāginaḥ |
utkrośatāṃ janānāṃ tu hriyamāṇe dhane tathā ||
śrutvā ye nābhidhāvanti te ‘pi taddoṣabhāginaḥ | iti |
corādarśane dravyaprāptyupāyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
ghātite ‘pahṛte doṣo grāmabhartur anirgate |
vivītabhartus tu pathi coroddhartur avītake ||
svasīmni dadyād grāmas tu padaṃ vā yatra gacchati |
pañcagrāmī bahiḥ krośād daśagrāmy atha vā punaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.271–72)
ayam arthaḥ | yadā grāmamadhye ‘pi vadho dravyaharaṇaṃ vā jāyate tadā grāmapater eva coropekṣādoṣaḥ | tatparihārārthaṃ grāmapatir eva coraṃ gṛhītvā rājñe samarpayet | tad aśaktau dhanine hṛtaṃ dadyāt | yadi svagrāmāc corapadaṃ nirgataṃ na darśayati | darśane tu tatpadaṃ yatra praviśati tadviṣayādhipatir eva coraṃ dhanaṃ cārpayet | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
[448]         gocare yasya muṣyeta tena cauraḥ prayatnataḥ |
grāhyo dāpyo ‘tha vā dravyaṃ padaṃ yadi na nirgatam ||
nirgataṃ punar etasmān na ced anyatra yāti tat |
sāmantān mārgapālāṃś ca dikpālāṃś caiva dāpayet || iti |
vivīte tv apahāre vivītasvāmina eva doṣaḥ | yadā tv adhvany eva tad hṛtaṃ bhavati avivītake vā vivītād anyakṣetre tadā coroddhartur mārgapālasya dikpālasya cāparādhaḥ | yadā punar grāmād bahiḥ sīmāntaparyante kṣetre doṣo jāyate tadā tadgrāmavāsina eva dadyur yadi sīmno bahiś corapadaṃ na nirgatam | nirgate punar yatra tat praviśati sa eva grāmaś corārpaṇādikaṃ kuryāt | yadā tv anekagrāmamadhye krośamātrād bahiḥ pradeśe moṣādikaṃ jāyate corapadaṃ ca janasaṃmardād bhagnaṃ tadā pañcagrāmī daśagrāmī vā dadyāt | vikalpas tu pratyāsattyādyapekṣayā vyavasthitaḥ | yadā dāpayitum aśakto rājā tadā svayaṃ dadyāt | tathā ca gautamaḥ: [449] “corahṛtam avajitya yathāsthānaṃ gamayet | svakośād vā dadyāt” iti | steyasaṃdehe nirṇayopāyam āha vṛddhamanuḥ |
yadi tasmin dāpyamāne bhaven moṣe tu saṃśayaḥ |
muṣitaḥ śapathaṃ dāpyo bandhubhir vāpi sādhayet || iti |
coravadhaprakāraviśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
yāṃs tatra corān gṛhṇīyāt tān vitāḍyābhibandhya ca |
avakṛṣya ca sarvatra hanyāc citravadhena tu || iti |
iti steyaprakaraṇam
atha sāhasam
tatsvarūpaṃ nāradenoktam |
sahasā kriyate karma yat kiṃcid baladarpitaiḥ |
tat sāhasam iti proktaṃ saho balam ihocyate || iti |
nanu – sāhasaṃ cauryavāgdaṇḍapāruṣyastrīsaṃgrahaṇebhyo na vyatiricyate, teṣāṃ tadavāntaraviśeṣatvāt | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
[450]         manuṣyamāraṇaṃ cauryaṃ paradārābhimarśanam |
pāruṣyam ubhayaṃ caiva sāhasaṃ tu caturvidham || iti |
tat kathaṃ pṛthag asya vyavahārapadatā |
satyam | tathāpi baladarpāvaṣṭambhopādhis tebhyo bhidyate iti daṇḍātirekārthaṃ pṛthag abhidhānam | manuṣyamāraṇarūpasya sāhasasya tebhyo ‘tirekāt tadarthaṃ vā pṛthag abhidhānam | tasya ca traividhyam āha nāradaḥ |
tat punas trividhaṃ jñeyaṃ prathamaṃ madhyamaṃ tathā |
uttamaṃ ceti śāstreṣu tasyoktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ pṛthak ||
phalamūlodakādīnāṃ kṣetropakaraṇasya ca |
bhaṅgākṣepāvamardādyaiḥ prathamaṃ sāhasaṃ smṛtam ||
vāsaḥ pāsvannapānānāṃ gṛhopakaraṇasya ca |
etenaiva prakāreṇa madhyamaṃ sāhasaṃ smṛtam ||
vyāpādo viṣaśastrādyaiḥ paradārābhimarśanam |
prāṇoparodhi yac cānyad uktam uttamasāhasam || iti |
trividhe ‘pi sāhase daṇḍam āha sa eva |
tasya daṇḍaḥ kriyāpekṣaḥ prathamasya śatāvaraḥ |
madhyamasya tu śāstrajñair dṛṣṭaḥ pañcaśatāvaraḥ ||
uttame sāhase daṇḍaḥ sahasrāvara iṣyate |
vadhaḥ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ purān nirvāsanāṅkane ||
tadaṅgacheda ity ukto daṇḍa uttamasāhase ||
[451] iti | paradravyāpaharaṇarūpe sāhase daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
tanmūlyād dviguṇo daṇḍo nihnave tu caturguṇaḥ |
yaḥ sāhasaṃ kārayati sa dāpyo dviguṇaṃ damam |
yaś caivam uktvāhaṃ dātā kārayet sa caturguṇam || iti | (YDh 2.230–31)
sāhasaviśeṣeṣu daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
arghyākrośātikramakṛt bhrātṛbhāryāprahāradaḥ |
saṃdiṣṭasyāpradātā ca samudragṛhabhedakṛt ||
sāmantakulikādīnām apakārasya kārakaḥ |
pañcāśatpaṇiko daṇḍa eṣām iti viniścayaḥ ||
svacchandaṃ vidhavāgāmī vikruṣṭe nābhidhāvakaḥ |
akāraṇe ca vikroṣṭā caṇḍālaś cottamān spṛśan ||
śūdrapravrajitānāṃ ca daive pitrye ca bhojakaḥ |
ayuktaṃ śapathaṃ kurvann ayogyo yogakarmakṛt |
vṛṣakṣudrapaśūnāṃ ca puṃstvasya pratighātakṛt |
[452]         sādhāraṇasyāpalāpī dāsīgarbhavināśakṛt ||
pitṛputrasvasṛbhrātṛdampatyācāryaṛtvijām |
eṣām apatitānnyonyatyāgī ca śatadaṇḍabhāk || (YDh 2.232–37)
śastrāvapāte garbhasya pātane cottamo damaḥ |
uttamo vādhamo vāpi puruṣastrīpramāpaṇe ||
vipraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ caiva puruṣaghnīm agarbhiṇīm |
setubhedakarīṃ cāpsu śilāṃ baddhvā praveśayet ||
viṣāgnidāṃ patigurunijāpatyapramāpaṇīm |
vikarṇakaranāsauṣṭhīṃ kṛtvā gobhiḥ pramāpayet ||
kṣetraveśmavanagrāmavivītakhaladāhakāḥ |
rājapatnyabhigāmī ca dagdhavyās tu kaṭāgninā || iti | (YDh 2.277–82)
avijñātakartṛke sāhase sāhasikajñānopāyam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
[453]         hataḥ saṃdṛśyate yatra ghātakas tu na dṛśyate |
pūrvavairānumānena jñātavyaḥ sa mahībujā ||
prativeśyānuveśyau ca tasya mitrāribāndhavāḥ |
praṣṭavyā rājapuruṣaiḥ sāmādibhir upakramaiḥ ||
vijñeyo ‘sādhusaṃsargāc cihnād gūḍhaiś ca lakṣaṇaiḥ |
eṣoditā ghātakānāṃ taskarāṇāṃ ca bhāvanā || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
avijñātahatasyāpi kalahaṃ sutabāndhavāḥ |
praṣṭavyā yoṣitaś cāsya prapuṃsi ratāḥ pṛthak ||
strīdravyavṛttikāmo vā kena vāyaṃ gataḥ saha |
mṛtyudeśasamāsannaṃ pṛcched vāpi janaṃ śanaiḥ || iti | (YDh 2.280–81)
uktajñānopāyāsaṃbhave tu kātyāyanaḥ |
vinā cihnais tu yat kāryaṃ sāhasākhyaṃ pravartate |
śapathaiḥ sa viśodhyaḥ syāt sarvavādeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
[454] iti | sāhasikavadhe viśeṣam āha vyāsaḥ |
jñātvā tu ghātakaṃ samyak sasahāyaṃ sabāndhavam |
hanyāc citravadhopāyair udvejanakarair nṛpaḥ || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
prakāśavadhakā ye tu tathā copāṃśughātakāḥ |
jñātvā samyag dhanaṃ hṛtvā hantavyā vividhair vadhaiḥ || iti |
etad brahmaghnakṣatriyādiviṣayam | tad āha baudhāyanaḥ: “kṣatriyādīnāṃ brāhmaṇavdhe vadhaḥ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ ca | teṣām eva tulyāpakṛṣṭavadhe yathābalam anurūpaṃ daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet” iti (BDh 1.10.18.19–20) | bahūnām ekaghātārthe pravṛttānāṃ doṣānurūpadaṇḍābhidhānārtham āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ekaṃ ced bahavo hanyuḥ saṃrabdhāḥ puruṣaṃ narāḥ |
marmaghātī tu yas teṣāṃ sa ghātaka iti smṛtaḥ || iti |
yo marmaghātakaḥ sa eva vadhānurūpadaṇḍabhāg bhavatīty arthaḥ | tathā ca ghātakavyatiriktānām api daṇḍas tenaivoktaḥ |
[455]         ārambhakṛt sahāyaś ca tathā dharmānudeśakaḥ |
āśrayaḥ śastradātā ca bhaktadātā vikarmiṇām ||
yuddhopadeśakaś caiva tadvināśapravartakaḥ |
upekṣākārakaś caiva doṣavaktānumodakaḥ ||
aniṣeddhā kṣamo yaḥ syāt sarve tatkāryakāriṇaḥ |
yathāśakty anurūpaṃ tu daṇḍaṃ teṣāṃ prakalpayet || iti |
anurūpaṃ doṣānurūpam | marmahantur doṣabhāgitvaṃ dvayor darśayati sa eva |
ārambhakṛt sahāyaś ca doṣabhājau tadardhataḥ | iti |
evaṃ mārgānudeśakānāṃ kālāntare ‘pi doṣalāghavam ūhyam | sāhasasadṛśāparādheṣv api daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
vasānas trīn paṇān daṇḍyo nejakas tu parāṃśukam |
vikrayāvakrayādhānayāciteṣu paṇān daśa || iti | (YDh 2.238)
avakrayaḥ etāvat kālam upabhogārthaṃ vastraṃ dāsyāmi tvaṃ [456] mahyam etāvad dhanaṃ dehīti samayaṃ kṛtvā vastrapradānaṃ nejakasya niyamātikrame daṇḍaprāptyarthaṃ niyamam āha manuḥ |
śālmalīphalake ślakṣṇe nenijyāt nejakaḥ śanaiḥ |
na ca vāsāṃsi vāsobhir nirharen na ca vāsayet || iti | (MDh 8.396)
pramādān nāśane nāradaḥ |
mūlyāṣṭabhāgo hīyeta sakṛd dhautasya vāsasaḥ |
dviḥ pādas tris tṛtīyāṃśaś caturdhaute ‘rdham eva ca ||
ardhakṣayāt tu parataḥ pādāṃśāpacayaḥ kramāt |
yāvat kṣīṇadaśaṃ jīrṇaṃ jīrṇasyāniyamaḥ kṣayaḥ || iti |
aṣṭapaṇakrītasya sakṛd dhautasya vastrasya nāśane ekapaṇanyūnaṃ mūlyaṃ deyam | dvirdhautasya paṇadvayonaṃ trirdhautasya tripaṇonaṃ caturdhautasya paṇacatuṣṭayam | tataḥ paraṃ pratinirṇejanam avaśiṣṭaṃ mūlyaṃ śiṣṭapādāpacayena yāvajjīrṇaṃ deyam | jīrṇasya nāśane [457] tv icchāto mūlyadānaṃ kalpanīyam ity arthaḥ | pitāputravirodhe sākṣyādīnāṃ daṇḍam āha sa eva |
pitāputravirodhe tu sākṣiṇas tripaṇo damaḥ |
antareṇa tayor yaḥ syāt tasyāpy aṣṭaguṇo damaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.239)
pitāputrayoḥ kalahe yaḥ sākṣyam aṅgīkaroti na punaḥ kalahaṃ vārayati sa paṇatrayaṃ daṇḍyaḥ | yaś ca tayoḥ sapaṇe vivāde paṇadāne pratibhūr bhavati kalahaṃ vā vardhayati sa tu tripaṇād aṣṭaguṇaṃ caturviṃśatipaṇaṃ daṇḍanīya ity arthaḥ | anyeṣv api tatsadṛśāparādheṣu daṇḍam āha sa eva |
tulāśāsanamānānāṃ kūṭakṛn nāṇakasya ca |
ebhis tu vyavahartā yaḥ sa dāpyo damam uttamam ||
akūṭaṃ kūṭakaṃ brūte kūṭaṃ yaś cāpy akūṭakam |
sa nāṇakaparīkṣī tu dāpya uttamasāhasam ||
bhiṣaṅ mithyā caran daṇḍyas tiryakṣu prathamaṃ damam |
mānuṣe madhyamaṃ rājapuruṣeṣūtamaṃ damam ||
abadhyaṃ yaś ca badhnāti baddhaṃ yaś ca pramuñcati |
aprāptavyavahāraṃ ca sa dāpyo damam uttamam ||
[458]         mānena tulayā vāpi yo ‘ṃśam aṣṭakaṃ haret |
daṇḍaṃ sa dāpyo dviśataṃ vṛddhau hānau ca kalpitam ||
bheṣajasnehalavaṇagandhadhānyaguḍādiṣu |
paṇyeṣu prakṣipan hīnaṃ paṇān daṇḍyas tu ṣoḍaśa ||
mṛccarmamaṇisūtrāyaḥkāṣṭhavalkalavāsasām |
ajātau jātikaraṇe vikreyāṣṭaguṇo damaḥ ||
samudraparivartaṃ ca sārabhāṇḍaṃ ca kṛtrimam |
ādhānaṃ vikrayaṃ cāpi nayato daṇḍakalpanā ||
hīne paṇe tu pañcāśat paṇe tu śatam ucyate |
dvipaṇe dviśato daṇḍo mūlyavṛddhau tu vṛddhimān ||
saṃbhūya kurvatām arghaṃ sabādhaṃ kāruśilpinām |
arghasya hrāsaṃ vṛddhiṃ vā jānatāṃ dama uttamaḥ ||
saṃbhūya vaṇijā paṇyam anargheṇoparundhatām |
vikrīṇatāṃ ca vihito daṇḍa uttamasāhasaḥ ||
rājani sthāpyate yo ‘rghaḥ prayahaṃ tena vikrayaḥ |
krayo vā niḥsravas tasmād vaṇijāṃ lābhakṛt smṛtaḥ ||
svadeśapaṇye tu śataṃ vaṇig gṛhṇīta pañcakam |
daśakaṃ pāradeśye tu yaḥ sadyaḥ krayavikrayī ||
[459]         paṇyasyopari saṃsthāpya vyayaṃ paṇyasamudbhavam |
argho ‘nugrahakṛt kāryaḥ kretur vikretur eva ca || iti | (YDh 2.240–53)
tulā tolanadaṇḍaḥ | prasthādi parimāṇam | nāṇakaṃ mudrācihnitaṃ drammaniṣkādi | eteṣāṃ kūṭakṛd deśaprasiddhaparimāṇād anyathākṛt | nyūnatvam ādhikyaṃ vā drammāder avyavahārikamudritatvaṃ vā tāmrādigarbhatvaṃ vā karoti | yaś ca trapusīsādirūpais tair vyavaharati tāv ubhau pratyekam uttamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīyau | yaḥ punar nāṇakaparīkṣakaḥ samyag eva kūṭam iti brūte asamyag vā samyag iti so py uttamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | yaḥ punar vaidyaḥ āyurvedānabhijña eva jīvanārthaṃ cikitsājño ‘ham iti tiryaṅmanuṣyarājapuruṣeṣu cikitsāṃ karoti sa yathākramaṃ prathamamadhyamottamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | yo ‘py anaparādhinaṃ rājājñayā vinā badhnāti baddhaṃ tayā vinā muñcati vyavahārārtham āhūtam anirvṛttavyavahāraṃ cotsṛjati so ‘pi uttamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | yo ‘pi vaṇig vrīhikārpāsādeḥ paṇyasyāṣṭamāṃśaṃ kūṭamānena kūṭatulayā vāpaharati [460] asau paṇānāṃ dviśataṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | apahriyamāṇadravyasya punar vṛddhau hānau ca daṇḍasyāpi vṛddhihānī kalpanīye | bheṣajam auṣadhadravyam | sneho ghṛtādiḥ | gandhadravyam uśīrādi | eteṣv asāradravyaṃ vikrayārthaṃ miśrayataḥ ṣoḍaśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ | na vidyate bahumūlyā jātir yasmin mṛccarmādike tad ajāti tasmin jātikaraṇe vikrayārthaṃ gandhavarṇarasāntarasaṃcāraṇena bahumūlyajātīyasādṛśyasaṃpādane vikreyasyāpāditasādṛśyasya mṛccharmādeḥ paṇyasyāṣṭaguṇo daṇḍaḥ | samudrakasya karaṇḍakādeḥ parivartanaṃ vyatyāsaḥ | yo ‘nyad eva muktānāṃ pūrṇaṃ karaṇḍakaṃ darśayitvā anyad eva sphaṭikānāṃ pūrṇaṃ hastalāghavāt samarpayati yaś ca sārabhāṇḍaṃ kastūrikādikaṃ kṛtrimaṃ kṛtvā vikrayam ādhiṃ vā nayati tasyaivaṃ daṇḍakalpanā | kṛtrimakastūrikāder mūlyabhūte paṇe hīne nyūne nyūnapaṇamūlye iti yāvat | tasmin kṛtrime vikrīte pañcāśatpaṇo daṇḍaḥ | paṇamūlye tu śataṃ dvipaṇamūlye tu dviśato daṇḍaḥ | evaṃ mūlyavṛddhau daṇḍavṛddhir unneyā | rājanirūpatārghasya hrāsaṃ vṛddhiṃ vāpi jānanto vaṇijaḥ militvā kāruśilpināṃ kārūṇāṃ rajakādīnāṃ śilpināṃ citrakārādīnāṃ pīḍākaram arghāntaraṃ lābhalobhāt kurvantaḥ paṇasahasraṃ daṇḍanīyāḥ | ye punar deśāntarād āgataṃ paṇyaṃ hīnamūlyena prārthayamānā uparundhanti mahārgheṇa vā vikrīṇate teṣām uttamasāhaso daṇḍaḥ | [461] rājani saṃnihite ‘pi sati yas teṇārgho nirūpyate tenārgheṇa krayo vā vikrayo vā kāryaḥ | niḥsravaḥ nirgataḥ sravaḥ avaśeṣaḥ | tasmād rājanirūpitād arghāt yo niḥsravaḥ sa eva vaṇijāṃ lābhakārī na punaḥ svachandaparikalpitād arghāt | arghakaraṇe viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
pañcarātre pañcarātre pakṣe pakṣe ‘tha vā gate |
kurvīta caiṣāṃ pratyakṣam arghasaṃsthapanaṃ nṛpaḥ || iti | (MDh 8.402)
svadeśapaṇye śatapaṇamūlye pañcakaṃ lābhārthaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | pāradeśye tu daśapaṇaṃ lābhaṃ gṛhṇīyāt yasya paṇagrahaṇadivasa eva vikrayaḥ | yaḥ punaḥ kālāntare vikrīṇīte tasya kālotkarṣavaśāl lābhotkarṣaḥ kalpyaḥ | deśāntarād āgate paṇye deśāntaragamanapratyāgamana-bhāṇḍagrahaṇaśulkādisthāneṣu prayuktam arthaṃ parigaṇayya paṇyamūlyena saha melayitvā yathā śatapaṇamūlye daśapaṇo lābhaḥ saṃpadyate tathā ketṛvikretror anugrahakāry argho rājñā sthāpanīyaḥ |
iti sāhasaprakaraṇam
[462]
atha strīsaṃgrahaṇam
tasya traividhyam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
pāpamūlaṃ saṃgrahaṇaṃ triprakāraṃ nibodhata |
balopādhikṛte dve tu tṛtīyam anurāgajam ||
anicchayā tv apakṛtaṃ mattonmattakṛtaṃ tathā |
pralaye yat tu rahasi balātkārakṛtaṃ tu tat ||
chadmanā gṛham ānīya datvā vā madakāraṇam |
saṃyogaḥ kriyate yat tu tad upādhikṛtaṃ viduḥ ||
anyonyam anurāgeṇa dūtasaṃpreṣaṇena vā |
kṛtaṃ rūpārthalobhena jñeyaṃ tad anurāgajam || iti |
punar api traividhyam āha sa eva |
tat punas trividhaṃ proktaṃ prathamaṃ madhyamottamam |
apāṅgaprekṣaṇaṃ hāsyaṃ dūtasaṃpreṣaṇaṃ tathā ||
sparśo bhūṣaṇaṃ vastrāṇāṃ prathamaḥ saṃgrahaḥ smṛtaḥ |
preṣaṇaṃ gandhamālyānāṃ dhūpamdhvānnavāsasām ||
[463]         saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ rahasi ca madhyamaṃ saṃgrahaṃ viduḥ |
ekaśayyāsanaṃ krīḍā cumbanāliṅganaṃ tathā ||
etat saṃgrahaṇaṃ proktam uttamaṃ śāstravedibhiḥ || iti |
yoṣitsaṃgrahaṇajñānopāyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pumān saṃgrahaṇe grāhyaḥ keśākeśi parastriyā |
sadyo vā kāmajaiś cihnaiḥ pratipattau dvayos tathā ||
nīvīstanaprāvaraṇasakthikeśāvamarśanam |
adeśakālasaṃbhāṣā sahaikasthānam eva ca || iti | (YDh 2.283–84)
stīpuṃsayor mithunībhāvaḥ saṃgrahaṇam | tatra pravṛttaḥ parabhāryayā saha keśākeśikrīḍanena sadya abhinavaiḥ kāmajaiḥ kararuhadaśanādikṛtavraṇaliṅgaiḥ dvayoḥ saṃpratipatyā vā grāhyaḥ | yo ‘pi paradāraparidhānagranthipradeśakucaprāvaraṇajaghanaśiroruhādisparśane sābhilāṣa iva karoti nirjanadeśe janākīrṇe ‘py andhakārākule akāle saṃlāpaṃ karoti parabhāryayā sahaikatra mañcakādau tiṣṭhati so ‘pi grāhyaḥ | manur api |
striyaṃ spṛśed adeśe yaḥ spṛṣṭo vā marṣayet tayā |
parasparasyānumate sarvaṃ saṃgrahaṇaṃ smṛtam || (MDh 8.358)
[464]        darpād vā yadi vā mohāc chlāghayā vā svayaṃ vadet |
pūrvaṃ mayeyaṃ bhukteti tac ca saṃgrahaṇaṃ smṛtam || iti |
tatra daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
sajātāv uttamo daṇḍa ānulomye tu madhyamaḥ |
prātilomye vadhaḥ puṃso nāryāḥ karṇādikartanam || iti | (YDh 2.286)
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ balātkāreṇa sajātīyaguptaparabhāryāgamane sāśītipaṇasahasro daṇḍaḥ | yadā tv ānulomyena hīnavarṇaguptaparabhāryāgamanaṃ tadā madhyamasāhaso daṇḍaḥ | yadā punaḥ savarṇām aguptām ānulomyena guptāṃ vā vrajati tadā manunā viśeṣa uktaḥ |
sahasraṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍyo guptāṃ viprāṃ balād vrajan |
śatāni pañca daṇḍyaḥ syād icchantyā saha saṃgataḥ ||
sahasraṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍaṃ dāpyo gupte tu te vrajan |
śūdrāyāṃ kṣatriyaviśoḥ sahasraṃ tu bhaved damaḥ || iti | (MDh 8.378, 383)
etad gurusakhibhāryādivyatiriktaviṣayam, tatra daṇḍāntaravidhānāt | tad āha nāradaḥ |
[465]         mātā mātṛṣvasā śvaśrūr mātulānī pitṛṣvasā |
pitṛvyasakhiśiṣyastrī bhaginī tatsakhī snuṣā ||
duhitācāryabhāryā ca sagotrā śaraṇāgatā |
rājñī pravrajitā dhātrī sādhvī varṇottamā ca yā ||
āsām anyatamāṃ gacchan gurutalpaga ucyate |
śiśnasyotkartanaṃ tasya nānyo daṇḍo vidhīyate || iti |
pratilomyena utkṛṣṭastrīgamane kṣatriyāder vadhaḥ | etad guptāviṣayam | anyatra dhanadaṇḍaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
ubhāv api tu tāv eva brāhmaṇyā guptayā saha |
viplutau śūdravad daṇḍyau dagdhavyau vā kaṭāgninā ||
brāhmaṇīṃ yady aguptāṃtu gacchetāṃ vaiśyapārthivau |
vaiśyaṃ pañcaśataṃ kuryāt kṣatriyaṃ tu sahasriṇam || iti | (MDh 8.377, 376)
śūdrasyāguptotkṛṣṭastrīgamane liṅgachedanasarvasvaharaṇe guptāgamane tu vadhasarvasvāpahārau | tathā ca sa eva |
[466]        śūdro guptam aguptaṃ vā dvaijātaṃ varṇam āvasan |
aguptaikāṅgasarvasvī guptaṃ sarveṇa hīyate || iti | (MDh 8.374)
atraiva viṣaye bṛhaspatir api |
sahasā kāmayed yas tu dhanaṃ tasyākhilaṃ haret |
utkṛtya liṅgavṛṣaṇau bhrāmayed gardabhena tu || iti |
śūdrasyety anuvṛttau gautamaḥ: “āryastryabhigamane liṅgoddhāraḥ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ ca” iti (GDh 12.2) | nāryāḥ punar hīnavarṇagamane nāsādikartanam | ayaṃ vadhādyupadeśo rājñaḥ tasyaiva pālanādhikārāt na dvijātimātrasya, “brāhmaṇaḥ parīkṣārtham api śastraṃ nādadīta” (ĀpDh 1.10.29.6) [467] iti śastragrahaṇasya niṣedhāt | yadā tu rājño nivedane kālātipātaśaṅkā tadā dvijātimātrasyāpi vadhādikāro ‘sty eva,
śastraṃ dvijātibhir grāhyaṃ dharmo yatroparudhyate |
nātatāyivadhe doṣo hantur bhavati kaścana ||
prakāśaṃ vāprakāśaṃ vā manyus taṃ manyum ṛcchati || iti | (MDh 8.348, 351)
iti śastragrahaṇābhyanujñānāt | kṣatriyavaiśyayor anyonyastryabhigamane yathākramaṃ sahasrapañcaśatapaṇātmakau daṇḍau | tad āha manuḥ |
vaiśyaś cet kṣatriyāṃ guptāṃ vaiśyāṃ vā kṣatriyo vrajet |
yo brāhmaṇyām aguptāyāṃ tāv ubhau daṇḍam arhataḥ || iti | (MDh 8.382)
sādhāraṇastrīgamane daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
avaruddhāsu dāsīṣu bhujiṣyāsu tathaiva ca |
gamyāsv api pumān dāpyaḥ pañcāśatpaṇikaṃ damam || iti | (YDh 2.290)
uktalakṣaṇā varṇastriyo dāsyaḥ | tā eva svāminā śuśrūṣāhānivyudāsārthaṃ gṛha eva sthātavyam ity evaṃ [468] puruṣāntarabhogato niruddhāḥ avaruddhāḥ | niyatapuruṣaparigrahāḥ bhujiṣyāḥ | yadā dāsyo ‘varuddhā bhujiṣyā vā bhaveyuḥ tāsu tāsu | caśabdād veśyāsvairiṇīnām api sādhāranastrīṇāṃ bhujiṣyāṇāṃ grahaṇam | tāsu ca sarvapuruṣasādhāraṇatayā gamyāsv api gacchan pañcāśatpaṇaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ, paraparigṛhītatvena tāsāṃ paradāratulyatvāt | etad evābhipretya nārado ‘pi |
svairiṇy abrāhmaṇī veśyā dāsī niṣkāsinī ca yā |
gamyāḥ syur ānulomyena striyo na pratilomataḥ ||
āsv eva tu bhujiṣyāsu doṣaḥ syāt paradāravat |
gamyā api hi nopeyā yat tāḥ paraparigrahāḥ || iti |
niṣkāsinī svāmyavaruddhā dāsī | anavaruddhadāsyādyabhigamane yājñavalkyaḥ |
prasahya dāsyabhigame daṇḍo daśapaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
bahūnāṃ yady akāmāsau caturviṃśatikaḥ pṛthak || iti | (YDh 2.291)
puruṣasaṃbhogajīvikāsu dāsīṣu svairiṇyādiṣu ca śulkadānam antareṇa balātkāreṇābhigacchato daśapaṇo daṇḍaḥ | anicchantīm ekāṃ gacchatāṃ bahūnāṃ pratyekaṃ caturviṃśatipāṇātmako daṇḍaḥ | kanyāharaṇe daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[469]         alaṃkṛtāṃ haran kanyām uttamaṃ hy anyathādhamam |
daṇḍaṃ dadyāt savarṇāsu prātilomye vadhaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
sakāmāsv anulomāsu na doṣas tv anyathā damaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.287–88)
alaṃkṛtā vivāhābhimukhī tāṃ kanyām apaharan uttamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | tadanabhimukhīṃ savarṇām apaharan prathamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | uttamavarṇajāṃ kanyām apaharataḥ kṣatriyāder vadha eva | ānulomyena sakāmām apaharan na daṇḍabhāk bhavati | akāmām apaharan prathamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | kanyādūṣaṇe tu daṇḍam āha sa eva |
dūṣaṇe tu karacheda uttamāyāṃ vadhaḥ smṛtaḥ |
śataṃ strīdūṣaṇe dadyād dve tu mithyābhiśaṃsane ||
paśūn gacchan śataṃ dāpyo hīnastrīṃ gāṃ ca madhyamam || iti | (YDh 2.288–89)
yady akāmāṃ kanyā balātkāreṇa nakhakṣatādinā dūṣayati tadā tasya karachedaḥ | uttamāyā dūṣaṇe vadhaḥ | kanyāyāḥ yadi kaścid vidyamānān evāpasmārarājayakṣmādidoṣān [470] prakāśyeyam akanyeti dūṣayaty asau śataṃ dāpyaḥ | mithyābhiśaṃsane tu punar avidyamānadoṣāviṣkāreṇa dūṣaṇe dve śate dāpanīyaḥ | govyatiriktapaśugamane paṇaśataṃ dāpyaḥ | tathā hīnāṃ striyam antyāvasāyinīṃ gāṃ ca gacchan madhyamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīya ity arthaḥ | yadā punas tām eva aṅgulīprakṣepeṇa yonikṣataṃ kurvan dūṣayati tadā viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
abhiṣahya tu yaḥ kanyāṃ kuryād darpeṇa mānavaḥ |
tasyāśu kartye aṅgulyau daṇḍaṃ cārhati ṣaṭśatam ||
sakāmāṃ dūṣayaṃs tulyo nāṅgulichedam arhati |
dviśataṃ tu damaṃ dāpyaḥ praṅgavinivṛttaye ||
kanyaiva kanyāṃ yā kuryāt tasyāḥ syād dviśato damaḥ |
śulkaṃ ca dviguṇaṃ dadyāt śiphāś caivāpnuyād daśa ||
yā tu kanyāṃ prakuryāt strī sā sadyo mauṇḍyam arhati |
aṅgulyor eva vā chedaṃ khareṇodvahanaṃ tathā || iti | (MDh 8.367–70)
yadā punar utkṛṣṭajātīyāṃ kanyāṃ sānurāgām akāmāṃ gacchati tadā tasya kṣatriyāder vadhaḥ | yadā savarṇāṃ sakāmāṃ [471] abhigacchati tadā gomithunaṃ śulkaṃ tatpitre dadyāt | anicchati pitari daṇḍarūpeṇa rājñe dadyat | savarṇām akāmāṃ tu gacchato vadha eva | tad āha manuḥ |
uttamāṃ sevamānas tu jaghanyo vadham arhati |
śulkaṃ dadyāt sevamānaḥ samām icchet pitā yadi ||
yo ‘kāmāṃ dūṣayet kanyāṃ sa sadyo vadham arhati |
sakāmāṃ dūṣayāṃs tulyo na vadhaṃ prāpnuyān naraḥ || iti | (MDh 8.366, 364)
caṇḍālyādigamane daṇḍam āha sa eva |
antyābhigamane tv aṅkyaḥ kubandhena pravāsayet |
śūdras tathāṅkya eva syād antysyāryāgame vadhaḥ ||
ayonau gacchato yoṣāṃ puruṣaṃ vāpi mehataḥ |
caturviṃśatiko daṇḍas tathā pravrajitāsu ca || iti | (YDh 2.294, 293)
antyā cāṇḍālī | tāṃ gacchantaṃ traivarṇikaṃ prāyaścittānabhimukhaṃ “sahasraṃ tv antyajastriyam” (MDh 8.385) [472) iti | manuvacanānusāreṇa sahasraṃ daṇḍayitvā kutsitavarṇena bhagākāreṇāṅkayitvā svarāṣṭrān nirvāsayet | śūdraḥ punaś caṇḍālīṃ gacchann aṅkya eva | antyaja iti pāṭhe caṇḍāla eva bhavati | caṇḍālasya tūtkṛṣṭajātistriyābhigamane vadha eva | yoṣāṃ mukhādāv abhigacchataḥ puruṣaṃ vā mukhe mehataḥ pravrajitāṃ gacchataḥ caturviṃśatipaṇo daṇḍaḥ | vañcanayā strīsaṃgrahaṇe daṇḍam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
chadmanākārayed yas tu tasya sarvaharo damaḥ |
aṅkayitvā bhagāṅkena purān nirvāsayet tataḥ || iti |
iti strīsaṃgrahaṇam
atha strīpuṃsayogākhyaṃ vivādapadam
tasya svarūpam āha nāradaḥ |
vivāhādividhiḥ strīṇāṃ yatra puṃsāṃ ca kīrtyate |
strīpuṃsayogasaṃjñaṃ tad vivādapadam ucyate || iti |
strīrakṣaṇam āha manuḥ |
[473]         asvatantrāḥ striyaḥ kāryāḥ puruṣaiḥ svair divāniśam |
viṣayeṣu ca sajjantyaḥ saṃsthāpyā hy ātmano vaśe ||
sūkṣmebhyo ‘pi prasaṅgebhyaḥ striyo rakṣyā viśeṣataḥ |
dvayor hi kulayoḥ śokam āvaheyur arakṣitāḥ ||
imaṃ hi sarvavarṇānāṃ paśyanto dharmam uttamam |
yatante rakṣituṃ bhāryāṃ bhartāro durbalā api ||
svāṃ prasūtiṃ caritraṃ ca kulam ātmānam eva ca |
svaṃ ca dharmaṃ prayatnena jāyāṃ rakṣan hi rakṣati ||
na kaścid yoṣitaḥ śaktaḥ prasahya parirakṣitum |
etair upāyayogais tu śakyās tāḥ parirakṣitum ||
arthasya saṃgrahe caināṃ vyaye caiva niyojayet |
śauce dharme ‘nnapaktyāṃ ca pāriṇāhyasya cekṣaṇe || iti | (MDh 9.2, 5–7, 10–11)
svaiḥ puruṣaiḥ bhartṛbhiḥ sarvadā asvatantrāḥ kāryāḥ | viṣaye gītādau āsaktās tato vyāvartanīyāḥ | arakṣitās tu duścaritena bhartṛpitṛkulayoḥ śokaṃ kuryuḥ | tasmāt kuladvayavṛddhyarthaṃ rakṣyās tāḥ | yady api prasahyāvarudhya rakṣitum aśakyās [474] tathāpy arthasaṃgrahādau niyojanena puruṣāntaracintanāvsarasyāpradānena rakṣed ity arthaḥ | bṛhaspatir api |
sūkṣmebhyo ‘pi prasaṅgebhyo nivāryā strī svabandhubhiḥ |
śaśrvādibhir gurustrībhiḥ pālanīyā divāniśam || iti |
doṣarahitastrīparityāginaṃ pray āha nāradaḥ |
anukūlām avāgduṣṭāṃ dakṣāṃ sādhvīṃ prajāvatīm |
tyajan bhāryām avasthāpyo rājñā daṇḍena bhūyasā || iti |
daṇḍena sthāpayitum aśakye tv āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
ājñāsaṃpādinīṃ dakṣāṃ vīrasūṃ priyavādinīm |
tyajan dāpyas tṛtīyāṃśam adravyo bharaṇaṃ striyāḥ || iti | (YDh 1.76)
buddhvā striyaṃ tyajed ity āha nāradaḥ |
anyonyaṃ tyajator āgaḥ syād anyonyaviruddhayoḥ |
strīpuṃsayor na coḍhāyā vyabhicārād ṛte striyāḥ || iti |
vivāhasaṃskārarahitayor hīnajātīyastrīpuṃsayor virodhenānyonyaṃ tyajato doṣo nāsti | vivāhasaṃskṛtāyās tu vyabhicārād eva tyāgo na virodhamātreṇa | etac ca svachandavyabhicāriṇīviṣayam,
[475]        svacchandagā tu yā nārī tasyās tyāgo vidhīyate |
iti yamasmaraṇāt | śiṣyagādyā api saṃtyājyāḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
catasras tu parityājyāḥ śiṣyagā gurugā tathā |
patighnī tu viśeṣeṇa juṅgitopagatā tathā || iti | (VaDh 21.19)
hārīto ‘pi: “garbhaghnīm adhamavarṇāṃ śiṣyasutagāminīṃ pānavyasanāsaktāṃ dhanadhānyavikrayakarīṃ vivarjayet” iti (HārSm 13.21) | vivarjanaṃ ca vyavahāraparityāgaḥ | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “vyavāye tīrthagamane dharmebhyaś ca nivartate” iti (VaDh 21.9) | vyavāyaḥ  saṃyogaḥ | tīrthagamanaśabdena smārtaṃ karma lakṣyate | dharmaśabdena śrautam | caśabdena saṃbhāṣaṇādikam | vyādhitādīnāṃ tu saṃbhogamātrasya tyāga ity āha devalaḥ |
vyādhitāṃ strīprajāṃ vandhyām unmattāṃ vigatārtavām |
aduṣṭāṃ labhate tyaktuṃ tīrthān na tv eva karmaṇaḥ ||
[476] iti | tīrthāt saṃbhogād ity arthaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
vandhyāṃ strījananīṃ nindyāṃ pratikūlāṃ ca sarvadā |
kāmato nābhinandeta kurvann evaṃ na doṣabhāk ||
anarthaśīlāṃ satataṃ tathaivāpriyavādinīm |
pūrvāśinīṃ ca yā bhartuḥ kṣipraṃ nirvāsayed gṛhāt ||
strīṃ dhanabhraṣṭasarvasvāṃ garbhavidhvaṃsinīṃ tathā |
bhartuś ca vadham icchantīṃ striyaṃ nirvāsayet purāt || iti |
baudhāyano ‘pi |
aprajāṃ daśame varṣe strīprajāṃ dvādaśe tyajet |
mṛtaprajāṃ pañcadaśe sadyas tv apriyavādinīm || iti | (BDh2.2.4.6)
saṃyoga iti śeṣaḥ |
[477]        bhartuḥ pratiniviśena yā bhāryā skandayed ṛtum |
tāṃ grāmamadhye vikhyāpya bhrūṇaghnīṃ nirdhamed gṛhāt || (BDh 4.1.12.20)
aśuśrūṣākarīṃ nārīṃ bandhakīṃ patihiṃsakam |
tyajanti puruṣāḥ prājñāḥ kṣipram apriyavādinīm || iti |
tyāgaś ca vadhapratinidhitvena kāryaḥ | tathā ca yamaḥ |
svachandavyabhicāriṇyāḥ vivasvāṃs tyāgam abravīt |
na vadhaṃ na ca vairūpyaṃ vadhaṃ strīṇāṃ vivarjayet |
na caiva strīvadhaḥ kuryān na kṣaivāṅgavikartanam || iti |
strīṇāṃ vadhaṃ kurvan tāsāṃ vivarjanaṃ kuryād bhartā na karṇanāsādikartanam ity arthaḥ | anye ca strīpuṃdharmā ācārādhyāye pracañcitā iti nātra prapañcitāḥ |
iti strīsamgrahaḥ
atha dāyabhāgākhyaṃ vyavahārapadaṃ kathyate
tallakṣaṇaṃ nāradenoktam |
vibhāgo ‘rthasya paitryasya putrair yatra prakalpyate |
dāyabhāga iti proktaṃ vyavahārapadaṃ budhaiḥ ||
[478] iti | dāyo nāma yad dhanaṃ svāmisaṃbandhād eva anyasya svaṃ bhavati tad ucyate | sa dvividhaḥ | apratibandhaḥ sapratibandhaś ceti | pitṛdhanaṃ pitāmahadhanaṃ vā apratibandho dāyaḥ | putrādidhanaṃ tu pitrādīnāṃ sapratibandho dāyaḥ | tasya vibhāgo dāyavibhāga ity ucyate | ata eva yogīśvaraḥ dāyaśabdena pitṛdvārāgataṃ mātṛdvārāgataṃ ca dravyam evocyate iti | saṃgrahakāraś ca |
pitṛdvārāgataṃ dravyaṃ mātṛdvārāgataṃ ca yat |
kathitaṃ dāyaśabdena tadvibhāgo ‘dhunocyate || iti |
vibhāgakālam āha manuḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ pituś ca mātuś ca sametya bhrātaraḥ samam |
bhajeran paitṛkaṃ ṛktham anīśās te hi jīvatoḥ || (MDh 9.104)
[479] iti | ūrdhvaṃ pitur iti pitṛdhanavibhāgakālaḥ | mātur ūrdhvam iti mātṛdhanavibhāgakālaḥ | tataś caitad uktaṃ bhavati | pitur ūrdhvaṃ mātari jīvantyām api pitṛdhanavibhāgaḥ kāryaḥ | tathā mātur ūrdhvaṃ pitari jīvate ‘pi mātṛdhanavibhāgaḥ kārya eva, anyataradhanavibhāge ubhayor ūrdhvakālapratīkṣaṇānupayogād iti | tad uktaṃ saṃgrahakāreṇa |
pitṛdravyavibhāgasya jīvantyām api mātari |
asvatantratayāsvāmyaṃ yasmān mātuḥ patiṃ vinā ||
mātṛdravyavibhāgo ‘pi tathā pitari jīvati |
satsv apatyeṣu yasmān na strīdhanasya patiḥ patiḥ || iti |
ayam arthaḥ | patimaraṇe pitṛbhāryāyāḥ patyuparamāt patyur dhane yasmāt asvātantryeṇa na svāmitvam yasmāc cāpatyeṣu vidyamāneṣu bhāryādhanasya bhāryāmaraṇe ‘pi patir na svāmī tasmāt tayor anyatarasmin jīvaty apy anyataradhanavibhāgo yukta iti | etena jīvatos tat tad dravyavibhāgeṣu putrāṇāṃ na svātantryam ity arthād uktaṃ bhavati | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ: “na jīvati pitari putrā rikthaṃ bhajeran | yady api syāt paścād adhigataṃ taiḥ anarhā eva putrāḥ | arthadharmayoḥ asvātantryāt” iti | asyāyam arthaḥ | yady api janmānantaram eva putrāḥ pitṛdhane svāmitvaṃ pratipannāḥ tathāpi pitari jīvati taddhanaṃ na [480] vibhajeran | yato dharmārthayor asvātantryād vibhāgakaraṇe ‘narhāḥ | arthāsvātantryam nāma tadādānapradānaos asvātantryam iti | tad āha hārītaḥ: “jīvati pitari putrāṇām arthādānavisargākṣepeṣv asvātantryam” iti (HārSm 17.21) | arthādānam arthopabhogaḥ | visargo vyayaḥ | ākṣepo bhṛtyādeḥ śikṣārtham adhikṣepādiḥ | dharmāsvātantryaṃ pṛthagiṣṭāpūrtādāv apravṛttiḥ | yat tu devalenoktam,
pitary uparate putrā vibhajeran pitur dhanam |
asvāmyaṃ hi bhavet teṣāṃ nirdoṣe pitari sthite || iti |
tad apy asvātantryapratipādanaparam, pitṛdhane putrāṇāṃ janmanā svāmyasya lokasiddhatvāt |
nanu – śāstraikasamadhigamyasya svatvasya kathaṃ lokasiddhatā | śāstrasiddhatvaṃ ca, “svāmī rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādhigameṣu | brāhmaṇasyādhikaṃ labdham | kṣatriyasya vijitam | nirviṣṭaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ” (GDh 10.39–42) [481] iti gautamavacanād avagamyate | apratibandho dāyo ṛkthaṃ na sapratibandho dāyaḥ | saṃvibhāgaḥ sapratibandho dāyaḥ | ananyapūrvasya jalatṛṇakāṣṭhādeḥ svīkāraḥ parigrahaḥ | nidhyādiprāptir adhigamaḥ | eteṣu nimitteṣu satsu svāmī bhavati | brāhmaṇasya pratigrahādinā yal labdhaṃ tad adhikam asādhāraṇam | kṣatriyasya vijayadaṇḍādilabdhaṃ yat tad asādhāraṇam | vaiśyasya kṛṣigorakṣādilabdhaṃ nirviṣṭaṃ tad asādhāraṇam | śūdrasya dvijaśuśrūṣādinā bhṛtirūpeṇa yad labdhaṃ tad asādhāraṇam | evam anulomapratilomajānāṃ svasvavihitāśvasārathyādinā yal labdhaṃ tad adhikam ity arthaḥ | atraiva saṃgrahakāro nyāyam āha |
vartate yasya yad dhaste tasya svāmī sa eva tu |
anyasvam anyahasteṣu cauryād yaiḥ kiṃ na vartate || iti |
anyasvasyānyahaste sthitasya darśanena tasyaiva svāmitāpatteḥ | ataḥ śāstraikasamadhigamyaṃ svatvatvam | kiṃ ca yadi yasyāntike yad dhanaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tasya sa eva svāmī tarhi asya svam anenāpahṛtam iti na brūyāt, yasyaivāntike dṛṣṭaṃ tasya svāmitvāt | svatvasya laukikatve,
[482]         yo ‘dattādāyino hastāl lipseta brāhmaṇo dhanam |
yājanādhyāpanenāpi yathā stenas tathaiva saḥ || iti | (MDh 8.340)
yājanādinā adattādāyinaḥ sakāśād dravyam arjayato daṇḍavidhānam anupapannaṃ syāt | tasmāc chāstraikasamadhigamyaṃ svatvam iti ||
maivam, laukikam eva svatvaṃ laukikārthakriyāsādhanatvāt, vrīhyādivat | āhavanīyādīnāṃ vaidikādīnām api laukikapākādisādhanatvam astīty anaikāntiko hetuḥ – iti cet |
na | na hi teṣām āhavanīyādirūpeṇa pādādisādhanatvaṃ kiṃ tarhi laukikāgnyādirūpeṇety asti vaiṣamyam | kiṃ ca pāmarāṇām api svatvavyavahāradarśanāt svatvasya laukikatvam avagamyate | yat tu gautamavacanam: “svāmī rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgeṣu” (GDh 10.39) ityādy anupannam ity uktam ||
tan na | pratigrahādyupāyakasya svatvasya laukikatve sthite brāhmaṇādīnāṃ pratigrahādyupāyaniyamārthatvāt śāstrasya | yad apy uktam anyasya svam anyenāpahṛtam – iti na brūyād iti, tad asat, svatvahetubhūtakrayādisaṃdehāt svatvasaṃdehopapatteḥ | yad api coktam “yo ‘dattādāyinaḥ” [483] iti adattādāyinaḥ sakāśād yājanādinā dravyam arjayitur daṇḍavidhānam anupapannam iti | tad apy asat, pratigrahādiniyatopāyakasyaiva svatvasya laukikatvāt niyamātikrameṇa dravyam arjayato daṇḍavidhānam upapadyate | evam “tasyotsargeṇa śudhyanti” iti prāyaścittavidhānam api | evaṃ ca svatvasya laukikatve asatpratigrahādilabdhaṃ dhanaṃ tatputrādīnāṃ dāyatvena svam iti vibhājyam | na teṣāṃ doṣasaṃbandhaḥ,
sapta vittāgamā dharmyā dāyo lābhaḥ krayo jayaḥ |
prayogaḥ karmayogaś ca satpratigraha eva ca || (MDh 10.115)
iti manusmaraṇāt |
idam atra cintanīyam: vibhāgāt svaṃ svaya vā vibhāgaḥ iti |
atrāyaṃ pūrvapakṣaḥ | vibhāgāt svaṃ janmanaiva svatve utpannamātrasya putrasyāpi svaṃ sādhāraṇam iti dravyasādhyeṣv ādhānādiṣu pitur adhikāravidhir na syāt | kiṃ ca,
bhartrā prītena yad dattaṃ striyai tasmin mṛte ‘pi tat |
sā yathākāmam aśnīyād dadyād vā sthāvarād ṛte || iti |
prītidānavacanam apy anupannaṃ syāt | yad api –
[484]         maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ sarvasyaiva pitā prabhuḥ |
sthāvarasya tu sarvasya na pitā na pitāmahaḥ ||
pitṛprasādād bhujyante vastrāṇy ābharaṇāni ca |
sthāvaraṃ tu na bhujyeta prasāde sati paitṛke ||
iti, tat pitāmahopāttasthāvaraviṣayam | tasmāt svāmināśād vibhāgād vā svatvaṃ na janmanaiva |
rāddhāntas tu | janmanaiva svatvaṃ loke prasiddham | vibhāgaśabdaś ca bahusvāmikadhanaviṣaye loke prasiddho nānyadīyadhanaviṣayo na prahīṇaviṣayaḥ | kiṃ ca “utpattyaivārthaṃ svāmitvāl labhetety ācāryāḥ” iti gautamavacanāj janmanaiva svatvam avagamyate | yad uktam: “manimuktāpravālānām’ ityādivacanaṃ pitāmahopāttasthāvaraviṣayam iti, tad ayuktam | “na pitā na pitāmahaḥ” iti vacanāt, pitāmahasya hi svārjitam api dhanaṃ putrapautrayoḥ sator adeyam iti vacanam janmanā svatvaṃ gamayatīti | yad apy uktam: arthasādhyeṣv ādhānādiṣu pitur anadhikāra iti, tad ayuktam, vacanād evādhikārāvagamāt | yad api coktam janmanaiva svatve “bhartrā prītena yad dattam” ityādi viṣṇuvacanaṃ nopapadyate iti, tad apy ayuktam, sādhāraṇye ‘pi dravyasya vacanād eva [485] prītidāne pitur adhikāropapatteḥ | sthāvarādau tu svārjite ‘pi putrādipāratantryam eva,
sthāvaraṃ dvipadaṃ caiva yady api svayam arjitam |
asaṃbhūya sutān sarvān na dānaṃ na ca vikrayaḥ ||
ye jātā ye ‘py ajātāś ca ye ca garbhe vyavasthitāḥ |
vṛttiṃ ca te ‘bhikāṅkṣanti na dānaṃ na ca vikrayaḥ ||
ityādivacanāt | āpadādau tu svātantryam asty eva,
eko ‘pi sthāvare kuryād dānādhamanavikrayam |
āpatkāle kuṭumbārthe dharmārtheṣu viśeṣataḥ ||
iti smaraṇāt |
tasmāt suṣṭūktaṃ janmanaiva svatvam iti |
prakṛtam anusarāmaḥ | aparam api vibhāgakālam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
vibhāgaṃ cet pitā kuryād icchayā vibhajet sutān |
jyeṣṭhaṃ vā śreṣṭhabhāgena sarve vā syuḥ samāṃśinaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.114)
yadā pitā vibhāgaṃ kartum icchati tadā putrān ātmanaḥ sakāśād icchayā vibhajet | icchayā vibhāgaprakāraḥ “jyeṣṭhaṃ vā śreṣṭhabhāgena” iti | śreṣṭhabhāgaḥ soddhāravibhāgaḥ uddhāraprakāraḥ smṛtyantare darśitaḥ |
[486]         jyeṣṭhasya viṃśa uddhāraḥ sarvadravyāc ca yad varam |
tato ‘rdhaṃ madhyamasya syāt turīyaṃ tu yavīyasaḥ || iti | (MDh 9.112)
atha vā – sarve jyeṣṭhādayaḥ putrāḥ samāṃśabhājaḥ kāryāḥ syuḥ | atha ca viṣamo bhāgaḥ svārjitadravyaviṣayaḥ | kramāgate tu sarveṣām api samasvāmyāt samāṃśaḥ syāt, pitur icchayā viṣamavibhāgasyāyuktvatvāt | nārado ‘pi kālāntaram āha |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pituḥ putrā vibhajeyur dhanaṃ samam |
mātur nivṛtte rajasi prattāsu bhaginīṣu ca ||
nivṛtte vāpi ramaṇe pitary uparataspṛhe ||
[487] iti | śaṅkho ‘pi: “akāme pitari ṛkthavibhāgo vṛddhe viparītacetasi dīrgharogiṇi ca” iti | asyārthaḥ | akāme vibhāgam anicchati pitari ativṛddhe viparītacetasi aprakṛtisthe dīrgharogiṇi acikitsyarogagraste ca putrāṇām icchayaiva vibhāgo bhavatīty arthaḥ | dīrgharogagrahaṇam atikupitāder upalakṣaṇam | ata eva nāradaḥ |
vyādhitaḥ kupitaś caiva viṣayāsaktamānasaḥ |
anyathāśāstrakārī ca na vibhāge pitā prabhuḥ || iti |
pitrā samavibhāgakaraṇe viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
yadi kuryāt samā aṃśān patnyaḥ kāryāḥ samāṃśikāḥ |
na dattaṃ strīdhanaṃ yāsāṃ bhartrā vā śvaśureṇa vā || iti | (YDh 2.115)
yadi svecchayā pitā putrān samabhāginaḥ karoti tadā adattastrīdhanāḥ patnyo ‘pi putrasamāṃśabhājaḥ karyāḥ | datte tu strīdhane, “datte tv ardhaṃ prakalpayet” (YDh 2.148) [488] iti, putrāṃśād ardhāṃśabhājo bhavanti | pitur ūrdhvaṃ dharmavivṛddhyarthaṃ vibhāgaḥ kartavya ity āha prajāpatiḥ |
evaṃ saha vaseyur vā pṛthag vā dharmakāmyayā |
pṛthag vivardhate dharmas tasmād dharmyā pṛthakkriyā || iti | (MDh 9.111)
bṛhaspatir api |
ekapākena vasatāṃ pitṛdevadvijārcanam |
ekaṃ bhaved vibhaktānāṃ tad eva syād gṛhe gṛhe || iti |
pitror ūrdhvaṃ vibhāge prakāraniyamam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[489]        vibhajeyuḥ sutāḥ pitror ūrdhvam ṛktham ṛṇaṃ samam || iti | (YDh 2.117)
nanu – pitror ūrdhvaṃ vibhāge ‘pi viṣamavibhāgo manunā darśitaḥ, “ūrdhvaṃ pituś ca mātuś ca” (MDh 9.104) ity upakramya,
jyeṣṭha eva tu gṛhṇīyt pitryaṃ dhanam aśeṣataḥ |
śeṣās tam upajīveyur yathaiva pitaraṃ tathā ||
jyeṣṭhasya viṃśa uddhāraḥ sarvadravyāc ca yad varam |
tato ‘rdhaṃ madhyamasya syāt turīyaṃ tu yavīyasaḥ || iti |
tathā –
uddhāre ‘nuddhṛte teṣām iyaṃ syād aṃśakalpanā |
ekādhikaṃ harej jyeṣṭhaḥ putro ‘dhyarthaṃ tato ‘nujaḥ ||
aṃśam aṃśaṃ yavīyāṃsa iti dharma vyavasthitaḥ || iti | (MDh 9.105, 112, 116–27)
gautamo ‘pi: “viṃśatibhāgo jyeṣṭhasya mithunam ubhayatodadyukto ratho govṛṣaḥ | [490] kāṇaḥ khoḍaḥ kūṭaḥ vaṇḍo madhyamasyānekaś cet | avir dhānyāyasī gṛham anoyuktaṃ catuṣpadāṃ caikaikaṃ yavīyasaḥ | samaṃ cetarat sarvam” iti (GDh 28.5–8) | ayam arthaḥ | sarvasmāt pitṛdhanād viṃśatitamo bhāgo jyeṣṭhasya | mithunaṃ gomithunaṃ prasiddham | ubhayatodato ‘śvāśvataragardabhāḥ teṣāṃ yathāsaṃbhavam anyatarābhyāṃ yukto rathaḥ | khoḍo vṛddhaḥ | kūṭaḥ śṛṅgavikalaḥ | vaṇḍo vilopitavāladhiḥ | aviśeṣitatvā gavāśvadīnāṃ yathāsaṃbhavam anyatarasyoddhāraḥ kartavyo madhyamasya | yavīyasas tu dhānyaṃ vrīhyādi ayo loham | anoyuktaṃ śakaṭayuktam | catuṣpadāṃ gavādīnām ekaikaṃ pṛthak pṛthag evānupūrvyeṇa yavīyasa uddhāraḥ | bṛhaspatir api |
janmavidyāguṇair jyeṣṭho dvyaṃśaṃ dāyādam āpnuyāt | iti |
kātyāyano ‘pi |
yathā yathā vibhāgāptaṃ dhanaṃ yāgārthatām iyāt |
tathā tathā vidhātavyaṃ tadvidbhir bhāgagauravam ||
[491] iti | jīvadvibhāge ‘pi viṣamavibhāgo nāradenoktaḥ |
pitaiva vā svayaṃ putrān vibhajed vayasi sthitaḥ |
jyeṣṭhaṃ śreṣṭhavibhāgena yathā vāsya matir bhavet ||
pitraiva tu vibhaktā ye samanyūnādhikair dhanair |
teṣāṃ sa eva dharmaḥ syāt sarvasya hi pitā prabhuḥ |
dvāv aṃśau pratipadyeta vibhajann ātmanaḥ pitā ||
[492] iti | bṛhaspatir api |
samanyūnādhikā bhāgāḥ pitrā yeṣāṃ prakalpitāḥ |
tathaiva te pālanīyā vineyās te syur anyathā || iti |
tasmāj jīvadvibhāge ajīvadvibhāge ca viṣamavibhāgo ‘stīti kathaṃ sutāḥ samam eva vibhajerann iti niyamyate |
maivam | satyaṃ śāstrato viṣamavibhāgo ‘sti tathāpi lokavidviṣṭatvād anubandhyādivat nānuṣṭhīyate | uktaṃ ca saṃgrahakāreṇa |
yathā niyogadharmo ‘yaṃ nānubandhyāvadho ‘pi vā |
tathoddāravibhāgo ‘pi naiva saṃprati vartate ||
[493] iti | āpastambo ‘pi: “jīvan putrebhyo dāyaṃ vibhajet samam” (ĀpDh 2.6.14.1) iti svamatam upanyasya, “jyeṣṭho dāyāda ity eke” (ĀpDh 2.6.14.6) ity ekīyamatena kṛtsnadhanagrahaṇaṃ jyeṣṭhasyopanyasya, “deśaviśeṣe suvarṇaṃ kṛṣṇā gāvaḥ kṛṣṇaṃ bhaumaṃ jyeṣṭhasya | rathaḥ pituḥ parībhāṇḍaṃ ca gṛhe | alaṃkāro bhāryāyā jñātidhanaṃ cety eke” (ĀpDh 2.6.14.7–9) ity ekīyamatenaivoddhāravibhāgaṃ darśayitvā, “tac chāstravipratiṣiddham” (ĀpDh 2.6.14.10) iti nirākṛtavān; taṃ ca śāstrapratiṣedhaṃ svayam eva darśitavān: “manuḥ putrebhyo dāyaṃ vyabhajad ity aviśeṣeṇa śrūyate” (ĀpDh 2.6.14.11) [494] iti | tasmād viṣamavibhāgaḥ śāstrasiddho ‘pi lokavirodhāc chrutivirodhāc ca nānuṣṭheyaḥ | iti samam eva vibhajetann iti nyamo ghaṭate |
svayaṃ dravyārjanasamarthatayā pitṛdravyam anicchato ‘pi yat kiṃcid datvā dāyavibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ tatputrādīnāṃ dāyagrahaṇecchānivṛtyartham ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
śaktasyānīhamānasya kiṃcid datvā pṛthak kriyā | iti | (YDh 2.116)
putrāṇāṃ mātṛdhanavibhāgo duhitrabhāve draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca sa eva |
mātur duhitaraḥ śeṣam ṛṇāt tābhya ṛte ‘nvayaḥ | iti | (YDh 2.117)
mātṛkṛtarṇāpākaraṇāvaśiṣṭaṃ mātṛdhanaṃ duhitaro vibhajeran | ataś ca mātṛkṛtarṇasamaṃ nyūnaṃ ca mātṛdhanaṃ duhitṝṇāṃ sadbhāve ‘pi putrā eva vibhajeran – ity arthād avagamyate | atra gautamena viśeṣo darśitaḥ: [495] “strīdhanaṃ duhitṝṇām aprattānām apratiṣṭhitānāṃ ca” iti (GDh 28.24) | ūḍhānūḍhaduhitṛsamavāye matṛdhanam anūḍhānām eva | ūḍhāsv api sadhananirdhanaduhitṛsamavāye nirdhanānām evety arthaḥ | paitāmahe pautrāṇāṃ vibhāge viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
anekapitṛkāṇāṃ tu pitṛto bhāgakalpanā | iti | (YDh 2.120)
yadā pitṛtaḥ avibhaktā bhrātarah putrān utpādya mṛtāḥ tatraikasya dvau putrau anyasya trayaḥ aparasya catvāraḥ | tatra pautrāṇāṃ paitāmahe dravye yady api janmanaiva svatvaṃ putrair aviśiṣṭaṃ tathāpi pitryaṃśaṃ dvāv ekaṃ trayo ‘py ekaṃ catvāro ‘py ekaṃ labhante ity arthaḥ | etad evābhipretya bṛhaspatir api |
tatputrā viṣamasamāḥ pitṛbhāgaharāḥ smṛtāḥ | iti |
tatputrāḥ pramītapitṛkāṇām ekaikasya putrāḥ viṣamasamāḥ nyūnādhikasaṃkhyāḥ svaṃ svaṃ paitṛkaṃ bhāgam eva labhante ity arthaḥ | yadā svasutayor avibhaktayor madhye kaścid bhrātā mṛtaḥ tatsutas tu pitāmahād apy aprāptāṃśaḥ pitāmaho ‘pi nāsīt tadā tv āha kātyāyanaḥ |
avibhakte ‘nuje prete tatsutam ṛkthabhājinam |
kurvīta jīvanaṃ yena labdhaṃ naiva pitāmahāt ||
[496]         labhetāṃśaṃ sa pitryaṃ tu pitṛvyāt tasya vā sutāt |
sa evāṃśas tu sarveṣāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ nyāyato bhavet ||
labheta tatsuto vāpi nivṛttiḥ parato bhavet || iti |
labheta tatsuto vā ity asya ayam arthaḥ | tasyāpi vibhājyadhanasvānipautrasya suto ‘pi pitur abhāve tadbhāgaṃ labheta tata ūrdhvaṃ tatsantatau vṛddhaprapitāmahadhanavibhāgakaraṇanivṛttiḥ iti | tathā ca devalaḥ |
avibhaktavibhaktānāṃ kulyānāṃ vasatāṃ saha |
bhūyo dāyavibhāgaḥ syād ā caturthād iti sthitiḥ ||
tāvat kulyāḥ sapiṇḍāḥ syuḥ piṇḍabhedas tataḥ param || iti |
jīvatpitṛkasya punaḥ pitrā saha kathaṃ paitāmahadhanavibhāga ity ākāṅkṣāyām āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
dravye pitāmahopātte jaṅgame sthāvare ‘pi vā |
samam aṃśitvam ākhyātaṃ pituḥ putrasya caiva hi || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
bhūr yā pitāmahopāttā nibandho dravyam eva vā |
tatra syāt sadṛśaṃ svāmyaṃ pituḥ putrasya cobhayoḥ || iti | (YDh 2.121)
bhūḥ śālikṣetrādikā | nibandhaḥ ekasya parṇabhārasya iyanti parṇāni tathaikasya kramukaphalabhārasya iyanti [497] kramukaphalānityādyuktalakṣaṇaḥ | dravyaṃ suvarṇarajatādi yat pitāmanhena pratigrahavijayādilabdhaṃ tatra pituḥ putrasya ca svāmyaṃ lokaprasiddham iti vibhāgo ‘sti | yasmāt sadṛśaṃ samānaṃ svāmyaṃ tasmān na pitur icchayaiva vibhāgo nāpi pitur bhāgadvayam | tataś ca pitṛto bhāgakalpanety etatsvāmye samāne ‘pi vācanikam | ataḥ,
dvāviṃśau pratipadyeta vibhajann ātmanaḥ pitā | iti |
