evamādikaṃ yugāntare viṣamavibhāgapratipādanatayā sthāpitam | svārjitadravyaviṣayaṃ vā | paitāmahadhanaviṣaye tu na kvāpi viṣamavibhāgaḥ iti | tathā avibhaktena pitrā paitāmahe dravye dīyamāne vikrīyamāṇe vā pautrasya niṣedhe ‘py adhikāro ‘stīti gamyate | [498] paitāmahopātte ‘pi kvacit pitur icchayaiva svārjitavad vibhāgo bhavatīty āha manuḥ |
paitṛkaṃ tu pitā dravyam anavāptaṃ yad āpnuyāt |
na tatputrair bhajet sārdham akāmaḥ svayam arjitam || iti | (MDh 9.209)
yat pitāmahārjitaṃ kenāpy apahṛtaṃ yadi pitoddharati tadā svārjitam iva putraiḥ sardham akāmataḥ svayaṃ na vibhajet  iti | evaṃ ca sati pitāmahopārjite na svecchayā vibhāga ity uktaṃ bhavati | bṛhaspatir api |
paitāmahaṃ hṛtaṃ pitrā svaśaktyā yad upārjitam |
vidyāśauryādinā prāptaṃ tatra svāmyaṃ pituḥ smṛtam || iti |
kātyāyano ‘pi |
svaśaktyāpahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ svayam āptaṃ ca yad bhavet |
etat sarvaṃ pitā putrair vibhāgaṃ naiva dāpyate || iti |
yat parair apahṛtaṃ kramāyātaṃ svaśaktyaivoddhṛtaṃ yan naṣṭaṃ kramāyātaṃ yac ca vidyāśauryādinā svayam evārjitaṃ tat sarvaṃ pitā vibhāge putrair na dāpya ity arthaḥ | vibhāgottarakālotpannasya putrasya bhāgakalpanāprakāram āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[499]         vibhakteṣu suto jātaḥ savarṇāyāṃ vibhāgabhāk | iti | (YDh 2.122)
ayam arthaḥ | vibhakteṣu putreṣu savarṇāyāṃ bhāryāyāṃ jātaḥ putraḥ pitror vibhāgaṃ bhajate iti vibhāgabhāk – iti, mātṛbhāgaṃ cāsatyāṃ duhitari “tābhya ṛte ‘nvayaḥ” (YDh 2.117) ity uktatvāt | asavarṇāyāṃ jātas tu svāṃśam eva pitryāl labhate mātṛkaṃ tu sarvam eva | ata eva manuḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ vibhāgāj jātas tu pitryam eva hard dhanam | iti | (MDh 9.216)
pitror idaṃ pitryam,
anīśāḥ pūvajāḥ pitroḥ bhrātṛbhāge vibhaktajāḥ |
iti smaraṇāt | mātāpitror bhāge vibhāgāt pūrvam utpanno na svāmī pitrā saha pūrvaṃ vibhaktatvāt | vibhaktajaś ca bhrātur dhane na svāmīty arthaḥ | vibhāgottarakālaṃ pitrā svayam arjitaṃ vibhāgottarakālam utpannasyaiva | tathā ca manuḥ |
putraiḥ saha vibhaktena pitrā yat svayam arjitam |
vibhaktajasya tat sarvam anīśāḥ pūrvjāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
ye ca vibhaktāḥ punaḥ pitrā saha saṃsṛṣṭās teṣāṃ vibhāgottarakālam utpannena saha vibhāgo ‘stīty āha manuḥ |
[500]         saṃsṛṣṭās tena vā ye syur vibhajeta sa taiḥ saha | iti | (MDh 9.216)
ajīvadvibhāgottarakālaṃ jātasya putrasya bhāgakalpanām āha yājñavakyaḥ |
dṛśyād vā tadvibhāgaḥ syād āyavyayaviśodhitāt | iti | (YDh 2.122)
pitari mṛte bhrātṛvibhāgasamaye ‘spaṣṭagarbhāyāṃ mātari bhrātṛvibhāgottarakālam utpannasya vibhāgaḥ dṛśyād bhrātṛbhir gṛhītād āyavyayaviśodhitāt upacayāpacayābhyām avadhāritaparimāṇāt śodhitād dhanāt kiṃcid uddhṛtya svāṃśasamo dātavyaḥ syād ity arthaḥ |
[501]        etac ca mṛtabhrātṛbhāryāyām api vibhāgasamaye aspaṣṭagarbhāyāṃ vibhāgād ūrdhvam utpannasyāpi veditavyam | spaṣṭagarbhāyāṃ tu prasavaṃ pratīkṣyaiva vibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ, “atha brātṝṇāṃ dāyavibhāgo yāś cānapatyāḥ striyas tāsām ā putralābhāt” iti vasiṣṭasmaraṇāt (VaDh 17.40–41) | vibhaktebhyaḥ pitṛbhyām arthadāne vibhaktajasya putrasya niṣedhādhikāro nāsti dattaṃ ca tena na pratyāhartavyam ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitṛbhyāṃ yasya yad dattaṃ tat tasyaiva dhanaṃ bhavet | iti | (YDh 2.123)
ajīvadvibhāge mātur aṃśakalpanām āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhajatāṃ mātāpy aṃśaṃ samaṃ haret | iti | (YDh 2.123)
etac ca strīdhanasya apradāne veditavyam | datte tv ardham eva, [502] “datte tv ardhāṃśahāriṇī” (YDh 2.148) iti smaraṇāt | ata eva smṛtyantaram |
janany asvadhanā putrair vibhāge ‘ṃśaṃ samaṃ haret | iti |
asvadhanā prātisvikastrīdhanaśūnyā jananī putrair vibhāge kriyamāṇe putrāṃśasamam aṃśaṃ hared ity arthaḥ | jananīgrahaṇaṃ sāpatnyāder upalakṣaṇārtham | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
asutās tu pituḥ patnyaḥ samānāṃśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
pitāmahyaś ca sarvās tā mātṛtulyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || iti |
yat tu kaiścid uktaṃ mātāpy aṃśaṃ samaṃ hard iti jīvanopayuktam eva dhanaṃ mātā svīkaroti iti,
tan na, aṃśasamaśabdayor ānarthakyaprasaṅgāt |
athocyeta bahadhane jīvanopayuktaṃ gṛhṇāti svalpadhane putrasamāṃśam iti |
tad api na, vidhivaiṣamyaprasaṅgāt | bhinnamātṛkāṇāṃ savarṇānāṃ samasaṃkhyānāṃ vibhāgaprakāram āha vyāsaḥ |
[503]        samānajātisaṃkhyā ye jātās tv ekena sūnavaḥ |
vibhinnamātṛkās teṣāṃ mātṛbhāgaḥ praśasyate || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
yady ekajātā bahavaḥ samānā jātisaṃkhyāyā|
svadhanais tair vibhaktavyaṃ mātṛbhāgena dharmataḥ || iti |
viṣamasaṃkhyānāṃ tu vibhāgaṃ sa evāha |
savarṇaliṅgasaṃkhyā ye vibhāgas teṣu śasyate | iti |
vibhinnajātīnāṃ putrāṇāṃ vibhāgaprakāram āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
catustridvyekabhāgāḥ syur varṇaśo brāhmaṇātmajāḥ |
kṣatrajātis tridvyekabhāgā viḍjās tu dvyekabhāginaḥ || (YDh 2.125)
[504] iti | varṇaśo brāhmaṇātmajāḥ brāhmaṇādivarṇastrīṣu brāhmaṇenotpannā brāhmaṇamūrdhāvasiktāmbaṣṭhaniṣādāḥ | yathākramaṃ pratyekaṃ catustridvyekabhāgā bhaveyuḥ | kṣatriyādivarṇastrīṣu kṣatriyeṇotpannāḥ kṣatriyamāhiṣyogrās tridvyekabhāgāḥ vaiśyena vaiśyāśūdrotpannau vaiśyakaraṇau dvyekabhāginau śūdrasaikaiva bhāryeti bhinnajātīyaputrābhāvāt tatputrāṇāṃ pūrvokta eka eva vibhāgaḥ | manur api |
brāhmaṇasyānupūrvyeṇa catasras tu yadi striyaḥ |
tāsāṃ putreṣu jāteṣu vibhāge ‘yaṃ vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
[505]         tryaṃśaṃ dāyād dhared vipro dvāv aṃśau kṣatryāsutaḥ |
vaiśyājaḥ sārdham evāṃśam aṃśaṃ śūdrāsuto haret ||
sarvaṃ vā ṛkthajātaṃ tad daśadhā pravibhajya tu |
dharmyaṃ vibhāgaṃ kurvīta vidhinānena dharmavit ||
caturo ‘ṃśān hared vipraḥ trīn aṃśān kṣtriyāsutaḥ |
vaiśyāsuto hared dvyaṃśam ekaṃ śūdrāsuto haret || iti | (MDh 9.149, 151–53)
etac ca pratigrahaprāptabhūmyatriktaviṣayam | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
na pratigrahabhūr deyā kṣatriyādisutāya vai |
yady apy eṣāṃ pitā dadyān mṛte viprāsuto haret || iti |
pratigrahaviśeṣaṇasāmarthyāt krayādilabdhā bhūḥ kṣatriyādisutānām api bhavaty eva śūdrāputrasya viśeṣapratiṣedhāc ca,
[506]        śūdryāṃ dvijātibhir jāto na bhūmer bhāgam arhati | iti |
yat tu manuvacanam,
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ śūdrāputro na ṛkthabhāk |
yad evāsya pitā dadyāt tad evāsya dhanaṃ bhavet || (MDh 9.155)
[507] iti, tat prītidattadhanasadbhāvaviṣayaṃ ity aviruddham | ānulomyena jātasyaikaputrasya ṛkthagrahaṇaprakāram āha devalaḥ |
ānulomy ekaputras tu sarvasvabhāg bhavet | iti |
etac ca niṣādavyatiriktaviṣayam | ata evoktaṃ tenaiva |
niṣāda ekaputras tu viprasvasya tṛtīyabhāk |
dvau sapiṇḍaḥ sakulyo vā svadhādātā tu svaṃ haret || iti |
yat tu manuvacana,
yady api syāt tu satputro yady aputro ‘pi vā bhavet |
nādhikaṃ daśamād dadyāc chūdrāputrāya dharmataḥ || (MDh 9.154)
iti, tad aśuśrūṣuśūdrāputraviṣayam | kṣatriyeṇa vaiśyena vā śūdrāyām utpannaḥ ekaḥ putraḥ aradham eva haret na niṣādavat tṛtīyam aṃśam | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: [508] “dvijātīnāṃ śaudras tv ekaḥ putro ‘rdhaharaḥ | aputrasya ṛkthasya yā gatiḥ sā bhāgārdhasya” iti (ViDh 18.32–33) | pratyāsannasapiṇḍasyānyad ardhaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | ajīvadvibhāge keṣucid bhrātṛṣv asaṃskṛteṣu bhaginīṣu vāsaṃskṛtāsu tatsaṃskāraḥ pūrvasaṃkṛtair bhrātṛbhiḥ kartavya ity āha vyāsaḥ |
asaṃskṛtās tu ye tatra paitṛkād eva te dhanāt |
saṃskāryā bhrātṛbhiḥ jyeṣṭhaiḥ kanyakāś ca yathāvidhi |
[509] iti | bhaginīsaṃskāre tu viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
asaṃskṛtās tu saṃskāryā bhrātṛbhiḥ pūrvasaṃskṛtaiḥ |
bhaginyaś ca nijād aṃśād datvā ty aṃśaṃ turīyakam || iti | (YDh 2.124)
pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhajadbhir bhrātṛbhir asaṃkṛtā bhrātaraḥ samudāyadravyeṇa saṃskartavyāḥ | bhaginyaś cāsaṃskṛtāḥ nijād aṃśād yajjātīyā kanyakā tajjātīyaputrabhāgāt turīyaṃ caturthaṃ bhāgaṃ datvā saṃskartavyāḥ | anena pitur ūrdhvaṃ duhitaro ‘py aṃśabhāginya ity gamyate | ata eva manuḥ |
svebhyo ‘ṃśebhyas tu kanyābhyaḥ svaṃ dadyur bhrātaraḥ pṛthak |
svāt svād aṃśāc caturbhāgaṃ patitāḥ syur aditsavaḥ || iti | (MDh 9.118)
brāhmaṇādayo bhrātaraḥ brāhmaṇyādibhyo bhaginībhyo svajātivihitebhyo ‘ṃśebhyaḥ svāt svād aṃśād ātmīyād bhāgāc caturthaṃ caturthaṃ bhāgaṃ dadyuḥ |
etad uktaṃ bhavati | yadi kasyacid brāhmaṇy eva patnī putraś caikaḥ kanyā caikā tatra pitryaṃ dravyaṃ dvedhā vibhajya tatraikaṃ bhāgaṃ caturdhā vibhajya turīyāṃśaṃ kanyāyai datvā śeṣaṃ putro gṛhṇīyāt | atha dvau putrau kanyā caikā tadā pitṛdhanaṃ tredhā vibhajya tatraikaṃ bhāgaṃ caturdhā vibhajya turīyāṃśaṃ kanyāyai datvā śeṣaṃ dvau putrau vibhajya gṛhṇītaḥ | atha tv ekaḥ putraḥ dve kanye tadā pitryaṃ [510] dhanaṃ tridhā vibhajya tatraikaṃ bhāgaṃ caturdhā vibhajya dvau bhāgau dvābhyāṃ kanyābhyāṃ datvāvaśiṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ putro gṛhṇāti | evaṃ samānajātīyeṣu samaviṣameṣu bhrātṛṣu bhaginīṣu ca samaviṣamāsu yojanīyam |
yadā tu brāhmaṇīputra ekaḥ kṣatriyākanyā caikā tatra pitryaṃ dravyaṃ saptadhā vibhajya kṣatriyāputrabhāgān trīn caturdhā vibhajya turīyāṃśaṃ kṣatriyākanyāyai datvā  śeṣaṃ brāhmaṇīputro gṛhṇāti | yadā tu dvau brāhmaṇīputrau kṣatriyākanyaikā tatra pitryaṃ dhanam ekādaśadhā vibhajya teṣu trīn bhāgān caturdhā vibhajya caturtham aṃśaṃ kṣatriyākanyāyai datvā śeṣaṃ sarvaṃ brāhmaṇīputrau vibhajya gṛhṇīyātām |
evaṃ jātivaiṣamye bhrātṝṇāṃ bhaginīnāṃ ca saṃkhyayā vaiṣamye ca sarvatrāyaṃ niyama iti medhātither viyākyānam | etad eva vijñāneśvarayogino ‘py abhipretam | bhārucis tu caturbhāgapadena vivāhasaṃskāramātropayogi dravyaṃ vivakṣitam | ato dāyabhāktvam asaṃskṛtakanyānāṃ nāstīti manyate | etad eva candrikākārasyāpy abhipretam tad āha: “yata eva na dāyabhāgārtham ṃśaharaṇaṃ kiṃ tu vivāhasaṃskārārtham” | ata eva devalenoktam |
kanyābhyaś ca pitṛdravyaṃ deyaṃ vaivāhikaṃ vasu |
[511] iti | atra yad yuktiyuktaṃ tad grāhyam | jīvadvibhāge tu yat kiṃcit pitā dadāti tad eva labhate kanyāviśeṣāśravaṇāt | pitryadhanābhāve nāradaḥ |
avidyamāne pitrye ‘rthe svāṃśād uddhṛtya vā punaḥ |
avaśyakāryāḥ saṃskārāḥ saṅkoco ‘tra vivakṣitaḥ || iti |
bhrātṝṇāṃ bhaginīnāṃ ca saṃskārāḥ jātakarmādyāḥ pūrvasaṃskṛtaiḥ bhrātṛbhiḥ pitṛdhanābhāve ‘pi sāvaśyakatvena kartavyāḥ | paitṛkadravyavibhāgakāle svadhṛtālaṃkārādikam iti kanyakā prāpnotīty āha śaṅkhaḥ: “vibhajyamāne dāye kanyālaṃkāram eva hi | vaivāhikaṃ strīdhanaṃ ca labhate” iti | mukhyagauṇaputrāṇāṃ svarūpaṃ dāyagrahaṇakramaṃ cāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[512]         auraso dharmapatnījaḥ tatsamaḥ putrikāsutaḥ |
kṣetrajaḥ kṣetrajātas tu sagotreṇetareṇa vā ||
gṛhe pracchanna utpanno gūḍhajas tu sutaḥ smṛtaḥ |
kānīnaḥ kanyakājāto mātāmahasuto mataḥ ||
[513]         akṣatāyāṃ kṣatāyāṃ vā jātaḥ paunarbhavaḥ smṛtaḥ |
dadyān mātā pitā vā yaṃ sa putro dattako bhavet ||
krītaś ca tābhyāṃ vikrītaḥ kṛtrimaḥ syāt svayaṃkṛtaḥ |
dattātmā tu svayaṃdatto garbhe vinnaḥ sahoḍhajaḥ ||
utsṛṣṭo gṛhyate yas tu so ‘pagiddho bhavet sutaḥ |
piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś caiṣāṃ pūrvābhāve paraḥ paraḥ || (YDh 2.128–32)
[514] iti | eṣāṃ putrāṇāṃ dvādaśānāṃ purvasya pūrvasyābhāve uttara uttaraḥ piṇḍadaḥ śrāddhadaḥ aṃśaharo dhanahara ity arthaḥ | aurasaputrikayoḥ samavāye aurasasyaiva dhanaharaṇe prāpte apavādam āha manuḥ |
putrikāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ tu yadi putro ‘nu jāyate |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syāj jyeṣṭhatā nāsti hi striyāḥ || iti | (MDh 9.134)
vasiṣṭho ‘pi kvacid apavādam āha: [515] “tasmiṃś cet pratigṛhīte aurasa utpadyeta | caturthabhāgabhāgī syād dattakaḥ” iti (VaDh 15.9) | kātyāyano ‘pi |
utpanne tv aurase putre caturthāṃśaharāh sutāḥ |
savarṇā asavarṇā vā grāsācchādanabhājanāḥ || iti |
savarṇāḥ kṣetrajadattakādayaḥ | te aurase saty api caturthāṃśaharāḥ | asavarṇāḥ kānīnagūḍhotpannasahoḍhapaunarbhavāḥ aurase sati na caturthāṃśaharāḥ api tu grāsācchādanam eva labhante ity arthaḥ | yat tu viṣṇunoktam,
apraśastās tu kānīnagūḍhotpannasahoḍhajāḥ |
paunarbhavaś ca te naiva piṇḍaṛkthāṃśabhāginaḥ || iti |
tadaurase sati caturthāṃśaniṣedhanaparam eva | yac ca manunoktam,
eka evaurasaḥ putraḥ pitryasya vasunaḥ prabhuḥ |
śeṣāṇām ānṛśaṃsyārthaṃ pradadyāt tu prajīvanam || (MDh 9.163))
[516] iti, tad aurasapraśaṃsāparam eva na caturthāṃśabhāganiṣedhaparam | anyathā caturthāṃśabhāktvapratipādakavasiṣṭhakātyāyanavacanayor ānarthakyaprasaṅgāt | yad api tenaivoktam,
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu kṣetrajasyāṃśaṃ pradadyāt paitṛkād dhanāt |
auraso vibhajan dāyaṃ pitryaṃ pañcamam eva vā || iti | (MDh 9.164)
tatreyaṃ vyavasthā | atyantaguṇavattve caturthāṃśabhāgitvaṃ pratikūlatvanirguṇatvayoḥ ṣaṣṭhāṃśabhāgitvaṃ pratikūlatvamātre nirguṇatvamātre ca pañcamāṃśabhāgitvam iti | yad api hārītenoktam: [517] “vibhajiṣyamāṇa ekaviṃśaṃ kānīnāya dadyāt | viṃśaṃ paunarbhavāya | ekonaviṃśaṃ dvyāmuṣyāyaṇāya | aṣṭādaśāṃśaṃ ca kṣetrajāya | saptadaśāṃśaṃ putrikāputrāya | itarad aurasāya putrāya dadyāt” iti, etad asavarṇanirguṇaputraviṣayam | yat tu manunā,
aurasaḥ kṣetrajaś caiva dattaḥ kṛtrima eva ca |
gūḍhotpanno ‘paviddhaś ca dāyādā bāndhavāś ca ṣaṭ ||
kānīnaś ca sahoḍhaś ca krītaḥ paunarbhavas tathā |
svayaṃdattaś ca śaudraś ca ṣaḍ adāyādabāndhavāḥ || (MDh 9.159–60)
iti ṣaṭkadvayam abhidhāya pūrvaṣaṭkasya dāyādabāndhavatvaṃ uttaraṣaṛkasyādāyādabāndhavatvam abhihitam, tad api svapitṛsāpiṇḍye samānodakānāṃ saṃnihitarikthaharāntarābhāve pūrvaṣaṭkasya tadrikthabhāgaharatvam uttaraṣaṭkasy tu tan nāsti bhāndavatvaṃ punaḥ [518] samānagotratvena sapiṇḍatvena vā udakapradānādikāryakaratvṃ ṣaṭkadvayasyāpi samam eveti vyākhyeyam | pitṛdhanagrahaṇaṃ tu pūrvasya pūrvasyābhāve sarveṣām asty eva,
na bhrātaro na pitaraḥ putrā ṛkthaharāḥ pituḥ | (MDh 9.185)
iti aurasavyatiriktānāṃ putrapratinidhīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ ṛkthahāritvasya manunaiva pratipāditatvāt | dvyāmuṣyāyaṇas tu janayitur api ṛkthaṃ bhajate | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
aputreṇa parakṣetre niyogotpāditaḥ sutaḥ |
ubhayor apy asau ṛkthī piṇḍadātā ca dharmataḥ || iti | (YDh 2.127)
yadā gurvādinā niyukto devarādiḥ svayam apy aputraḥ sann aputrasya kṣetre svaparaputrārthaṃ pravṛtto yaṃ janayati sa dvipitṛko dvyāmuṣyāyaṇo dvayor api ṛkthahārī piṇḍadaḥ | yadā svayaṃ putravān paraputrārtham eva parakṣetre putram utpādayati tadutpannaḥ kṣetriṇa eva putro bhavati na bījinaḥ | yathoktaṃ manunā |
kriyābhyupagamāt kṣetraṃ bījārthaṃ yat pradīyate |
tasyeha bhāginau dṛṣṭau bījī kṣetrika eva ca ||
phalaṃ tv anabhisaṃdhāya kṣetrinā bījinā tathā |
pratyakṣaṃ kṣetriṇām artho bījād yonir balīyasī || (MDh 9.53, 52)
[519] iti | asyārthaḥ | atrotpannam apatyam ubhayor api bhavatu – iti saṃvidaṃ kṛtvā yat kṣetraṃ svāminā bījāvāpārthaṃ bījine dīyate tasmin kṣetre utpannasyāpatyasya bījikṣetriṇau svāminau | yadā tu tatrotpannam apatyam avayor astv iti saṃvidam akṛtvā parakṣetre bīninā yad apatyam utpādyate tadapatyaṃ kṣetriṇa eva na bījinaḥ | yato bījād yonir balīyasī | gavāśvādiṣu dṛṣṭatvād ity arthaḥ | gurvādiniyogo ‘pi vāgdattāviṣaya eva, anyasya niyogasya manunā niṣiddhatvāt:
devarād vā sapiṇḍād vā striyā samyaṅ niyuktayā |
prajepsitādhigantavyā santānasya parikṣaye ||
vidhavāyāṃ niyuktas tu ghṛtākto vāgyato niśi |
ekam utpādayet putraṃ na dvitīyaṃ kathaṃcana || (MDh 9.59–60)
putre niyogād utpanne yathāvad vidhavaiva sā || iti |
evaṃ niyogam upanyasya manuḥ svayam eva niṣedhati |
nānyasmin vidhavā nārī niyoktavyā dvijātibhiḥ |
anyasmin hi niyuñjānā dharmaṃ hanyuḥ sanātanam ||
nodvāhikeṣu mantreṣu niyogaḥ kīrtyate kvacit |
na vivāhavidhāv uktaṃ vidhavāvedanaṃ punaḥ ||
ayaṃ dvijair hi vidvadbhiḥ paśudharmo vigarhitaḥ |
manuṣyāṇām api prokto vene rājyaṃ praśāsati ||
[520]         sa mahīm akhilāṃ bhuñjan rājarṣipravaraḥ purā |
varṇānāṃ saṃkaraṃ cakre kāmopahatacetanaḥ ||
tataḥ prabhṛti yo mohāt pramītapatikāṃ striyam |
niyogayaty apatyārthaṃ taṃ vigarhanti sādhavaḥ || (MDh 9.64–68)
[521] iti |
nanu – atra vikalpo ‘stu vidhipratiṣedhayor ubhayor darśanāt | ato viniyogasya vāgdattādiviṣyatvam anupapannam iti cet |
na, manunaiva niyogasya tadviṣayatvapratipādanāt |
yasyā mriyeta kanyāyā vācā satye kṛte patiḥ |
tām anena vidhānena nijo vindeta devaraḥ ||
yathāvidhy adhigamyaināṃ śuklavastrāṃ śucivratām |
mitho bhajetāprsavāt sakṛt sakṛd ṛtāv ṛtau || iti | (MDh 9.69–70)
dattakādīnāṃ na bījiṛktabhāktvam | tathā ca manuḥ |
[522]         gotraṛkthe janayitur na bhajed dattrimaḥ sutaḥ |
gotraṛkthānugaḥ piṇḍo vyapaiti dadataḥ svadhā || iti | (MDh 9.142)
dattrimagrahaṇaṃ krītopalakṣaṇārtham | dattavyatiriktānāṃ gauṇaputrāṇām ṛkthabhāktvapratipādakāni vākyāni yugāntaraviṣayāṇi, kalau yuge teṣāṃ putratvena parigrahaṇasya smṛtyantare niṣiddhatvāt |
dattaurasetareṣāṃ tu putratvena parigrahaḥ |
devareṇa sutotpattiḥ vānaprasthāśramagrahaḥ ||
kalau yuge tv imān dharmān varjyān āhur manīṣiṇaḥ || iti |
śūdradhanavibhāge viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
jāto ‘pi dāsyāṃ śūdreṇa kāmato ‘ṃśaharo bhavet |
mṛte pitari kuryus taṃ bhrātaras tv ardhabhāgikam ||
abhrātṛko haret sarvaṃ duhitṝṇāṃ sutād ṛte || (YDh 2.133–34)
[523] iti | kāmataḥ pitur icchayā bhāgaṃ labhate | mṛte pitari yadi pariṇītāputrā bhrātaraḥ santi tadā te dāsīputraṃ svabhāgād ardhabhāgikaṃ kuryuḥ | atha pariṇītāputrā duhitaro vā tatputrā vā na santi tadā taddhanaṃ dāsīputro labhate | tatsadbhāve ardham eva | dvijātīnāṃ dāsyām utpannas tu pitur icchayāpy aṃśaṃ na labhate nāpy ardhaṃ dūrata eva kṛtsnaṃ jāto ‘pi dāsyāṃ śūdreṇeti viśeṣaṇāt | kiṃ tv anukūlaś cej jīvanamātraṃ labhate ity abhiprāyaḥ | aputradāyagrahaṇakramam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
patnī duhitaraś caiva pitarau bhrātaras tathā |
tatsuto gotrajo bandhuḥ śiṣyaḥ sabrahmacāriṇaḥ ||
eṣām abhāve pūrvasya dhanabhāg uttarottaraḥ |
svaryātasya hy aputrasya sarvavarṇeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti | (YDh 2.135–36)
aurasādayo dvādaśavidhaputrā yasya na santy asāv aputraḥ | tasya mṛtasya dhanaṃ patnyādīnāṃ pūrvasya pūrvasyābhāve uttarottaro gṛhṇāti | ayaṃ dāyagrahaṇakramaḥ sarveṣu mūrdhāvasiktādiṣv anulomajeṣu varṇeṣu ca brāhmaṇādiṣu veditavya ity arthaḥ | patnī vivāhādisaṃskrtā nārī | sā prathamaṃ patyur dhanaṃ gṛhṇāti | tad āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
kulyeṣu vidyamāneṣu pitṛbhrātṛsanābhiṣu |
asutasya pramītasya patnī taddhanahāriṇī || iti |
atra viśeṣam āha vṛddhamanuḥ |
[524]        aputrā śayanaṃ bhartuḥ pālayantī vrate sthitā |
patny eva dadyāt tat piṇḍaṃ kṛtsnam aṃśaṃ labheta ca || iti |
kṛtsnam aṃśaṃ jaṅgamasthāvarātmakam | tad ayam aputradāyagrahaṇakramaḥ | dvādaśavidhaputraśūnyasya mṛtasya dhanaṃ patnī gṛhṇāti | tathā ca prajāpatiḥ |
jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ hema kupyaṃ dhānyam athāmbaram |
ādāya dāpayec chrāddhaṃ māsasaṃvatsarādikam ||
pitṛvyagurudauhitrān bhartṛśvaśuramātulān |
pūjayet kavyapūrtābhyāṃ vṛddhān apy atithīṃs tathā || iti |
tadabhāve duhitaraḥ samānajātīyā asamānajātīyāś ca yathābhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhartur dhanaharī patnī tāṃ vinā duhitā smṛtā |
aṅgād aṅgāt saṃbhavati putravad duhitā nṛṇām ||
sadṛśī sadṛśenoḍhā sādhvī śuśrūṣaṇe ratā |
tasmāt pitṛdhanaṃ tv anyaḥ kathaṃ gṛhṇīta mānavaḥ || iti |
ūḍhānūḍhaduhitṛsamavāye kātyāyana āha |
patnī patyur dhanaharī yā syād avyabhicāriṇī |
tadabhāve tu duhitā yady anūḍhā bhavet tadā ||
[525] iti | ūḍhāsv api pratiṣṭhitāpratiṣṭhitaduhitṛsamavāye apratiṣṭhitaiva gṛhṇāti | tad āha gautamaḥ: “strīdhanaṃ duhitṝṇām aprattānām apratiṣṭhitānāṃ ca” iti (GDh 28.24) | na caitad gautamavacanaṃ mātṛdhanaviṣayam eveti mantavyam, pitṛdhane ‘py asya samānatvāt | duhitrabhāve dauhitro dhanahārī | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ |
aputrapautrasaṃtāne dauhitrā dhanam āpnuyuḥ |
pūrveṣāṃ tu svadhākāre pautrā dauhitrikā matā || iti |
manur api |
akṛtā vā kṛtā vāpi yaṃ vindet sadṛśāt sutam |
pautrī mātāmahas tena dadyāt piṇḍaṃ hared dhanam || (MDh 9.136)
[526] iti | na ca – duhitranantaraṃ pitarāv ity upādānam anupapannam, dauhitrasya dhanahāritvena vaktavyatvād iti mantavyam | duhitaraś ceti caśabdena sumuccayāt dauhitrābhāve pitarau dhanabhājau | yady apy atra pitror dhanagrahaṇe kramo na śrūyate tathāpi pitarāv ity asya vigrahavākye mātṛśabdasya pūrvanipātāt pratyāsattyatiśayāc ca mātur eva pūrvaṃ dhanagrahaṇaṃ yuktam iti kecid āhuḥ | apare tv aputradhanaṃ patnyabhigāmi tadabhāve duhitṛgāmi tadabhāve pitṛgāmi tadabhāve mātṛgāmi iti, vṛddhaviṣṇuvacanāt,
aputrasyātha kulajā patnī duhitaro ‘pi ca |
tadabhāve pitā mātā bhrātā putraś ca kīrtitā ||
iti, kātyāyanavacanāc ca: “pitaiva pūrvadhanahārīty āhuḥ | atra yady uktaṃ tad grāhyam | pitror abhāve bhrātaro dhanabhājaḥ” | tathā a manuḥ |
pitā hared aputrasya rikthaṃ bhrātara eva ca | iti | (MDh 9.185)
[527] bhrātṛṣv api sodaryāḥ prathamaṃ dhanabhājaḥ teṣāṃ pratyāsannatvāt | ata eva manuḥ |
anantaraḥ sapiṇḍādyas tasya tasya dhanaṃ bhaveet | iti | (MDh 9.187)
bhrātṝṇām abhāve bhrātṛputrā iti pūrvavad avagantavyam | bhrātṛbhrātṛputrasamavāye ‘pi bhrātṛputrāṇāṃ dhanasaṃbandho nāsti, bhrātrabhāve bhrātṛputrāṇām adhikāraśravaṇāt | yadā tv aputre bhrātari mṛte tadbhrātṝṇām aviśeṣeṇa dhanasaṃbandhe prāpte bhrātṛdhanavibhāgāt prāg eva yadi kaścid bhrātā mriyeta tatputrāś ca santi tadā tatputrāṇāṃ pitṛto ‘dhikāre prāpte teṣāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ ca vibhāgo yuktaḥ | bhrātṛputrāṇām abhāve gotrajā dhanaṃ gṛhṇanti | gotrajāś ca pitāmahī sapiṇḍāḥ samānodakāś ca ataḥ prathamaṃ pitāmahī dhanaṃ gṛhṇāti | yat tu,
mātary api ca vṛttāyāṃ pitur mātā dhanaṃ haret | (MDh 9.217)
iti mātranantaraṃ pitāmahyā dhanagrahaṇe prāpakaṃ vacanam, tad anapatyasya svaryātasya dhanaṃ patnyabhigāmi tadabhāve mātṛgāmi tadabhāve bhrātṛputragāmi tadabhāve sakulyagāmi tadabhāve bandhugāmi sahādhyāyigāmi tadabhāve brāhmaṇadhanavarjaṃ rājagāmi brāhmaṇadhanaṃ brāhmaṇa eva gṛhṇīyād iti | tathā ca,
[528]        aputrasyātha kulajā patnī duhitaro ‘pi vā |
tadabhāve pitā mātā bhrātā putrāś ca kīrtitāḥ ||
ityādi, kramapratipādakabahuvacanavirodhāt pitāmahyā dhanasaṃbandhamātraprāpakaṃ na kramaparam ity avagamyate | pitāmahyā abhāve pitāmahaḥ pitṛvyās tatputrāś ca yathākramaṃ dhanaṃ gṛhṇanti | pitāmahasantānābhāve prapitāmahī prapitāmahatatputrās tatputrāś ceti saptamaparyantaṃ gotrajā dhanaṃ gṛhṇanti | sapiṇḍānām abhāve samānodakā dhanaṃ gṛhṇanti | samānodakāś ca sapiṇḍānām upari sapta puruṣāḥ janmanāmajñānaparyantā vā | tad uktaṃ bṛhanmanunā |
sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate |
samānodakabhāvas tu nivartetācaturdaśāt ||
janmanāmasmṛter eke tatparaṃ gotram ucyate || iti |
gotrajānām abhāve bāndhavā dhanaṃ gṛhṇanti bāndhavāś ca trividho baudhāyanena darśitāḥ |
ātmapitṛṣvasuḥ putrāḥ ātmamātṛṣvasuḥ sutāḥ |
ātmamātulaputrāś ca vijñeyā ātmabāndhavāḥ ||
pituḥ pitṛṣvasuḥ putrāḥ pitur mātṛṣavasuḥ sutāḥ |
pitur mātulaputrāś ca vijñeyāḥ pitṛbāndhavāḥ ||
mātuḥ pitṛṣvasuḥ putrā mātur mātṛṣvasuḥ sutāḥ ||
mātur mātulaputrāś ca vijñeyā mātṛbhāndhavāḥ ||
[529] iti | bandhuṣv api yas tv āsannataraḥ sa eva pūrvaṃ gṛhṇāti | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
bahavo jñātayo yatra sakulyā bāndhavās tathā |
yas tv āsannataras teṣāṃ so ‘napatyadhanaṃ haret || iti |
bandhūnām abhave ācāryaḥ | ācāryābhāve śiṣyaḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
yo yo hy anantaraḥ piṇḍāt tasya tasya dhanaṃ bhavet |
ata ūrdhvaṃ sakulyaḥ syād ācāryaḥ śiṣya eva ca || iti | (MDh 9.187)
āpatambo ‘pi: “sapiṇḍābhāve ācāryaḥ ācāryābhāve antevāsī” iti (ĀpDh 1.6.14.3) | śiṣyābhāve sabrahmacārī tasyābhāve yaḥ kaścic chrotriyo gṛhṇāti | tad āha gautamaḥ: “śrotriyā brāhmaṇasyānapatyasya ṛkthaṃ bhajeran” iti (GDh 28.41) | tadabhāve brāhmaṇaḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
sarveṣām apy abhāve tu brāhmaṇā ṛkthabhaginaḥ |
traividyāḥ śucayo dāntās tathā dharmo na hīyate || [MDh 9.188)
[530] iti | brāhmaṇadhanaṃ na kadācid api rājagāmi | kṣatriyādidhanaṃ tu sabrahmacāriparyantānām abhāve rājagāmi | tad uktaṃ manunā |
ahāryaṃ brāhmaṇadravyaṃ rājñā nityam iti sthitiḥ |
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāṃ sarvābhāve haren nṛpaḥ || iti | (MDh 9.189)
nāradenāpi |
brāhmaṇārthasya tannāśe dāyādaś cen na kaścana |
brāhmaṇāyaiva dātavyam enasvī syān nṛpo ‘nyathā || iti |
saṃgrahakāreṇāpi |
pitary avidyamāne ‘pi dhanaṃ tatpitṛsantateḥ |
tasyām avidyamānāyāṃ tatpitāmahasantateḥ ||
asatyām api tasyāṃ tu prapitāmahasantateḥ |
evam evoparitanāṃ sapiṇḍā ṛkthabhāginaḥ ||
tadabhāve ‘sapiṇḍāḥ syur ācāryaḥ śiṣya eva vā |
sabrahmacārī sadvipraḥ pūrvābhāve paraḥ paraḥ ||
śūdrasyaikodakābhāve rājā dhanam avāpnuyāt |
ācāryasyāpy abhāve tu tathā kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ || iti |
nanu – anapatyasya dhanaṃ prathamam eva patnī gṛhṇātīty etad anupapannam, patnīsadbhāve ‘pi bhrātṝṇāṃ dhanagrahaṇasya patnīnāṃ vā bharaṇamātrasya nāradenoktatvāt |
[531]        bhrātṝṇām aprajāḥ preyāt kaścic cet pravrajeta vā |
vibhajeran dhanaṃ tasya śeṣās te strīdhanaṃ vinā ||
bharaṇaṃ cāsya kurvīran strīṇām ājīvanakṣayāt |
rakṣanti śayyāṃ bhartuś ced āchindyur itarāsu tat || iti | (NSm 13.25–26)
tan na – “saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ tu yo bhāgas teṣām eva sa iṣyate” iti prakramya “bhrātṝṇām aprajāḥ preyāt” ityādivacanasya paṭhitatvena saṃsṛṣṭabhrātṛbhāryāṇām anapatyānāṃ bharaṇamātram saṃsṛṣṭabhrātṝṇāṃ ca taddhanahāritvam anena vacanena pratipādyata ity avagamanāt | atha vā “bhrātṝṇām aprajāḥ preyāt” ityādivacanasya saṃsṛṣṭitvena,
saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ tu yo bhāgas teṣām eva sa iṣyate |
anapatyo ‘ṃśabhāgyo ‘pi nirbījeṣv itarān iyāt || (NSm 13.24)
ity anena paunaruktyaprasaṅgāt | atha vā avibhaktaviṣayatvam astu yājñavalkyavacanaṃ tu vibhaktasyāsaṃsṛṣṭino bhartur dhanaṃ patny eva prahamaṃ gṛhṇātīty evaṃparam ity avirodhaḥ | yat tu manunoktam,
pitā hared aputrasya ṛkthaṃ bhrātara eva vā | (MDh 9.185)
iti, yad api kātyāyanenoktam,
[532]         vibhakte saṃsthite dravyaṃ putrābhāve pitā haret |
bhrātā vā jananī vātha mātā vā tatpituḥ kramāt ||
iti — manuvacanaṃ tāvan na kramapratipādanaparam bhrātara eva veti vikalpaśravaṇāt, kātyāyanavacanaṃ tu patnyāṃ vyabhicāriṇyāṃ pitrāder anapatyadhanagrāhitvapratipādanaparam,
bhartur dhanaharī patnī yā syād avyabhicāriṇī |
apacārakriyāyuktā nirlajjā vārthanāśikā ||
vyabhicāraratā yā ca strī dhanaṃ sā na cārhati ||
iti tenaivoktatvāt | dhanaṃ jīvanāyopakḷptaṃ kṣetrāṃśaṃ nārhatīty arthaḥ | dhāreśvaras tu anapatyadhanaṃ patnī gṛhṇātīty evamādivacanajātasya prakārāntareṇa viṣayavyavasthām āha | niyogārthinī patnī anapatyasya vibhaktasya patyur dhanaṃ gṛhṇāti | tathā ca manuḥ |
dhanaṃ yo bibhṛtyād bhrātur mṛtasya striyam eva vā |
so ‘patyaṃ bhrātur utpadya dadyāt tasyaiva tad dhanam ||
kanīyāñ jyeṣthabhāryāyāṃ putram utpādayed yadi |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syād iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ || iti | (MDh 9.146, 120)
vibhaktadhane bhrātari mṛte apatyadvāreṇaiva patnyā dhanasaṃbandhaḥ | nānyathā | avibhaktadhane ‘pi tathety abhiprāyaḥ | gautamo ‘pi [533]: “piṇḍagotrarṣisaṃbandhā ṛkthaṃ bhajeran strī cānapatyasya | bījaṃ vā lipseta” iti (GDh 28.21–22) | ayam arthaḥ – anapatyasya ṛkthaṃ piṇḍagotraṛṣisaṃbandhā bhajeran strī vā yadi bījaṃ lipsyate iti | saṃgrahakāro ‘pi |
bhrātṛṣu pravibhakteṣu saṃsṛṣṭeṣv apy asatsu vā |
gurvādeśaniyogasthā patnī dhanam avāpnuyāt || iti |
tad anupapannam, patnī duhitara ity atra niyogāśravaṇāt |
aśruto ‘pi niyogo gautamādivacanabalāt kalpyate iti yuktam iti cet,
tan na, gautamādivacanānām arthāntaraparatvāt | tathā hi | tatra yad gautamavacanam: “piṇḍagotrarṣisaṃbandhā ṛkthaṃ bhajeran strī cānapatyasya | bījaṃ vā lipseta” iti | tasyāyam arthaḥ | yadi bījaṃ lipseta tadā patnī anapatyadhanaṃ gṛhṇātīti | api tarhy anapatyasya dhanaṃ piṇḍagotrarṣisaṃbandhā gṛhṇīyuḥ | strī vā gṛhṇīyāt | sā strī bījaṃ vā lipseta saṃyatā vā bhaved iti | vāśabdasya pakṣāntaravacanatvena yady arthe prayogābhāvāt | yad api dhanaṃ yo [534] bibhṛyād ityādimanuvacanaṃ tad api kṣetrajasyaiva dhanasaṃbandhaṃ vakti na patnyā iti | saṃgrahavacanam api saṃyatāyā eva dhanasaṃbandhaṃ vakti na tu devarādiniyuktāyāḥ | anyathā,
aputrā śayanaṃ bhartuḥ pālayantī vrate sthitā |
patny eva dadyāt tat piṇḍaṃ kṛtsnam aṃśaṃ labheta ca || iti |
tathā,
aputrā śayanaṃ bhartuḥ pālayantī gurau sthitā |
bhuñjītāmaraṇāt kṣāntā dāyādā ūrdhvam āpnuyuḥ ||
iti manu-kātyāyanavacanavirodhaprasaṅgāt | tasmās anapatyasya vibhaktasyāsaṃsṛṣṭino mṛtasya dhanaṃ patnī gṛhṇātīty eṣaiva vyavasthā jyāyasī | yat tu strīṇāṃ dhanasaṃbandhābhavapratipādakavacanam,
yajñārthaṃ dravyam utpannaṃ tatrānadhikṛtās tu ye |
arikthabhājas te sarve grāsācchādanabhajanāḥ ||
yajñārthaṃ vihitaṃ vittaṃ tasmāt tadviniyojayet |
sthāneṣu dharmajuṣṭeṣu na strīmūrkhavidharmiṣu ||
iti, tad yajñārtham eva saṃpāditadhanaviṣayam | yad api kātyāyanenoktam,
[535]        adāyikaṃ rājagāmi yoṣidbhṛtyaurdhvadehikam |
apāsya śrotriyadravyaṃ śrotriyebhyas tad arpayet ||
iti — ayam arthaḥ: strīṇām aśanācchādanopayuktaṃ dhaninaḥ śrāddhādyupayuktaṃ ca muktvā adāyikadhanaṃ rājagāmi bhavati | śrotriyadravyaṃ tu yoṣidbhṛtyaurdhavadaihikam apāsya śrotriyasya na rājña ity arthaḥ | — yad api naradenoktam,
anyatra brāhmaṇāt kiṃcid rājā dharmaparāyaṇaḥ |
tat strīṇāṃ jīvanaṃ dadyād eṣa dāyavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || (NSm 13.52)
iti, tad ubhayam apy avaruddhastrīviṣayaṃ patnīśabdaśravaṇāt | yad api ca hārītenoktam,
vidhavā yauvanasthā cet patnī bhavati karkaśā |
āyuṣo rakṣaṇārthaṃ tu dātavyaṃ jīvanaṃ tadā ||
iti, tad api śaṅkitavyabhicārastrīviṣayam | yad api prajāpativacanam,
āḍhakaṃ bhartṛhīnāyāḥ dadyād āmaraṇāntikam |
[536] iti, yad api smṛtyantare:
annārthaṃ taṇḍulaprastham aparāhṇe tu sendhanam |
iti, tad etad vacanadvayaṃ hārītavacanena samānārtham | yā ca śrutiḥ: “tasmāt striyo nirindriyā adāyādāḥ” iti, sā pātnīvatagrahe tatpatnyā aṃśo nāstīty evaṃparā | indriyaśabdasya “indriyaṃ vai somapīthaḥ” iti some prayogadharśanāt | yat tu patnyāḥ sthāvaragrahaṇaniṣedhakaṃ bṛhaspativacanam,
yadvibhakte dhane kiṃcid ādhyādividhisaṃsmṛtam |
tajjāyā sthāvaraṃ muktvā labheta gatabhartṛkā ||
iti, tad itaradāyādānumatim antareṇa sthāvaravikrayaniṣedhaparam, anyathā
jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ hema rūpyaṃ dhānyarasāmbaram |
ādāya dāpayec chrāddhaṃ māsasaṃvatsarādikam ||
pitṛvyagurudauhitrān bhartṛsvasrīyamātulān |
pūjayet kavyapūrtābhyāṃ vṛddhānāthātithīṃs tathā ||
iti anena virodhaprasaṅgāt | saṃsṛṣṭinām vibhāgaprakāram āha manuḥ |
[537]        vibhaktāḥ saha jīvanto vibhajeran punar yadi |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syāj jyaiṣṭhyaṃ tatra na vidyate || iti | (MDh 9.210)
samānavibhāgavidhānād eva viṣamavibhāganirākaraṇasiddheḥ jyaiṣṭhyaṃ tatra na vidyate iti punar viṣamavibhāganirākaraṇaṃ viṣamadhanena saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ dhanānusāreṇa viṣamavibhāgaprāptyartham | saṃsargaḥ kair ity apekṣite bṛhaspatiḥ |
vibhakto yaḥ punaḥ pitrā bhrātrā caikatra saṃsthitaḥ |
pitṛvyeṇātha vā prītyā tatsaṃsṛṣṭaḥ sa ucyate || iti |
yaḥ pūrvaṃ pitrādinā vibhaktaḥ putrādiḥ punaḥ prītyā tena saha samāpannaḥ sa saṃsṛṣṭa ucyate | yena kenāpi sahavāsam āpanna ity arthaḥ | kvacit saṃsṛṣṭināṃ viṣamavibhāgakāraṇam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ tu yaḥ kaścid vidyāśauryādinādhikam |
prāpnoti tatra dātavyo dvyaṃśaḥ śeṣāḥ samāṃśinaḥ || iti |
vidyādinā prāpte adhike dhane aṃśadvayaṃ dātavyaṃ na sarvasminn iti | etat saṃsṛṣṭadravyānuparodhenārjite ‘pi vibhājyatvaprāptyartham | aputrasya saṃsṛṣṭinaḥ ṛkthagrāhiṇaṃ darśayati yājñavalkyaḥ |
[538]        saṃsṛṣṭinas tu saṃsṛṣṭī sodarasya tu sodaraḥ |
dadyāc cāpaharec cāṃśaṃ jātasya ca mṛtasya ca || iti | (YDh 2.138)
ayam arthaḥ | saṃsṛṣṭino mṛtasyāṃśaṃ vibhāgakāle ‘vijñātagarbhāyāṃ bhāryāyāṃ paścād utpannasya putrasya itaraḥ saṃsṛṣṭī dadyāt putrābhāve saṃsṛṣṭy evāpaharet | na patnyādiḥ | patnīnām aprattaur duhitṝṇāṃ ca bharaṇamātram | tad āha nāradaḥ |
bharaṇaṃ cāsya kurvīran strīṇām ā jīvanakṣayāt |
rakṣanti śayyāṃ bhartuś ced āchindyur itarāsu tat ||
yadā duhitaras tasyāḥ pitryo ‘ṃśo bharaṇe mataḥ |
ā saṃskārād dhared bhāgaṃ parato bibhṛyāt patiḥ || iti |
“sodarasya tu sodaraḥ” iti sodarasya saṃsṛṣṭino mṛtasyāṃśaṃ sodaraḥ saṃsṛṣṭipaścād utpannasya putrasya dadyāt | tadabhāve svayam evāpaharet na bhinnodaraḥ | saṃsṛṣṭīti pūrvoktasyāpavādaḥ | saṃsṛṣṭino bhinnodarasya sodarasyāsaṃsṛṣṭinaḥ sadbhāve ubhayor api vibhajya dhanagrahaṇam ity āha sa eva |
anyodaryas tu saṃsṛṣṭī nānyodaryo dharaṃ haret |
asaṃsṛṣṭy api vādadyāt saṃsṛṣṭo nānyamātṛjaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.139)
sāpatnabhrātā saṃsṛṣṭo anyodaryadhanaṃ haret na [539] tv asaṃsṛṣṭī | asaṃsṛṣṭy apy sodaraḥ dhanam ādadīta | na punar anyodaryaḥ saṃsṛṣṭy eva | ata eva manuḥ |
yeṣāṃ jyeṣṭhaḥ kaniṣṭho vā hīyetāṃśapradānataḥ |
mriyetānyataro vāpi tasya bhāgo na lupyate ||
sodaryā vibhajeyus taṃ sametya sahitāḥ samam |
bhrātaro ye ca saṃsṛṣṭā bhaginyaś ca sanābhayaḥ || iti | (MDh 9.211–12)
ayam arthaḥ | yeṣāṃ saṃsṛṣṭināṃ bhinnodarāṇāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ madhye yaḥ ko ‘pi kaniṣṭho madhyamo vā vibhāgakāle deśāntaragamanādinā svāṃśād bhraśyeta tasya bhāgo na lupyate, pṛthag uddharaṇīyaḥ | na saṃsṛṣṭina eva gṛhṇīyuḥ | kiṃ tu tam uddhṛtaṃ bhāgam asaṃsṛṣṭinaḥ sodarāḥ saṃsṛṣṭinaś ca bhinnodarāḥ sanābhayo bhaginyaś ca deśāntaragatā api samāgamya saṃbhūya nyūnādhikabhāvam antareṇa vibhajeyuḥ |
anye manyante,
asaṃsṛṣṭy api vā dadyāt saṃsṛṣṭo nānyamātṛjaḥ | (YDh 2.139)
ity asyāyam arthaḥ | yatra saṃsṛṣṭā bhinnodarāḥ asaṃsṛṣṭāś ca sodarāḥ tatrāsaṃsṛṣṭā ap sodarā eva dhanaṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ na tu [540] bhinnodarāḥ saṃsṛṣṭā apīti | yat tu “yeṣāṃ jyeṣṭhaḥ” ityādimanuvacanaṃ saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ bhinnodarāṇām asaṃsṛṣṭānām ekodarāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ dhanagrahaṇapratipādakam | taj jaṅgamasthāvarātmakobhayadravyasadbhāvaviṣayam | ata eva prajāpatiḥ |
antardhanaṃ tu yad dravyaṃ saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ ca tad bhavet |
bhūmiṃ gṛhaṃ tv asaṃsṛṣṭāḥ pragṛhṇīyur yathāṃśataḥ || iti |
ayam arthaḥ | saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ bhinnodarabhrātṝṇām antardhanaṃ gūḍhadhanaṃ dravyaṃ vā jaṅgamātmakaṃ yathāṃśato bhavet | sodarāṇām asaṃsṛṣṭānāṃ gṛhakṣetrādikaṃ sthāvararūpaṃ yathāṃśato bhavet – iti | yājñavalkyavacanaṃ tu jaṅgamasthāvarayor anyatarasadbhāvaviṣayam iti | tatra yad yuktaṃ tad grāhyam |
yadā tu saṃsṛṣṭabhinnodarābhāvaḥ tadā pitā pitṛvyo vā yaḥ saṃsṛṣṭaḥ sa eva gṛhṇīyāt | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “saṃsṛṣṭini prete saṃsṛṣṭo ṛkthabhāk” iti (GDh 28.28) |
yadā pitā pitṛvyo vā saṃsṛṣṭo na vidyate tadā tv asaṃsṛṣṭabhinnodaro bhrātā gṛhṇīyat | tadabhāve tv asaṃsṛṣṭapitā | tadabhāve mātā | tadabhāve patnī | tad āha śaṅkhaḥ: “svaryātasya hy aputrasya bhrātṛgāmi dravyaṃ tadabhāve pitarau hareyātāṃ tadabhāve tv asaṃsṛṣṭaḥ pitā tadabhāve mātā tadabhāve jyeṣṭhā patnī” iti | [541] jyeṣṭhā guṇajyeṣṭhā na tu pūrvoḍhā | saṃsṛṣṭabhrātṛputrāṇāṃ patnyāś ca samavāye dhanagrahaṇaprakāram āha nārada |
mṛte patau tu bhāryāḥ syur abhrātṛpitṛmātṛkāḥ |
sarve sapiṇḍāḥ svadhanaṃ vibhajeyur yathāṃśataḥ || iti |
patyuḥ bhrātṛpitṛmatṛbhāvaviśiṣṭā abhātṛpitṛmātṛbhāryāḥ sarve sapiṇḍāḥ bhrātṛputrādayaḥ | tatra bhrātṛputrāṇāṃ svapitraṃśataḥ bhāryāṇāṃ bhartraṃśataḥ saṃsṛṣṭadhanasya vibhāga ity arthaḥ | patnīnām abhāve saṃsṛṣṭaputrāṃśaṃ tadbhrātṛbhaginī gṛhṇāti | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
yā tasya bhaginī sā tu tato ‘ṃśaṃ labdhum arhati |
anapatyasya dharmo ‘yam abhāryāpitṛkasya ca ||
iti paṭhitvā patnīnām abhāve duhitā gṛhṇītety āhuḥ | duhitṛbhaginyor abhāve,
anantaraḥ sapiṇḍādyas tasya tasya dhanaṃ bhavet |
[452] iti | uktapratyāsattikrameṇa sarve sapiṇḍādayo dhanaṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ, pratipakṣe doṣāṇām abhāvāt | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
mṛto ‘napatyo ‘abhāryaś ced abhrātṛpitṛmātṛkaḥ |
sarve sapiṇḍās tad dāyaṃ vibhajeyur yathāṃśataḥ || iti |
vānaprasthayatinaiṣṭhikabrahmacāriṇāṃ dhanaṃ ko vā gṛhṇātīty apekṣite āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
vānaprasthayatibrahmacāriṇām ṛkthabhāginaḥ |
krameṇācāryasacchiṣtadharmabhrātrekatīrthinaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.137)
atra prātilomyakrameṇa naiṣṭhikabrahmacāriṇāṃ dhanaṃ ācāryo gṛhṇāti na pitrādiḥ | upakurvāṇakasya dhanaṃ pitrādaya eva gṛhṇanti | yates tu dhanam adhyātmaśāstraśravaṇadhāraṇatadanuṣthānakṣamaḥ sacchiṣyo gṛhṇāti, durvṛttasya bhāgānarhatvāt | vānaprasthasya dhanaṃ dharmabhrātraikatīrthī gṛhṇāti | dharmabhrātā samānācāryakaḥ | ekatīrthī ekāśramī | dharmabhrātā cāsāv ekatīrthī ca dharmabhrātrekatīrthī |
[543]        atha vā | vānaprasthayatibrahmacāriṇāṃ dhanam ācāryasacchiṣyadharmabhrātrekatīrthinaḥ krameṇaiva gṛhṇanti | pūrvapūrvābhāve uttarottaro gṛhṇātīty arthaḥ | yat tu vasiṣṭhenoktam: “anaṃśās tv āśramāntaragatāḥ” (VaDh 17.52) iti, tad anyāśramiṇām anyāśramidhanagrahaṇaniṣedhaparam | na tu samānāśramiṇāṃ parasparaṛkthagrahaṇaniṣedhaparam |
nanu – eteṣāṃ dhanasaṃbandha eva nāsti kutas tadvibhāgaḥ, pratigrahāder dhanārjanopāyasya niṣiddhatvāt, “anarthanicayo bhikṣuḥ” (GDh 3.11) iti gautamasmaraṇāc ca |
tatra –
ahno māsasya ṣaṇṇāṃ vā tathā saṃvatsarasya ca |
arthasya nicyaṃ kuryāt kṛtam āśvayuje tyajet ||
iti vānaprasthasya dhanasaṃyogo ‘sti |
kaupīnācchādanārthaṃ tu vāso ‘pi bibhṛyād yatiḥ |
yogasaṃbhārabhedāṃś ca gṛhṇīyāt pāduke tathā ||
ityevamādivacanād yater api vastrapustakādikaṃ vidyata eva | [544] naiṣṭhikasyāpi śarīrayātrārthaṃ vastrādiparigraho ‘sty eveti tadvibhāgo ghaṭata eva | dāyānarhān āha manuḥ |
anaṃśau klībapatitau jātyandhabadhirau tathā |
unmattajaḍamūkāś ca ye ca kecin nirindriyāḥ || iti | (MDh 9.201)
nirindriyāḥ vyādhinaḥ vikalendriyāḥ | nārado ‘pi |
pitṛdviṭ patitaḥ ṣaṇḍho yaś ca syād aupapātikaḥ |
aurasā api naite ‘ṃśaṃ labheran kṣetrajāḥ kutaḥ || iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “anaṃśās tv āśramāntaragatāḥ” iti (VaDh 17.52) | yājñavalkyaḥ |
klībo ‘tha patitas tajjaḥ paṅgur unmattako jaḍaḥ |
andho ‘cikitsyarogādyā bhartavyāḥ syur niraṃśakāḥ || iti | (Ydh 2.140)
tajjaḥ patitotpannaḥ | ādiśabdena mūkādayo gṛhyante | ete niraṃśakāḥ ṛkthabhājo na bhavanti | kevalam [545] aśanācchādanadānena bhartavyāḥ poṣaṇīyāḥ | abharaṇe tu pratyavāyam āha manuḥ |
sarveṣām api tu nyāyyaṃ dātuṃ śaktyā manīṣiṇā |
grāsācchādanam atyantaṃ patito hy adadad bhavet || iti | (MDh 9.202)
atyantaṃ yāvajjīvam ity arthaḥ | patitasya bhartavyatvādy api nāstīty āha devalaḥ |
teṣāṃ patitavarjyebhyo bhaktaṃ vastraṃ pradīyate | iti |
patitaśabdena tajjāto ‘py upalakṣyate, “patitatajjavarjyaṃ bibhṛyuḥ” iti baudhāyanasmaraṇāt | āśramāntaragatā api te bhartavyāḥ | ata eva vasiṣṭhaḥ: “anaṃśās tv āśramāntaragatāḥ | klībonmattapatitāś ca bharaṇaṃ klībonmattānā”" iti (VaDh 17.52–54) | aṃśānarhāṇāṃ putrās tv aṃśabhājaḥ | tad āha devalaḥ |
tatputrāḥ pitṛdāyāṃśaṃ labheran doṣavarjitāḥ | iti |
niraṃśakānāṃ putrā aurasāḥ kṣetrajāś ca klaibyādidoṣavarjitā bhāgahāriṇo na dattakādayaḥ | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ parisaṃcaṣṭe |
[546]        aurasāḥ kṣetrajās teṣāṃ nirdoṣā bhāgahāriṇaḥ | iti |
niraṃśakānāṃ duhitaro yāvad vivāhaṃ bhartavyāḥ saṃskartavyāś ca patnyaś ca sādhuvṛttayo yāvajjīvaṃ bhartavyāḥ | tathā ca sa eva |
sutāś caiṣāṃ ca bhartavyā yāvad vai bhartṛsātkṛtāḥ |
aputrā yoṣitaś caiṣāṃ bhartavyāḥ sādhuvṛttayaḥ |
nirvāsyā vyabhicāriṇyaḥ pratikūlās tathaiva ca || iti | (YDh 2.141–42)
anyān api bhāgānarhān darśayati kātyāyanaḥ |
akramoḍhāsutaś caiva sagotrādyaś ca jāyate |
pravrajyāvasitaś caiva na ṛkthaṃ teṣu cārhati || iti |
manur api |
aniyuktāsutaś caiva putriṇyāptaś ca devarāt |
ubhau tau nārhato bhāgaṃ jārajātakakāmajau || iti | (MDh 9.143)
strīdhanavibhāgam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitṛmātṛpatibhrātṛdattam adhyagnyupāgatam |
ādhivedanikādyaṃ ca strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam ||
[547]        bandhudattaṃ tathā śulkam anvādheyakam eva ca |
aprajāyām atītāyāṃ bāndhavās tad avāpnuyuḥ || iti | (YDh 2.143–44)
adhyagnyupāgataṃ vivāhakāle ‘gnisaṃnidhau mātulādibhir dattam | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
vivāhakāle yat strībhyo dīyate hy agnisaṃnidhau |
tad adhyagnikṛtaṃ sadbhiḥ strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam || iti |
ādhivedanikam adhivedananimittam adhivinnastriyai dattam | āddyaśabdena adhyāvāhanikaṛkthakrayādiprāptaṃ ca | tathā ca manuḥ |
adhyagnyadhyāvāhanikaṃ dattaṃ ca prītikarmaṇi |
bhrātṛmātṛpitṛprāptaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ strīdhanaṃ smṛtam || iti | (MDh 9.194)
ṣaḍvidham iti nyūnasaṃkhyāvyavacchedārtham, nādhikasaṃkhyāvyavacchedāya | adhyāvāhanikaprītidattayoḥ svarūpaṃ kātyāyanenoktam |
yat punar labhate nārī nīyamānā pitur gṛhāt |
[548]        adhyāvāhanikaṃ nāma strīdhanaṃ tad udāhṛtam ||
prītyā dattaṃ tu yat kiṃcit śvaśrvā vā śvaśureṇa vā |
pādavandanikaṃ caiva prītidattaṃ tad ucyate || iti |
bandhudattaṃ kanyāyā mātṛpitṛbhir dattam | śulkaṃ yad gṛhītvā kanyā dīyate | anvādheyakaṃ pariṇayanād anu paścād dattam | tad uktaṃ kātyāyanena |
gṛhopaskaravāhyānāṃ dohyābharaṇakarmiṇām |
mūlyaṃ labdhaṃ tu yat kiṃcit śulkaṃ tat parikīrtitam ||
vivāhāt parato yat tu labdhaṃ bhartuḥ kulāt striyā |
anvādheyaṃ tu tad dravyaṃ labdhaṃ pitṛkulāt tathā || iti |
pitrādibhiḥ strībhyo danadāne viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
pitṛmātṛpatibhrātṛjñātibhiḥ strīdhanaṃ striyai |
yathāśaktyā dvisāhasrād dātavyaṃ sthāvarād ṛte || iti |
yathāśakti sthāvaravyatiriktaṃ dhanaṃ dvisahasrakārṣāpaṇaparyantaṃ dātavyam ity arthaḥ | ayaṃ ca niyamaḥ pratyabdādāne veditavyaḥ | anikābdeṣu tūpajīvanārthaṃ sakṛd eva dāne nāyam avadhiniyamaḥ | nāpi sthāvaraparyudāsaḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
[549]        dadyād dhanaṃ ca paryāptaṃ kṣetrāṃśaṃ vā yad icchati | iti |
ata eva saudāyike sthāvare ‘pi yatheṣṭaviniyogārhatvam uktaṃ tenaiva |
ūḍhayā kanyayā vāpi bhartuḥ pitṛgṛhe ‘pi vā |
bhrātuḥ sakāśāt pitror vā labdhaṃ saudāyikaṃ smṛtam ||
saudāyikaṃ dhanaṃ prāpya strīṇāṃ svātantryam iṣyate |
yasmāt tadānṛśaṃsyārthaṃ tair dattam upajīvanam ||
vikraye caiva dāne ca yatheṣṭaṃ sthāvareṣv api || iti |
patidattasthāvare tu viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
bhartrā prītena yad dattaṃ striyai tasmin mṛte ‘pi tat |
sā yathākāmam aśnīyād dadyād vā sthāvarād ṛte || iti |
pitrādibhir upādhyādinā dattaṃ strīdhanaṃ na bhavatīty āha kātyāyanaḥ |
tatra sopādhi yad dattaṃ yac ca yogavaśena vā |
pitrā bhrātrātha vā patyā na tat strīdhanam iṣyate || iti |
utsavādau dhāraṇārthaṃ dattam alaṃkārādikaṃ sopādhidattam | yogavaśena vañcanādinety arthaḥ | śilpādiprāptam api strīdhanaṃ na bhavatīty āha sa eva |
[550]         prāptaṃ śilpais tu yad dattaṃ prītyā caiva yad anyataḥ |
bhartuḥ svāmyaṃ tadā tatra śeṣaṃ tu strīdhanaṃ smṛtam || iti |
anyataḥ sakhyādita iti yāvat | tad etat strīdhanaṃ duhitṛdauhitratadduhitṛputrarahitāyāṃ striyām atītāyāṃ bāndhavā bhartrādayo gṛhṇanti | atraivaṃ kramaḥ | mātari vṛttāyāṃ prathamaṃ duhitā gṛhṇāti | ata evoktam yājñavalkyena |
mātur duhitaraḥ śeṣam ṛṇāt tābhya ṛte ‘nvayaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.117)
gautamo ‘pi: “strīdhanaṃ duhitṝṇām aprattānām apratiṣṭhitānāṃ ca” iti (GDh 28.24) | duhitṝṇām abhāve duhitur duhitaro gṛhṇanti | tadduhitṝṇāṃ prasūtā ced iti yājñavalkyasmaraṇāt | bhinnamātṛkāṇāṃ dauhitrāṇāṃ viṣamāṇāṃ samavāye mātṛto bhāgakalpanā | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “pitṛmātṛṣvasṛvarge bhāgaviśeṣaḥ” iti (GDh 28.17) | duhitṛdauhitrīṇāṃ samavāye manuḥ |
[551]        yās tāsāṃ syur duhitaras tāsām api yathārhataḥ |
mātāmahyā dhanāt kiṃcit pradeyaṃ prītipūrvakam || iti | (MDh 9.193)
dauhitrīṇām apy abhāve dauhitrā dhanahāriṇaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
mātur duhitaro ‘bhāve duhitṝṇāṃ tadanvayaḥ | iti |
duhitṛduhitṝṇām abhāve tadanvayo dauhitro gṛhṇātīty arthaḥ | dauhitrāṇām abhāve tu,
vibjajeran sutāḥ pitror ūrdhvaṃ ṛktham ṛṇaṃ samam | (YDh 2.117)
ityādiyājñavalkyavacanataḥ mātṛṛṇāpākaraṇato ‘vaśiṣṭaṃ mātṛdhanaṃ putrā gṛhṇanti | yat tu manunoktam,
jananyāṃ saṃsthitāyāṃ tu samaṃ sarve sahodarāḥ |
bhajeran mātṛkaṃ ṛkthaṃ bhaginyaś ca sanābhayaḥ || (MDh 9.192)
iti, etat putrāṇāṃ duhitṝṇāṃ ca saṃbhūya mātṛṛkathagrāhitvaparaṃ na bhavati | kiṃ tu teṣāṃ dhanasaṃbandhe prāpte samavibhāgaprāptyarthaṃ samaśabdaśravaṇāt | yad api śaṅkhalikhitābhyām uktam: “samaṃ sarve sahodarā mātṛkam ṛktham arhanti kumāryaś ca” [552] iti, tad api manuvacanena samānārtham | atha vā etad vacanadvayaṃ bhartuḥ kulalabdhastrīdhanaviṣayam | asminn eva viṣaye bṛhaspatiḥ |
strīdhanaṃ tadapatyānāṃ duhitā ca tadaṃśinī |
aprattā cet samūḍhā tu labhate sā na mātṛkam || iti |
apatyānāṃ pumapatyānām | yat tu pāraskareṇoktam,
aprattāyās tu duhituḥ strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam |
putras tu naiva labhate prattāyāṃ tu samāṃśabhāk ||
iti, tad apratiṣṭhitoḍhaduhitṛviṣayam | ata eva manuḥ |
mātus tu yautakaṃ yat syāt kumārībhāga eva saḥ | iti | (MDh 9.131)
yautakaṃ pitṛkulalabdham | anapatyahīnajātistrīdhanam uttamajātīyasapatnīduhitā gṛhṇāti | tadabhāve tadapatyam | tad uktam manunā |
striyās tu yad bhaved vittaṃ pitrā dattaṃ kathaṃcana |
brāhmaṇī tad dharet kanyā tadapatyasya vā bhavet || iti | (MDh 9.198)
brāhmaṇī jātyuttamajātyupalakṣaṇārtham | putrāṇām abhāve pautrā gṛhṇanti pautrāṇām api pitāmahyṛṇāpākaraṇe | “putrapautre ṛṇaṃ deyam” iti adhikāraśravaṇāt | astu ṛṇāpākaraṇe ‘dhakāraḥ |
ṛkthabhāktvaṃ kuta iti cet,
tan na,
[555]        pretāyāṃ putrikāyāṃ tu na bhartā dravyam arhati |
aputrāyāṃ kumāryāṃ ca bhrātrā tad grāhyam ity api || iti |
putrikāyāṃ pituḥ paścād aurasasadbhāve sa eva gṛhṇīyān na bhartā | yat tu manuvacanam,
aputrāyāṃ mṛtāyāṃ tu putrikāyāṃ kathaṃcana |
dhanaṃ tat putrikābhartā harec caivāvicārayan || (MDh 9.135)
iti, tat paścād utpannabhrātrabhāve veditavyam | yat tu kvacid anapatyaṃ strīdhanaṃ svasrīyādīnāṃ bhavatīty uktaṃ bṛhaspatinā |
mātṛṣavasā matulānī pitṛvyastrī pitṛṣvasā |
śvaśrūḥ pūrvajapatnī ca mātṛtulyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
yadāsām auraso na syāt suto dauhitra eva vā |
tatsuto vā dhanaṃ tāsāṃ svasrīyādyāḥ samāpnuyuḥ || iti |
asyāyam arthaḥ | brāhmādivivāheṣu bhartur abhāve āsurādiṣu mātāpitor abhāve mātṛṣvasrādīnāṃ dhanaṃ yathākramaṃ mātṛṣvasrīyādyā gṛhṇīyuḥ | kvacij jīvantyāḥ saprajāyā api patnyā dhanaṃ bhartā gṛhṇīyuād ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
durbhikṣe dharmakārye ca vyādhau saṃpratirodhake |
gṛhītaṃ strīdhanaṃ bhartā na striyai dātum arhati || (YDh 2.147)
[556] iti | saṃpratirodhake bandigrahādau svakīyadravyābhāve strīdhanaṃ gṛhītvā punas tasmai na dadyāt | prakārāntareṇa gṛhītaṃ punar dadyād eva | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
na bhartā naiva ca suto na pitā bhrātaro na ca |
ādāne vā visarge vā strīdhane prabhaviṣṇavaḥ ||
yadi caikataro ‘py eṣāṃ strīdhanaṃ bhakṣayed balāt |
savṛddhikaṃ pradāpyaḥ syād daṇḍaṃ caiva samāpnuyāt ||
tad eva yady anujñāpya bhakṣayet prītipūrvakam |
mūlam eva sa dāpyaḥ syād yady asau dhanavān bhavet || iti |
devalo ‘pi |
vṛttir ābharaṇaṃ śulkaṃ lābhaś ca strīdhanaṃ bhavet |
bhoktī tat svayam evedaṃ patir nārhaty anāpadi ||
vṛthā mokṣe ca bhoge ca striyai dadyāt savṛddhikam || iti |
vibhājyadravyam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
paitāmahaṃ ca pitryaṃ ca yac cānyat svayam arjitam |
dāyādānāṃ vibhāge tu sarvam eva vibhajyate || iti |
pitṛdravyopajīvanena svayam arjitaṃ yat tad eva vibhajet tadanupajīvanenārjitasyāvibhājyatvāt | etat tritayam api ṛṇāvaśiṣṭaṃ vibhajet | tathā ca sa eva |
[557]         ṛṇaṃ prītapradānaṃ ca datvā śeṣaṃ vibhājayet | iti |
ṛṇapradānārthaṃ dhanābhāve pitṛṛṇam api vibhajet, “ṛktham ṛṇaṃ samam” iti vacanāt | ṛktham ṛṇam iti vcanādinivṛttyarthaṃ (?) śodhyam ity āha sa eva |
ṛṇam evaṃvidhaṃ śodhyaṃ vibhāge bandhubhiḥ saha |
gṛhopaskaravāhyāś ca dohyābharaṇakarmiṇaḥ ||
dṛśyamānā vibhajyante kośaṃ gūḍham abravīd bhṛguḥ || iti |
atra kośagrahaṇam itaradivyapratiṣedhārtham | tathā ca sa eva |
śaṅkāviśvāsasaṃdhāne vibhāge ṛkthināṃ sadā |
kriyāsamūhakartṛtve kośam eva pradāpayet || iti |
avibhājyadravyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitṛdravyāvirodhena yad anyat svayam arjitam |
maitram audvāhikaṃ caiva dāyādānāṃ na tad bhavet ||
kramād abhyāgataṃ dravyaṃ hṛtam abhyuddharet tu yaḥ |
dāyādebhyo na tad dadyād vidyayā labdham eva ca || (YDh 2.118–19)
[558] iti | pitṛdravyāvināśena yat svayaṃ kṛṣyādinā upārjitaṃ yac ca vidyādinā labdhaṃ vivāhāc ca yal labdhaṃ tad bhrātrādīnāṃ na bhavet | yat tu pitrādikramāyātaṃ corādibhir apahṛtaṃ pitrādibhir anuddhṛtaṃ dravyaṃ putrāṇāṃ madhye yaḥ kaścid itarābhyanujñayoddharati tat tasyaiva bhavati | kṣetraṃ tu turīyāṃśam evoddhartā gṛhṇāti śeṣaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ samam eva | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ |
pūrvanaṣṭāṃ tu yo bhūmiṃ yaḥ kaścid uddharet śramāt |
yathābhāgaṃ labhanty anye datvāṃśaṃ tu turīyakam || iti |
tathā vidyayādhyāpanādinā labdham api svasyaiva bhavati | pitṛdravyāvirodheneti sarvatra śeṣaḥ | ata eva manuḥ |
anupaghnan pitṛdravyaṃ śrameṇa yad upārjayet |
(dāyādebhyo na tad dadyād vidyayā labdham eva ca |)
svayam īhitalabdhaṃ tan nākāmo dātum arhati || iti | (MDh 9.208)
śrameṇa kṛṣyādinā | pitṛgrahaṇam avibhaktopalakṣaṇārtham | vyāso ‘pi |
[559]        vidyāprāptaṃ śauryadhanaṃ yac ca saudāyikaṃ bhavet |
vibhāgakāle tat tasya nānveṣṭavyaṃ ca ṛkthibhiḥ || iti |
avibhājyavidyādhanasya lakṣaṇam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
parabhaktopayogena prāptā vidyānyatas tu yā |
tayā prāptaṃ dhanaṃ yat tu vidyāprāptaṃ tad ucyate ||
upanyasteṣu yal labdhaṃ vidyayā paṇapūrvakam |
vidhādhanaṃ tu tad vidyād vibhāge na niyojyate ||
śiṣyād ārtvijyataḥ praśnāt saṃdigdhapraśnanirṇayāt |
svajñānaśāsanād vādāt labdhaṃ prādhyāpanāc ca yat ||
paraṃ nirasya yal labdhaṃ vidyāto ‘dbhutapūrvakam |
vidyādhanaṃ tu tad vidyān na vibhājyaṃ bṛhaspatiḥ ||
śilpiṣv api hi dharmo ‘yaṃ svasthād yac cādhikaṃ bhavet |
vidyābalakṛtaṃ caiva yājyataḥ śiṣyatas tathā ||
etad vidyādhanaṃ prāhuḥ sāmānyaṃ yad ato ‘nyathā || iti |
anena vidyādhanād anyathābhūtam avibhaktapitrādidravyopayogaprāptaṃ tad vibhaktānāṃ sāmānyaṃ sādhāraṇam iti yāvat | kvacid vidyāprāptam api dhanaṃ vibhājyam ity āha nāradaḥ |
[560]        kuṭumbaṃ bibhṛyād bhrātur yo vidyām adhigacchataḥ |
bhāgaṃ vidyādhanāt tasmāt sa labhetāśruto ‘pi san || iti |
kātyāyano ‘pi |
kule vinītavidyānāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ pitṛto ‘pi vā |
śauryaprāptaṃ tu yad vittaṃ vibhājyaṃ tad bṛhaspatiḥ || iti |
avibhaktasya kule pitṛvyādeḥ pitṛto ‘pi vā prāptavidyānāṃ yad dhanaṃ śauryādināprāptaṃ vidyayaiva prāptaṃ tad vidyādhanaṃ vibhājyam iti | pitṛdravyārjitenārjite vidhādhane bhāgadvayam arjakasyāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “yena caiṣāṃ yad upārjitaṃ syād dvyaṃśam eva labheta” iti (VaDh 17.51) | yat tu,
sāmānyārthasamutthāne vibhāgas tu samaḥ smṛtaḥ |
iti, tad vidyetarakṛṣyādyupārjitadhanaviṣayam | avibhājyavidyādhane arjakecchayā aṃśam āha gautamaḥ: “svayam arjitaṃ caiva vaidyebhyo vaidyaḥ kāmaṃ dadyāt” iti | icchābhāve tv āha nāradaḥ |
vaidyo vaidyāya nākāmo dadyād aṃśaṃ svato dhanāt |
[561]        pitṛdravyaṃ samāśritya na cet tena tad āhṛtam ||
avaidyāya sakāmo ‘pi na dadyāt | tad āha kātyāyanaḥ |
nāvidyānāṃ tu vaidyena deyaṃ vidyādhanaṃ kvacit |
samavidyādhikānāṃ tu deyaṃ vaidyena tad dhanam || iti |
vidyāpāptadhanavat śauryādiprāptam api dhanam avibhājyam ity āha sa eva |
śauryaprāptaṃ vidyayā ca strīdhanaṃ caiva yat smṛtam |
etat sarvaṃ vibhāge tu vibhājyaṃ naiva ṛkthibhiḥ ||
dhvajāhṛtaṃ bhaved yat tu vibhājyaṃ naiva tat smṛtam || iti |
dhvajāhṛtasya lakṣaṇaṃ tenaivoktam |
saṃgrāmād āhṛtaṃ yat tu vidrāvya dviṣatāṃ balam |
svāmyarthe jīvitaṃ tyaktvā tad dhvajāhṛtam ucyate || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
pitāmahapitṛbhyāṃ ca dattaṃ mātrā ca yad bhavet |
tasya tan nāpahartavyaṃ śauryabhāryādhanaṃ tathā || iti |
śauryaprāptadhanasvarūpaṃ ca kātyāyanena darśitam |
āruhya saṃśayaṃ yatra prasabhaṃ karma kurvate |
tasmin karmaṇi tuṣṭena prasādaḥ svāminā kṛtaḥ ||
tatra labdhaṃ tu yat kiṃcid dhanaṃ śauryeṇa tad bhavet |
[562] iti | pitrādidravyopajīvanena vidyāprāptadhanavat śauryaprāptadhane ‘py arjakasya bhāgadvayam āha vyāsaḥ |
sādhāraṇaṃ samāśritya yat kiṃcid vāhanāyudham |
śauryādināpnoti dhanaṃ bhrātaras tatra bhāginaḥ ||
tasya bhāgadvayaṃ deyaṃ śeṣās tu samabhāginaḥ || iti |
anyad apy avibhājyam āha manuḥ |
vastraṃ patram alaṃkāraṃ kṛtānnam udakaṃ striyaḥ |
yogakṣemaṃ pracāraṃ ca na vibhājyaṃ pracakṣate || iti | (MDh 9.219)
vastraṃ pitṛdhṛtaṃ vastram | pitṛdhṛtaṃ vastraṃ pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhāge śrāddhabhoktre dātavyam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
vastrālaṃkāraśayyādi pitur yad vāhanādikam |
gandhamālyaiḥ samabhyarcya śrāddhabhoktre tad arpayet || iti |
anyāni tu vastrāṇi vibhājyāny eva | patram aśvaśibikādivāhanaṃ tad api yad yenārūḍhaṃ tat tasyaiva | anārūḍhaṃ tu sarvair vibhājyam | alaṃkāro ‘pi yo yena dhṛtaḥ sa tasyaiva | adhṛtaḥ sādhāraṇo vibhājya eva,
patyau jīvati yat strībhir alaṃkāro ghṛto bhavet |
na taṃ bhajeran dāyādā bhajamānāḥ patanti te || (MDh 9.200)
[563] iti tenaiva dhṛta iti viśeṣenopādānāt | kṛtānnaṃ taṇḍulamodakādi | tad api yathāsaṃbhavaṃ bhoktavyaṃ na vibhājyam | udakaṃ tadādhāraḥ kūpādiḥ | so ‘pi viṣamaḥ paryāyeṇa bhoktavyo na mūlyadvāreṇa vibhājyaḥ | striyaś ca dāsyo viṣamāḥ paryāyeṇa karma kārayitavyāḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
ekāṃ strīṃ kārayet karma yathāṃśena gṛhe gṛhe |
bahvyaḥ samāṃśato deyā dāsānām apy ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |
pitrāvaruddhās tu samā api na vibhājyāḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “strīṣu saṃyuāsv avibhāgaḥ” iti (GDh 28.47) | yoga iti śrautasmārtāgnisādhyam iṣṭaṃ karma lakṣyate | kṣemaḥ iti labdhaparīkṣaṇahetubhūtaṃ bahirvedidānataḍāgārāmanirmāṇādi pūrtaṃ karma lakṣyate | tad ubhayaṃ pitṛdravyavirodhenārjitam apy avibhājyam | tad uktaṃ laugākṣiṇā \
kṣemaṃ pūrtaṃ yāgam iṣṭam ity āhus tatvadarśinaḥ |
avibhājye ca te prokte śayanāsanam eva ca ||
[564] iti | atha vā yogakṣemaśabdena chatracāmaraśastravāhanādiprabhṛtaya ucyante | yat tu uśanasā kṣetrasyāpy avibhājyatvam uktam,
avibhājyaṃ sagotrāṇām ā sahasrakulād api |
yājyaṃ kṣetraṃ ca patraṃ ca kṛtānnam udakaṃ striyaḥ ||
iti, tat pratigrahalabdhakṣetraṃ kṣatriyāsutena sārdhaṃ brāhmaṇīsutena avibhājyam ity evaṃparam, “na pratigrahabhūr deyā kṣatriyādisutāya ca” iti smaraṇāt |
anye manyante | vastrādayo ‘pi vibhājyā eva | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
vastrādayo ‘vibhajyāyair uktaṃ tair na vicāritam |
dhanaṃ bhavet samṛddhānāṃ vastrālaṃkārasaṃśritam ||
madhyasthitam anājīvyaṃ dātuṃ naikasya śakyate |
yuktyā vibhajanīyaṃ tad anyathānarthakaṃ bhavet ||
vikrīya vastrābharaṇam ṛṇam udgrāhya lekhitam |
kṛtānnaṃ vā kṛtannena parivartya vibhajyate ||
uddhṛtya kūpavāpyambhas tv anusāreṇa gṛhyate |
[565]        ekāṃ strīṃ kārayet karma yathāṃśena gṛhe gṛhe ||
bahvyaḥ samāṃśato deyā dāsānām apy ayaṃ vidhiḥ |
yogakṣemavato lābhaḥ samatvena vibhajyate ||
pracāraś ca yathāṃśena kartavyo ṛkthibhiḥ saha || iti |
tena vastrādīnām avibhājyatvapratipādakaṃ manuvacanam uśanovacanaṃ cānādaraṇīyam |
tad anupapannam | virodhe hi vacanānāṃ viṣayavyavasthāśrayaṇaṃ yuktam, na cānyathākaraṇam | bṛhaspativacanānāṃ tu adhṛtavastrādiviṣayatvam | manvādivacanasya tu ghṛtavastrādiviṣayatvaṃ pūrvam evoktam iti viṣayavyavasthā ghaṭate |
vibhāgakāle kenacid vañcitaṃ paścād udbhāvitaṃ cet tat sarve samaṃ vibhajeyuḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
anyonyāpahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ vibhakte yat tu dṛśyate |
tat punas te samair aṃśaiḥ vibhajerann iti sthitiḥ || iti | (YDh 2.126)
samaśabdo viṣamavibhāganirāsārthaḥ | vibhajerann iti bahuvacanena yena dṛṣṭaṃ tenaiva na grāhyam iti darśayati | [566] manur api |
ṛṇe dhane ca sarvasmin pravibhakte yathāvidhi |
paścād dṛśyeta yat kiṃcit tat sarvaṃ samatāṃ nayet || iti | (MDh 9.218)
apahṛtadravyavac chāśtroktānatikrameṇa viṣamabhāgatayā vibhaktam api samatāṃ nayet | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
anyonyāpahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ durvibhaktaṃ ca yad bhavet |
paścāt prāptaṃ vibhajyeta samabhāgena tad bhṛguḥ || iti |
evaṃ ca sati vañcitadravyasya paścād dṛṣṭasyaiva vibhāgavidhānāt pūrvaṃ vibhaktasya punar vibhāgo nāstīty avagamyate | yat tu manunoktam,
vibhāge tu kṛte kiṃcit sāmānyaṃ yatra dṛśyate |
nāsau vibhāgo vijñeyaḥ kartavyaḥ punar eva hi ||
iti, tad vibhaktadraveṣv āyavyayakaraṇād arvāg veditavyam, anyonyāpahṛtam ityādivacanānāṃ nirviṣayāpatteḥ | atrānyonyavañcane doṣo ‘py asti | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “yo vai bhāginaṃ bhāgān nudate cayate vainaṃ sa yadi vainaṃ na cayate ‘tha putram atha pautraṃ cayate” iti | ayam arthaḥ | yo bhāginaṃ bhāgārhaṃ bhāgān nudate bhāgād apākaroti bhāgaṃ tasmai na prayacchatīti yāvat | atha bhāgāc cyutaḥ enam apahārakaṃ cayate nāśayati pāpinaṃ karotīti [567] yāvat | yadi taṃ na nāśayati tadā tasya putraṃ pautraṃ vā nāśayatīti | yat tu manunoktam,
yo jyeṣṭho vinikurvīta lobhād bhrātṝn yavīyasaḥ |
so ‘jyeṣṭhaḥ syād abhāgaś ca niyantavyaś ca rājabhiḥ || (MDh 9.213)
iti, tat svatantrasyāpi jyeṣṭhasya samudāyadravyāpahāre doṣo ‘sti kim utāsvatantrāṇāṃ yavīyasām ity evaṃ pratipādanaparam, na tu jyeṣṭhasyaiva doṣapratipādanaparam, anyathā smṛtyā śruteḥ saṅkocāpatteḥ | vibhaktānāṃ kartavyam āha nāradaḥ |
yady ekajātā bahavaḥ pṛthagdharmāḥ pṛthakkriyāḥ |
pṛthakkarmaguṇopetā na cet kāryeṣu saṃmatāḥ ||
svabhāgān yadi dadyus te vikrīṇīyur athāpi vā |
kuryur yatheṣṭaṃ tat sarvam īśās te svadhanasya ca || iti |
ekasmāj jātā vibhaktā bhrātaraḥ parasparānumatim antareṇa dhanasādhyeṣṭāpūrtādidharmakāriṇo bhaveyuḥ | tathā vibhinnolūkhalamusalādikarmopasarjanadravyopetāḥ syuḥ | tathā ca kāryeṣu bhrātaro yadi na saṃmatāḥ tadā tān anādṛtya kāryaṃ kuryuḥ | tathā vibhaktā [568] bhrātaraḥ svabhāgān yadi dadyur vikrīṇīyur vā na dadyur vā tat sarvaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ kuryuḥ | yasmāt te vibhaktāḥ svadhanasyeśāḥ svatantrāḥ svāmina ity arthaḥ | yat tu bṛhaspativacanam,
vibhaktā vāvibhaktā vā dāyādāḥ sthāvare samāḥ |
eko hy anīśaḥ sarvatra dānādhamanavikraye ||
iti, tad evaṃ vyākhyeyam | avibhakteṣu dravyeṣu sādhāraṇatvād ekasyānīśvaratvāt sarvair anujñāvaśyaṃ kāryā | vibhakteṣu tūttarakālaṃ vibhaktāvibhaktasaṃśayavyudāsanasaukaryāya sarvābhyanujñā | na punar ekasyānīśvaratvena | ato vibhaktānumativyatirekeṇāpi vyavahāraḥ sidhyaty eveti | yat tu smṛtyantaram,
svagrāmajñātisāmantadāyādānumatena ca |
hiraṇyodakadānena ṣaḍbhir gacchati medinī ||
iti, tasyāyam abhiprāyaḥ | tatra grāmānumatiḥ:
pratigrahaḥ prakāśaḥ syāt sthāvarasya viśeṣataḥ |
iti smaraṇāt, vyavahāraprakāśanārtham evāpekṣyate | na punar grāmānumatim antareṇa vyavahāro na sidhyatīti | sāmantānumatir api sīmāvipratipattinirāsārtham eva | dāyādānumatir api vibhaktāvibhaktasaṃśayavyudāsena vyavahārasaukaryārtham eva | hiraṇyodakadānam api vikraye kartavye sahiraṇyodakaṃ datvā dānarūpeṇa sthāvaravikrayaṃ kuryād ity evamartham,
[569]        sthāvare vikrayo nāsti kuryād ādhim anujñayā |
iti sthāvaravikrayasya niṣiddhatvāt,
bhūmiṃ yaḥ pratigṛhṇāti yaś ca bhūmiṃ prayacchati |
tāv ubhau puṇyakarmāṇau niyataṃ svargagāminau ||
iti dānapratigrahayoḥ praśastatvāc ca | vibhāgāpalāpe nirṇayakāraṇam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
vibhāganihnave jñātibandhusākṣyabhilekhitaiḥ |
vibhāgabhāvanā jñeyā gṛhakṣetraiś ca yautakaiḥ || iti | (YDh 2.149)
jñātayaḥ pitṛbandhavaḥ | bandhavas tu mātulādayaḥ | lekhyaṃ vibhāgapatram | ebhir vibhāganirṇayo jñātavyaḥ | yautakaiḥ pṛthakkṛtair gṛhakṣetraiḥ | anyad api vibhāgaliṅgam āha nāradaḥ |
vibhāgadharmasaṃdehe dāyādānāṃ vinirṇayaḥ |
jñātibhir bhāgalekhyena pṛthakkāryapravartanāt ||
bhrātṝṇām avibhaktānām eko dharmaḥ pravartate |
vibhāge sati dharmo ‘pi bhavet teṣāṃ pṛthak pṛthak ||
sākṣitvaṃ prātibhāvyaṃ ca dānaṃ grahaṇam eva ca |
vibhaktā bhrātaraḥ kuryur nāvibhaktāḥ parasparam ||
dānagrahaṇapaśvannagṛhakṣetrapratigrahāḥ |
vibhaktānāṃ pṛthak jñeyā dānadharmāgamavyayāḥ ||
[570]        yeṣām etāḥ loke pravartante svarikthiṣu |
vibhaktān avagaccheyur lekhyam apy antareṇa tān || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
pṛthagāyavyayadhanāḥ kusīdaṃ ca parasparam |
vaṇikpathaṃ ca ye kuryur vibhaktās te na saṃśayaḥ || iti |
kusīdavāṇijyādibhir liṅgair vibhāganirṇayaḥ sākṣyādyabhāve veditavyaḥ | tathā ca sa eva |
sāhasaṃ sthāvarasvāmyaṃ prāgvibhāgaś ca rikthinām |
anumānena vijñeyaṃ na syur yatra ca sākṣiṇaḥ || iti |
sāhasādisādhakaliṅgāny api sa evāha |
kulānubandhavyāghātahoḍhaṃ sāhasasādhakam |
svasvabhogasthāvarasya vibhāgasya pṛthagdhanam || iti |
kulānubandhaḥ pūrvapuruṣavairānubandhaḥ | vyāghātaḥ parasparasya hoḍhaṃ balād apahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ tasya darśanaṃ svasyātmano bhogaḥ parasparaṃ pṛthag grahaṇāder vibhāgaliṅgatvam avibhakteṣu niṣiddhatvenāvagantavyam | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
[571]        bhrātṝṇām atha dampatyoḥ pituḥ putrasya caiva hi |
prātibhāvyam ṛṇaṃ sākṣyam avibhaktena tu smṛtam || iti | (YDh 2.52)
sākṣilekhyayuktibhir nirṇayasyāśakyatve,
yuktiṣv apy asamarthāsu śapathair eva mardayet |
iti prāptaṃ divyaṃ niṣedhati vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
vibhāgadharmasaṃdehe bandhusākṣyabhilekhitaiḥ |
vibhāgabhāvanā kāryā na bhaved daivikī kriyā || iti | (YDh 2.149)
kathaṃ tarhi tatra nirṇaya ity ākāṅkṣāyām āha manuḥ |
vibhāge yatra saṃdeho dāyādānāṃ parasparam |
punar vibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ pṛthak sthānasthitair api || iti |
yatra saṃdeho yuktibhir api nāpaiti tatra punar vibhāgaḥ kartavya ity arthaḥ | yat tu tenaivoktam,
sakṛd aṃśo nipatati sakṛt kanyā pradīyate |
sakṛd āha dadāmīti trīṇy etāni sakṛt sakṛt ||
iti, tad yuktyādibhir nirṇetuṃ śaktau satyāṃ veditavyam | [572] svayaṃkṛtasya saṃdigdhasya vibhāgasya punaḥ pravartako rājñā daṇḍanīya ity āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
svecchākṛtavibhāgo yaḥ punar eva visaṃvadet |
sa rājñāṃ śakyate sthāpyaḥ śāsanīyo ‘nubandhataḥ || iti |
anubandhato nirbandhataḥ |
iti dāyavibhāgaḥ
atha dyūtasamāhvayākhye vivādapade nirūpyete
tayoḥ svarūpam āha manuḥ |
aprāṇibhir yat kriyate tal loke dyūtam ucyate |
prāṇibhiḥ kriyate yas tu sa vijñeyaḥ samāhvayaḥ || iti | (MDh 9.223)
aprāṇibhir akṣavadhraśalākādibhiḥ | prāṇibhiḥ kukkuṭādibhiḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
akṣavadhaśalākādyair devanaṃ jihmakāritam |
paṇakrīḍā vayobhiś ca padaṃ dyūtasamāhvayam ||
[573] iti | akṣāḥ pāśakāḥ | vadhrāś carmapaṭṭikāḥ | śalākā dantādimayāḥ dīrghacaturasrāḥ | ādyaśabdena kapardikādayo gṛhyante | taiḥ paṇapūrvakaṃ yad devanaṃ krīḍanaṃ kriyate tad dyūtam, vayobhiḥ kukkuṭādibhiḥ ca kārān mallameṣādibhiś ca prāṇibhir yā paṇapūrvikā krīḍā kriyate sa samāhvaya ity arthaḥ | bṛhaspatir api |
anyonyaparigṛhītāḥ pakṣimeṣavṛṣādayaḥ |
praharante kṛtapaṇās taṃ vadanti samāhvayam || iti |
dyūtasthānaṃ sabhikenādhiṣṭhitaṃ kāryam ity āha sa eva |
sabhikādhiṣṭhitaṃ kāryaṃ taskarajñānahetunā | iti |
tatra pakṣāntaram āha nāradaḥ |
atha vā kitavo rājñe datvā bhāgaṃ yathoditam |
prakāśaṃ devanaṃ kuryād evaṃ doṣo na vidyate || iti |
dyūtasabhādhikāriṇo vṛttim āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
glahe śatikavṛddhes tu sabhikaḥ pañcakaṃ śatam |
gṛhṇīyād dhūrtakitavād itarād daśakaṃ śatam || (YDh 2.199)
[574] iti | parasparasaṃpratipattyā kitavaparikalpitapaṇo glahaḥ | tatra tadāśrayā śatikā śataparimitā tadadhikaparimāṇā vā vṛddhir yasyāsau śatikavṛddhiḥ | tasmāt kitavāt pañcakaṃ śataṃ sabhikaḥ ātmavṛttyarthaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | pañcapaṇā āyo yasmin śate tac chatapañcakam jitaglahasya viṃśatitamaṃ bhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyad iti yāvat | kitavanivāsārthaṃ śālā sabhā tatrādhiṣṭhitaḥ sabhikaḥ | sabhāpatis tu kalpitākṣādinikhilakrīḍopakaraṇaḥ tadupacitadravyopajīvī | itarasmād apūrṇaśatavṛddheḥ kitavād daśakaṃ śataṃ jitadravyasya daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ | evaṃ sthāpitasya sabhikasya kṛtyam āha sa eva |
sa samyakpālito dadyād rājñe bhāgaṃ yathākṛtam |
jitaṃ tad vāhayet jetre dadyāt satyaṃ vacaḥ kṣamī || iti | (YDh 2.200)
yaḥ kḷptavṛttir dyūtādhikārī rājñā dhūrtakitavebhyo rakṣitaḥ sa rājñe yathāpratipannam aṃśaṃ dadyāt | jitaṃ dravyaṃ parājitasakāśād ādedhādinā uddhṛtya jetre dadyāt | tathā kṣamī bhūtvā dyūtakāriṇāṃ viśvāsārthaṃ satyaṃ vaco dadyāt | bṛhaspatir api |
sabhiko grāhakas tatra dadyāj jetre nṛpāya ca |
[575] iti | parājitakitavānāṃ bandhanādinā paṇagrāhako bhavet | paṇagrahaṇād arvāg eva svakīyaṃ paṇaṃ rājñe jetre ca yathābhāgaṃ dadyād ity arthaḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
jetur dadyāt svakaṃ dravyaṃ jitaṃ grāhyaṃ tripakṣakam |
sadyo vā sabhikenaiva kitavāt tu na saṃśayaḥ || iti |
yadā sabhiko jetre jitaṃ dravyaṃ dāpayituṃ na śaktaḥ tadā rājā dāpayed ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
prāpte nṛpatinā bhāge prasiddhe dhūrtamaṇḍale |
jitaṃ sasabhike sthāne dāpayed anyathā na tu || iti | (YDh 2.201)
anyathā pracchanne sabhikarahite adattarājabhāge dyūte jitaṃ paṇaṃ jetre na dāpayed ity arthaḥ | atra jayaparājayavipratipattau nirṇayakāraṇam āha sa eva |
draṣṭāro vyavahārāṇāṃ sākṣiṇaś ca ta eva hi | iti | (YDh 2.202)
dyūtākhyavyavahārāṇāṃ draṣṭāraḥ kitavā eva rājñā niyoktavyāḥ na tu śrutādhyayanasaṃpannā ity uktalakṣaṇāḥ | sākṣiṇaś ca ta eva dyūtakārā eva | viṣṇur api |
[576]         kitaveṣv eva tiṣṭheran kitavāḥ saṃśayaṃ prati |
ya eva tatra draṣṭāras ta evaiṣāṃ tu sākṣiṇaḥ || iti |
sākṣiṇāṃ parasparavirodhe rājā vicārayed ity āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
ubhayor api saṃdigdhau kitavāḥ syuḥ parīkṣakāḥ |
yadā vidveṣiṇas te tu tadā rājā vicārayet || iti |
kūṭadyūtakāriṇo daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
rājñā sacihnaṃ nirvāsyāḥ kūṭākṣopādhidevinaḥ | iti | (YDh 2.202)
kūṭair akṣādibhir upādhibhir maṇimantrādīnām ativañcanena ye divyanti tān śvapadenāṅkayitvā svarāṣṭrān nirvāsayed ity arthaḥ | nirvāsane viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
kūṭākṣadevinaḥ pāpān rājā rāṣṭrād vivāsayet |
kaṇṭhe ‘kṣamālām āsajya sa hy eṣāṃ vinayaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
daṇḍane viśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ: “dyūte kūṭākṣadevināṃ karacchedaḥ | upādhidevināṃ saṃdaṃśacchedaḥ” iti (ViDh 5.135–36) | aniyuktadyūtakāriṇo daṇḍam āha nāradaḥ |
[577]        anirdiṣṭas tu yo rājñā dyūtaṃ kurvīta mānavaḥ |
na śataṃ prāpnuyāt kāmaṃ vinayaṃ caiva so ‘rhati || iti |
dyūte vihitaṃ karmajātaṃ samāhvaye atidiśati yājñavalkyaḥ |
eṣa eva vidhir jñeyaḥ prāṇidyūte samāhvaye | iti | (YDh 2.203)
sabhikavṛttikalpanādilakṣaṇo dharmaḥ samāhvaye ‘pi vijñeya ity arthaḥ | prāṇidyūte prāṇināṃ jayaparājayau tatsvāminor ity āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
dvandvayuddhena yaḥ kaścid avasādam avāpnuyāt |
tatsvāminā paṇo deyo yas tatra parikalpitaḥ || iti |
paṇaparikalpanam api kṛtākṛtam ity āha nāradaḥ |
parihāsakṛtaṃ yac ca yac cāpy aviditaṃ nṛpe |
tatrāpi nāpnuyāt kāmyam atha vānumataṃ tayoḥ || iti |
kāmyaḥ kāmaḥ paṇaḥ | yat tu manunoktam,
dyūtaṃ samāhvayaṃ caiva yaḥ kuryāt kārayeta vā |
tān sarvān ghātayed rājā śūdrāṃś ca dvijaliṅginaḥ || (MDh 9.224)
[578]         prakāśam etat tāskaryaṃ yad devanasamāhvayau |
tayor nityaṃ pratīghāte nṛpatir yatnavān bhavet || (MDh 9.222)
dyūtaṃ samāhvayaṃ caiva rājā rāṣṭrān nivārayet |
rājāntakāriṇāv etau dvau doṣau pṛthivīkṣitām || (MDh 9.221)
kitavān kuśīlavān kaulān pāṣaṇḍān api mānavān |
vikarmasthān śauṇḍikāṃś ca kṣipraṃ nirvāsayet purāt || (MDh 9.225)
ityādi, tat sarvaṃ kūṭākṣadevanaviṣayatayā rājñām adhyakṣasabhikarahitaviṣayatayā vā yojyam | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
dyūtaṃ niṣiddhaṃ manunā satyaśaucadhanāpaham |
abhyanujñātam anyais tu rājabhāgasamanvitam |
sabhikādhiṣṭhitaṃ kāryaṃ taskarajñānahetunā || iti |
dyūtasamāhvayākhye vivādapade nirūpite
prasaktoddeśakramānurodhena ṛṇādānādisamāhvayāntāny
aṣṭādaśa vyavahārapadāni nirūpitāni
atha bṛhaspatinā nirūpitaṃ prakīrṇakākhyaṃ vivādapadam abhidhīyate
tatra lakṣaṇabhedau nāradena nirūpitau |
prakīrṇakeṣu vijñeyā vyavahārā nṛpāśrayāḥ |
rājñām ājñāpratīghātas tatkarmakaraṇaṃ tathā ||
punaḥ pramāṇasaṃbhedaḥ prakṛtīnāṃ tathaiva ca |
pāṣaṇḍanaigamaśreṇīgaṇadharmaviparyayāḥ ||
pitāputravivādaś ca lope āśramiṇām api |
varṇasaṃkaradoṣaś ca tadvṛttiniyamas tathā ||
na dṛṣṭaṃ yac ca pūrveṣu sarvaṃ tat syat prakīrṇakam || iti |
prakīrṇake vivāde ye vivādā rājājñollaṅghanatadājñākaraṇatatkarmarakṣaṇādiviṣayās te nṛpasamavāyina eva | tatra smṛtyācāravyapete mārge vartamānānāāṃ pratikūlatām ājñāya vyavahāranirṇayaṃ nṛpa eva kuryāt | evaṃ vadatā yo nṛpāśrayo vyavahāras tat prakīrṇam ity uktaṃ bhavati | tatra rājājñāpratighāte viśeṣadaṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[580]        nyūnaṃ vābhyadhikaṃ vāpi likhed yo rājaśāsanam |
pāradārikacoraṃ vā muñcato daṇḍa uttamaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.295)
rājadattabhūmer nibandhanasya vā parimāṇanyūnatvam ādhikyaṃ vā prakāśayan rājaśāsanaṃ yo vilikhati yaś ca pāradārikaṃ coraṃ vā gṛhītvā rājñe ‘nivedya muñcati tāv ubhāv uttamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīyau | vyāso ‘pi |
nyāyasthāne gṛhītvārtham adharmeṇa vinirṇayam |
karoty uttarakāryāṇi rājadravyavināśakaḥ ||
utkocajīvino dravyahīnān kṛtvā vivāsayet || iti |
tatkarmakaraṇe daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
rājayānāsanāroḍhur daṇḍa uttamasāhasaḥ | iti | (YDh 2.303)
kātyāyano ‘pi |
rājakrīḍāsu ye saktā rājavṛttyupajīvinaḥ |
apriyaṃ cāsya yo vaktā vadhaṃ teṣāṃ prakalpayet || iti |
rājñaḥ kośāpaharaṇādau daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
[581]        rājñaḥ kośāpahartṝṃś ca pratikūleṣu ca sthitān |
ghātayed vividhair daṇḍair haret sarvasvam eva ca || iti | (MDh 9.275)
sarvasvāpahāre ‘pi yad yasya jīvanopakaraṇaṃ tat tasya nāpahartavyam ity āha nāradaḥ |
āyudhāny āyudhīyānāṃ bījāni kṛṣijīvinām |
veśyāstrīṇām alaṃkārān vādyātodyāni tadvidām ||
yac ca yasyopakaraṇaṃ yena jīvanti kārukāḥ |
sarvasvaharaṇe ‘py etan na rājā hartum arhati || iti |
brāhmaṇasya vadhasthāne mauṇḍyam āha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇasya vadho mauṇḍyaṃ purān nirvāsanāṅkane |
lalāṭe cābhiśastāṅkaḥ prayāṇaṃ gardabhena tu || iti |
kopāt parasparabhedenādau daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
dvinetrabhedino rājadviṣṭādeśakṛtas tathā |
vipracihnena śūdrasya jīvato ‘ṣṭaśato damaḥ || iti | (YDh 2.304)
yat tu smṛtyantare ‘bhihitam,
dvijātiliṅginaḥ śūdrān citradaṇḍena ghātayet |
[582] iti, tad vṛttyarthaṃ brāhmaṇaliṅgadhāraṇe veditavyam | brahmaṇapīḍākāriṇo daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇān bādhamānaṃ tu kāmād avaravarṇajam |
hanyāc citrair vadhopāyais tam udvegakarair nṛpaḥ || iti | (MDh 9.248)
śūdrāṇāṃ pravrajyādau daṇḍam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
pravrajāvasitaṃ śūdraṃ japahomaparaṃ tathā |
vadhena śamayet pāpaṃ daṇḍyo vā dviguṇaṃ damam ||
tīritaṃ cānuśiṣṭaṃ ca yatra kvacana vidyate |
kṛtaṃ tad dharmato jñeyaṃ na tat prājño nivartayet || iti | (MDh 9.233)
tad arthipratyarthinor anyataravacanād vyavahārasya adharmato vṛtatvāśaṅkāyāṃ punar dviguṇadaṇḍapratijñāpūrvakaṃ vyavahāraṃ pravartayet | na punar dharmato vṛttatvaniścaye ‘pi rājñā lobhādinā pravartanīya ity evaṃparam | yat punar nṛpāntareṇāpi nyāyāpetaṃ kāryaṃ nivartitaṃ tad api samyakparīkṣaṇena dharmye pathi sthāpanīyam,
nyāyāpetaṃ yad anyena rājñājñānakṛtaṃ bhavet |
tad apy anyāyavihitaṃ punar nyāye niveśayet ||
iti smaraṇāt | anyāyapragṛhītadaṇḍadhanasya gatim āha |
rājñānyāyena yo daṇḍo gṛhīto varuṇāya tam |
nivedya dadyād viprebhyaḥ svayaṃ triṃśadguṇīkṛtam || iti |
anyāyena yo daṇḍo rājñā lobhādinā gṛhītaḥ taṃ triṃśadguṇīkṛtaṃ varuṇāyedam iti saṃkalpya brāhmaṇebhyaḥ svayaṃ dadyāt | yac ca daṇḍarūpeṇa yāvad gṛhītam anyāyena tat tasmai pratideyam, apahāradoṣaprasaṅgāt | itarathā anyāyadaṇḍagrahaṇe pūrvasvāminaḥ sattvavicchedāpatteḥ iti | evaṃ śāstroktamārgeṇa vyavahāradarśino rājñaḥ phalam āha yamaḥ |
evaṃ dharmapravṛttasya rājño daṇḍadharasya ca |
yaśo ‘smin prathathe loke svarge vāsas tathākṣayaḥ || iti |
manur api |
evaṃ sarvān imān rājā vyavahārān samāpayan |
vyapohya kilbiṣaṃ sarvaṃ prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim || (MDh 8.420)
iti śrīmahārājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārgapravartaka-
śrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyadhuraṃdharasya mādhavāmātyasya kṛtau
parāśarasmṛtivyākhyāyāṃ mādhavīyāyāṃ tṛtīyṃ vyavahārakāṇḍam samāptam
samāptaṃ cedaṃ vyavahārakāṇḍam
samāptā ceyaṃ parāśarasmṛtis tasyā vyākhyā ca ||
